1

brahmarātryāṃ vyatītāyāṃ prabuddhe padmasaṃbhave  |
viṣṇuḥ sisṛkṣurbhūtāni jñātvā bhūmiṃ jalānugām  || 1.1 ||

jalakrīḍāruci śubhaṃ kalpādhiṣu yathā purā  |
vārāhamāsthito rūpamujjahāra vasuṃdharām  || 1.2 ||

vedapādo yūpadaṃṣṭraḥ kratudantaścitīmukhaḥ  |
agnijihvo darbharomā brahmaśīrṣo mahātapāḥ  || 1.3 ||

ahorātrekṣaṇo divyo vedāṅgaśrutibhūṣaṇaḥ  |
ājyanāsaḥ sruvatuṇḍaḥ sāmaghoṣasvano mahān  || 1.4 ||

dharmasatyamayaḥ śrīmān kramavikramasatkṛtaḥ  |
prāyaścittamahāghoṇaḥ paśujānurmahākṛtiḥ  || 1.5 ||

udgātrāntro homaliṅgo bījauṣadhimahāphalaḥ  |
vedyantarātmā mantrasphig- vikṛtaḥ somaśoṇitaḥ  || 1.6 ||

vediskandho havirgandho havyakavyādivegavān  |
prāgvaṃṣakāyo dyutimānnānādīkṣābhiranvitaḥ  || 1.7 ||

dakṣiṇāhṛdayo yoga mahāmantramayo mahān  |
upākarmoṣṭharuciraḥ pravargyāvartabhūṣaṇaḥ  || 1.8 ||

nānācchandogatipatho guhyopaniṣadāsanaḥ  |
chāyāpatnīsahāyo vai maṇiśṛṅga ivoditaḥ  || 1.9 ||

mahīṃ sāgaraparyantāṃ saśailavanakānanāṃ  |
ekārṇavajalabhraṣṭāmekārṇavagataḥ prabhuḥ  || 1.10 ||

daṃṣṭrāgreṇa samuddhṛtya lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
ādidevo mahāyogī cakāra jagatīṃ punaḥ  || 1.11 ||

evaṃ yajñavarāheṇa bhūtvā bhūtahitārthinā  |
uddhṛtā pṛthivī devī rasātalagatā purā  || 1.12 ||

uddhṛtya niścale sthāne sthāpayitvā tathā svake  |
yathāsthānaṃ vibhajyāpastadgatā madhusūdanaḥ  || 1.13 ||

sāmudryaśca samudreṣu nādeyīśca nadīṣu ca  |
palvaleṣu ca pālvalyaḥ saraḥsu ca sarobhavāḥ  || 1.14 ||

pātālasaptakaṃ cakre lokānāṃ saptakaṃ tathā  |
dvīpānāmudadhīnāṃ ca sthānāni vividhāni ca  || 1.15 ||

sthānapālān lokapālānnadīḥ śailavanaspatīn  |
ṛṣīṃśca sapta dharmajñān vedān sānṅān surāsurān  || 1.16 ||

piśācoragagandharva yakṣarākṣasamānuṣān  |
paśupakṣimṛgādyāṃśca bhūtagrāmaṃ caturvidham  |
meghendracāpaśampādyān yajñāṃśca vividhāṃstathā  || 1.17 ||

evaṃ varāho bhagavān kṛtvedaṃ sacarācaram  |
jagajjagāma lokānāmavijñātāṃ tadā gatim  || 1.18 ||

avijñātāṃ gatiṃ yāte devadeve janārdane  |
vasudhā cintayāmāsa kā dhṛtirme bhaviṣyati  || 1.19 ||

pṛcchāmi kaśyapaṃ gatvā sa me vakṣyatyasaṃśayam  |
madīyāṃ vahate cintāṃ nityameva mahāmuniḥ  || 1.20 ||

evaṃ sā niścayaṃ kṛtvā devī strīrūpadhāriṇī  |
jagāma kaśyapaṃ draṣṭuṃ dṛṣṭavāṃstāṃ ca kaśyapaḥ  || 1.21 ||

nīlapaṅkajapatrākṣīṃ śāradendunibhānanām  |
alisaṃghālakāṃ śubhrāṃ bandhujīvādharāṃ śubhām  || 1.22 ||

subhrūṃ susūkṣmadaśanāṃ cārunāsāṃ natabhruvam  |
kambukaṇṭhīṃ saṃhatorūṃ pīnorujaghanasthalām  || 1.23 ||

virejatuḥ stanau yasyāḥ samau pīnau nirantarau  |
śakrebhakumbhasaṃkāśau śātakumbhasamadyutī  || 1.24 ||

mṛṇālakomalau bāhū karau kisalayopamau  |
rukmastambhanibhāvūrū gūḍhe śliṣṭe ca jānunī  || 1.25 ||

jaṅghe virome susame pādāvatimanoramau  |
jaghanaṃ ca ghanaṃ madhyaṃ yathā kesariṇaḥ śiśoḥ  || 1.26 ||

prabhāyutā nakhāstāmrā rūpaṃ sarvamanoharam  |
kurvāṇāṃ vīkṣitairnityaṃ nīlotpalayutā diśaḥ  || 1.27 ||

kurvāṇāṃ prabhayā devīṃ tathā vitimirā diśaḥ  |
susūkṣmaśuklavasanāṃ ratnottamavibhūṣitāṃ  || 1.28 ||

padanyāsairvasumatīṃ sapadmāmiva kurvatīṃ  |
rūpayauvanasaṃpannāṃ vinītavadupasthitām  || 1.29 ||

samīpamāgatāṃ dṛṣṭvā pūjayitvātha kaśyapaḥ  |
uvāca tāṃ varārohe vijñātaṃ hṛdgataṃ mayā  || 1.30 ||

dhare tava viśālākṣi gaccha devi janārdanam  |
sa te vakṣyatyaśeṣeṇa bhāvinī te yathā dhṛtiḥ  || 1.31 ||

kṣīrode vasatistasya mayā jñātā śubhānane  |
dhyānayogena cārvaṅgi tvadarthaṃ tatprasādataḥ  || 1.32 ||

ityevamuktā saṃpūjya kaśyapaṃ vasudhā tataḥ  |
prayayau keśavaṃ draṣṭuṃ kṣīrodamatha sāgaram  || 1.33 ||

sā dadarśāmṛtanidhiṃ candraraśmimanoharam  |
pavanakṣobhasaṃjāta- vīcīśatasamākulam  || 1.34 ||

himavacchatasaṃkāśaṃ bhūmaṇḍalamivāparam  |
vīcīhastaiḥ pracalitairāhvayānamiva kṣitim  || 1.35 ||

taireva śuklatāṃ candre vidadhānamivāniśam  |
antarasthena hariṇā vigatāśeṣakalmaṣam  || 1.36 ||

yasmāttasmāddhārayantaṃ suśuklāṃ tanumūrjitām  |
pāṇḍuraṃ khagamāgamyamadhobhuvanavartinam  || 1.37 ||

indranīlakaḍārāḍhyaṃ viparītamivāmbaram  |
phalāvalīsamudbhūta- vanasaṃghamivācitam  || 1.38 ||

nirmokamiva śeṣāhervistīrṇāntamatīva hi  |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā tatra madhyasthaṃ daḍṛśe keśavālayam  || 1.39 ||

anirdeśyaparīmāṇamanirdeśyarddhisaṃyutam  |
śeṣaparyaṅkagaṃ tasmin dadarśa madhusūdanam  || 1.40 ||

śeṣāhiphaṇaratnāṃśu durvibhāvyamukhāmbujam  |
śaśāṅkaśatasaṃkāśaṃ sūryāyutasamaprabham  || 1.41 ||

pītavāsasamakṣobhyaṃ sarvaratnavibhūṣitam [savaratna]  |
mukuṭenārkavarṇena kuṇḍalābhyāṃ virājitam  || 1.42 ||

saṃvāhyamānāṅghriyugaṃ lakṣmyā karatalaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
śarīradhāribhiḥ śastraiḥ sevyamānaṃ samantataḥ  || 1.43 ||

taṃ dṛṣṭvā puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ vavande madhusūdanam  |
jānubhyāmavaniṃ gatvā vijñāpayati cāpyatha  || 1.44 ||

uddhṛtāhaṃ tvayā deva rasātalatalaṃ gatā  |
svasthāne sthāpitā viṣṇo lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  || 1.45 ||

tatrādhunā hi deveśa kā dhṛtirme bhaviṣyati  |
evamuktastayā devyā devo vacanamabravīt || 1.46 ||

varṇāśramācāraratāḥ santaḥ śāstraikatatparāḥ  |
tvāṃ dhare dhārayiṣyanti teṣāṃ tvadbhāra āhitaḥ  || 1.47 ||

evamuktā vasumatī devadevamabhāṣata  |
varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca dharmān vada sanātana  || 1.48 ||

tvatto'haṃ śrotumicchāmi tvaṃ hi me paramā gatiḥ  |
namaste devadeveśa devāribalasūdana  || 1.49 ||

nārāyaṇa jagannātha śaṅkhacakragadādhara  |
padmanābha hṛṣīkeśa mahābalaparākrama  || 1.50 ||

atīndriya suduṣpāra deva śārṅgadhanurdhara  |
varāha bhīma govinda purāṇa puruṣottama  || 1.51 ||

hiraṇyakeśa viśvākṣa yajñamūrte nirañjana  |
kṣetrakṣetrajñadeveśa salilārṇavaśāyaka  || 1.52 ||

mantra mantravahācintya vedavedāṅgavigraha  |
jagato'sya samagrasya sṛṣṭisaṃhārakāraka  || 1.53 ||

dharmādharmajña dharmāṅga dharmayone varaprada  |
viṣvaksenāmṛta vyoma madhukaiṭabhasūdana  || 1.54 ||

bṛhatāṃ bṛṃhaṇājñeya sarva sarvābhayaprada  |
vareṇyānagha jīmūta jagannirmāṇakāraka  || 1.55 ||

āpyāyana apāṃ sthāna caitanyādhāra niṣkriya  |
saptaśīrṣādhvaraguro purāṇapuruṣottama  || 1.56 ||

dhruvākṣara susūkṣmeśa bhaktavatsala pāvana  |
tvaṃ gatiḥ sarvadevānāṃ tvaṃ gatirbrahmavādinām  || 1.57 ||

tathā viditavedyānāṃ gatistvaṃ puruṣottama  |
prapannāsmi jagannātha dhruvaṃ vācaspatiṃ prabhum  || 1.58 ||

subrahmaṇyamanādhṛṣyaṃ vasuṣeṇaṃ vasupradam  |
mahāyogabalopetaṃ pṛśnigarbhaṃ dhṛtārciṣam  || 1.59 ||

vāsudevaṃ mahātmānaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣamacyutam  |
surāsuraguruṃ devaṃ vibhuṃ bhūtamaheśvaram  || 1.60 ||

ekavyūhaṃ caturbāhuṃ jagatkāraṇakāraṇam  |
brūhi me bhagavan dharmāṃścāturvarṇyasya śāśvatān  || 1.61 ||

āśramācārasaṃyuktān sarahasyān sasaṃgrahān  |
evamuktastu deveśaḥ kṣoṇyā kṣoṇīmabhāṣata  || 1.62 ||

śṛṇu devi dhare dharmāṃścāturvarṇyasya śāśvatān  |
āśramācārasaṃyuktān sarahasyān sasaṃgrahān  || 1.63 ||

ye tu tvāṃ dhārayiṣyanti santasteṣāṃ parāyaṇān  |
niṣaṇṇā bhava vāmoru kāñcane'smin varāsane  || 1.64 ||

sukhāsīnā nibodha tvaṃ dharmānnigadato mama  |
śuśruve vaiṣṇavān dharmān sukhāsīnā dharā tadā  || 1.65 ||



2

om | brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ śūdraśceti varṇāścatvāraḥ || 2.1 ||

teṣāmādyā dvijātayastrayaḥ || 2.2 ||

teṣāṃ niṣekādyaḥ śmaśānānto mantravatkriyāsamūhaḥ || 2.3 ||

teṣāṃ ca dharmāḥ || 2.4 ||

brāhmaṇasyādhyāpanam || 2.5 ||

kṣatriyasya śastranityatā || 2.6 ||

vaiśyasya paśupālanam || 2.7 ||

śūdrasya dvijātiśuśrūṣā || 2.8 ||

dvijānāṃ yajanādhyayane || 2.9 ||

athaiteṣāṃ vṛttayaḥ || 2.10 ||

brāhmaṇasya yājanapratigrahau || 2.11 ||

kṣatriyasya kṣititrāṇam || 2.12 ||

kṛṣigorakṣavāṇijyakusīdayonipoṣaṇāni vaiśyasya || 2.13 ||

śūdrasya sarvaśilpāni || 2.14 ||

āpadyanantarā vṛttiḥ || 2.15 ||

kṣamā satyaṃ damaḥ śaucaṃ dānamindriyasaṃyamaḥ  |
ahiṃsā guruśuśrūṣā tīrthānusaraṇaṃ dayā  || 2.16 ||

ārjavaṃ lobhaśūnyatvaṃ devabrāhmaṇapūjanam  |
anabhyasūyā ca tathā dharmaḥ sāmānya ucyate  || 2.17 ||



3

atha rājadharmāḥ || 3.1 ||

prajāparipālanam || 3.2 ||

varṇāśramāṇāṃ sve sve dharme vyavasthāpanam || 3.3 ||

rājā ca jāṅgalaṃ paśavyaṃ sasyopetaṃ deśamāśrayet || 3.4 ||

vaiśyaśūdraprāyaṃ ca || 3.5 ||

tatra dhanvanṛmahīvārivṛkṣagiridurgāṇāmanyatamaṃ durgamāśrayet || 3.6 ||

tatrasthaśca svasvagrāmādhipān kuryāt || 3.7 ||

daśādhyakṣān || 3.8 ||

śatādhyakṣān || 3.9 ||

deśādhyakṣāṃśca || 3.10 ||

grāmadoṣāṇāṃ grāmādhyakṣaḥ parihāraṃ kuryāt || 3.11 ||

aśakto daśagrāmādhyakṣāya nivedayet || 3.12 ||

so'pyaśaktaḥ śatādhyakṣāya || 3.13 ||

so'pyaśakto deśādhyakṣāya || 3.14 ||

deśādhyakṣo'pi sarvātmanā doṣamucchindyāt || 3.15 ||

ākaraśulkataranāgavaneśvāptānniyuñjīta || 3.16 ||

dharmiṣṭhān dharmakāryeṣu || 3.17 ||

nipuṇānarthakāryeṣu || 3.18 ||

śūrān saṃgrāmakarmasu || 3.19 ||

ugrānugreṣu || 3.20 ||

ṣaṇḍhān strīṣu || 3.21 ||

prajābhyo balyarthaṃ saṃvatsareṇa dhānyataḥ ṣaṣṭhamaṃśamādadyāt || 3.22 ||

sarvasasyebhyaśca || 3.23 ||

dvikaṃ śataṃ paśuhiraṇyebhyo vastrebhyaśca || 3.24 ||

māṃsamadhughṛtauṣadhigandhapuṣpamūlaphalarasadārupatrājinamṛdbhāṇḍāśmabhāṇḍavaidalebhyaḥ ṣāṣṭhabhāgaṃ rājā || 3.25 ||

brāhmaṇebhyaḥ karādānaṃ na kuryāt || 3.26 ||

te hi rājño dharmakarāḥ || 3.27 ||

rājā ca prajābhyaḥ sukṛtaduṣkṛtebhyaḥ ṣaṣṭhāṃśabhāk || 3.28 ||

svadeśapaṇyācca śulkāṃśaṃ daśamamādadyāt || 3.29 ||

paradeśapaṇyācca viṃśatitamam || 3.30 ||

śulkasthānādapākrāman sarvāpahāramāpnuyāt || 3.31 ||

śilpinaḥ karmajīvinaśca māsenaikaṃ rājñaḥ karma kuryuḥ || 3.32 ||

svāmyamātyadurgakośadaṇḍarāṣṭramitrāṇi prakṛtayaḥ || 3.33 ||

taddūṣakāṃśca hanyāt || 3.34 ||

svarāṣṭrapararāṣṭrayośca cāracakṣuḥ syāt || 3.35 ||

sādhūnāṃ pūjanaṃ kuryāt || 3.36 ||

duṣṭāṃśca hanyāt || 3.37 ||

śatrumitrodāsīnamadhyameṣu sāmabhedadānadaṇḍān yathārhaṃ  yathākālaṃ prayuñjīta || 3.38 ||

saṃdhivigrahayānāsanasaṃśrayadvaidhībhāvāṃśca yathākālamāśrayet || 3.39 ||

caitre mārgaśīrṣe vā yātrāṃ yāyāt || 3.40 ||

parasya vyasane vā || 3.41 ||

paradeśāvāptau taddeśadharmānnocchindyāt || 3.42 ||

pareṇābhiyuktaśca sarvātmanā svarāṣṭraṃ gopāyet || 3.43 ||

nāsti rājñāṃ samare tanutyāgasadṛśo dharmaḥ || 3.44 ||

gobrāhmaṇanṛpamitradhanadārajīvitarakṣaṇātye hatāste svargalokabhājaḥ || 3.45 ||

varṇasaṃkararakṣaṇārthaṃ ca || 3.46 ||

rājā parapurāvāptau tatra tatkulīnamabhiṣiñcet || 3.47 ||

na rājakulamucchindyāt || 3.48 ||

anyatrākulīnarājakulāt || 3.49 ||

mṛgayākṣastrīpānābhiratiṃ pariharet || 3.50 ||

vākpāruṣyadaṇḍapāruṣye ca || 3.51 ||

nārthadūṣaṇaṃ kuryāt || 3.52 ||

ādyadvārāṇi nocchindyāt || 3.53 ||

nāpātravarṣī syāt || 3.54 ||

ākarebhyaḥ sarvamādadyāt || 3.55 ||

nidhiṃ labdhvā tadardhaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt || 3.56 ||

dvitīymardhaṃ kośe praveśayet || 3.57 ||

nidhiṃ brāhmaṇo labdhvā sarvamādadyāt || 3.58 ||

kṣatriyaścaturthamaṃśaṃ rājñe dadyāt, caturthamaṃśaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ, ardhamādadyāt || 3.59 ||

vaiṣyastu caturthamaṃśaṃ rājñe dadyāt, brāhmaṇebhyo'rdhaṃ, caturthamaṃśamādadyāt || 3.60 ||

śūdraścāvāptaṃ dvādaśadhā vibhajya pañcāṃśān rājñe dadyāt, pañcāṃśān brāhmaṇebhyaḥ, aṃśadvayamādadyāt || 3.61 ||

aniveditavijñātasya sarvamapaharet || 3.62 ||

svanihitādrājñe brāhmaṇavarjaṃ dvādaśamaṃśaṃ dadyuḥ || 3.63 ||

paranihitaṃ svanihitamiti bruvaṃstatsamaṃ daṇḍamāvahet || 3.64 ||

bālānāthastrīdhanāni rājā paripālayet || 3.65 ||

caurahṛtaṃ dhanamavāpya sarvameva sarvavarṇebhyo dadyāt || 3.66 ||

anavāpya ca svakośādeva dadyāt || 3.67 ||

śāntisvastyayanopāyairdaivopaghātān praśamayet || 3.68 ||

paracakropaghātāṃśca śastranityatayā || 3.69 ||

vedetihāsadharmaśāstrārthakuśalaṃ kulīnamavyaṅgaṃ tapasvinaṃ purohitaṃ ca varayet || 3.70 ||

śucīnalubdhānavahitān śaktisaṃpannān sarvārtheṣu ca sahāyān || 3.71 ||

svayameva vyavahārān paśyedvidvadbhirbrāhmaṇaiḥ sārdham || 3.72 ||

vyavahāradarśane brāhmaṇaṃ vā niyuñjyāt || 3.73 ||

janmakarmavratopetāśca rājñā sabhāsadaḥ kāryāḥ, ripau mitre ca ye samāḥ, kāmakrodhabhayalobhādibhiḥ kāryārthabhiranāhāryāḥ || 3.74 ||

rājā ca sarvakāryeṣu sāṃvatsarādhīnaḥ syāt || 3.75 ||

devabrāhmaṇān satatameva pūjayet || 3.76 ||

vṛddhasevī bhavet || 3.77 ||

yajñayājī ca || 3.78 ||

na cāsya viṣaye brāhmaṇaḥ kṣudhārto'vasīdet || 3.79 ||

na cānyo'pi satkarmanirataḥ || 3.80 ||

brāhmaṇebhyaśca bhuvaṃ pratipādayet || 3.81 ||

yeṣāṃ ca pratipādayetteṣāṃ svavaṃśyān bhuvaḥ parimāṇaṃ dānacchedopavarṇanaṃ ca paṭe tāmrapaṭṭe vā likhitaṃ svamudrāṅkitaṃ cāgāminṛpativijñāpanārthaṃ dadyāt || 3.82 ||

paradattāṃ ca bhuvaṃ nāpaharet || 3.83 ||

brāhmaṇebhyaḥ sarvadāyān prayacchet || 3.84 ||

sarvatastvātmānaṃ gopāyet || 3.85 ||

sudarśanaśca syāt || 3.86 ||

viṣaghnāgadamantradhārī ca || 3.87 ||

nāparīkṣitamupayuñjyāt || 3.88 ||

smitapūrvābhibhāṣī syāt || 3.89 ||

vadhyeṣvapi na bhruṃkuṭīmācaret || 3.90 ||

aparādhānurūpaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣu dāpayet || 3.91 ||

samyagdaṇḍapraṇayanaṃ kuryāt || 3.92 ||

dvitīyamaparādhaṃ na sa kasyacitkṣameta || 3.93 ||

svadharmamapālayannādaṇḍyo nāmāsti rājñām || 3.94 ||

yatra śyāmo lohitākṣo daṇḍaścarati nirbhayaḥ  |
prajāstatra vivardhante netā cetsādhu paśyati  || 3.95 ||

svarāṣṭro nyāyadaṇḍaḥ syādbhṛśadaṇḍaśca śatruṣu  |
suhṛtsvajihmaḥ snigdheṣu brāhmaṇeṣu kṣamānvitaḥ  || 3.96 ||

evaṃvṛttasya nṛpateḥ śiloñchenāpi jīvataḥ  |
vistīryate yaśo loke tailabindurivāmbhasi  || 3.97 ||

prajāsukhe sukhī rājā tadduḥkhe yaśca duḥkhitaḥ  |
sa kīrtiyukto loke'smin pretya svarge mahīyate  || 3.98 ||



4

jālasthārkamarīcigataṃ rajaḥ trasareṇusaṃjñakam || 4.1 ||

tadaṣṭakaṃ likṣā || 4.2 ||

tattrayaṃ rājasarṣapaḥ || 4.3 ||

tattrayaṃ gaurasarṣapaḥ || 4.4 ||

tatṣaṭkaṃ yavaḥ || 4.5 ||

tattrayaṃ kṛṣṇalam || 4.6 ||

tatpañcakaṃ māṣaḥ || 4.7 ||

taddvādaśakamakṣārdham || 4.8 ||

akṣārdhameva sacaturmāṣakaṃ suvarṇaḥ || 4.9 ||

catuḥsuvarṇako niṣkaḥ || 4.10 ||

dve kṛṣṇale samadhṛte rūpyamāṣakaḥ || 4.11 ||

tatṣoḍaśakaṃ dharaṇam || 4.12 ||

tāmrakārṣikaḥ kārṣāpaṇaḥ || 4.13 ||

paṇānāṃ dve śate sārdhe prathamaḥ sāhasaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
madhyamaḥ pañca vijñeyaḥ sahasraṃ tveṣa cottamaḥ  || 4.14 ||



5

atha mahāpātakino brāhmaṇavarjaṃ sarve vadhyāḥ || 5.1 ||

na śārīro brāhmaṇasya daṇḍaḥ || 5.2 ||

svadeśātbrāhmaṇaṃ kṛtāṅkaṃ vivāsayet || 5.3 ||

tasya ca brahmahatyāyāmaśiraskaṃ puruṣaṃ lalāṭe kuryāt || 5.4 ||

surādhvajaṃ surāpāne || 5.5 ||

śvapadaṃ steye || 5.6 ||

bhagaṃ gurutalpagamane || 5.7 ||

anyatrāpi vadhyakarmaṇi tiṣṭhantaṃ samagradhanamakṣataṃ vivāsayet || 5.8 ||

kūṭaśāsanakartṝṃśca rājā hanyāt || 5.9 ||

kūṭalekhyakārāṃśca || 5.10 ||

garadāgnidaprasahyataskarān strībālapuruṣaghātinaśca || 5.11 ||

ye ca dhānyaṃ daśabhyaḥ kumbhebhyo'dhikamapahareyuḥ || 5.12 ||

dharimameyānāṃ śatādabhyadhikam || 5.13 ||

ye cākulīnā rājyamabhikāmayeyuḥ || 5.14 ||

setubhedakāṃśca || 5.15 ||

prasahya taskarāṇāṃ cāvakāśabhaktapradāṃśca || 5.16 ||

anyatra rājāśakteḥ || 5.17 ||

striyamaśaktabhartṛkāṃ tadatikramaṇīṃ ca || 5.18 ||

hīnavarṇo'dhikavarṇasya yenāṅgenāparādhaṃ kuryāttadevāsya śātayet || 5.19 ||

ekāsanopaveśī kaṭyāṃ kṛtāṅko nirvāsyaḥ || 5.20 ||

niṣṭhīvyauṣṭhadvayavihīnaḥ kāryaḥ || 5.21 ||

avaśardhayitā ca gudahīnaḥ || 5.22 ||

ākrośayitā ca vijihvaḥ || 5.23 ||

darpeṇa dharmopadeśakāriṇāṃ rājā taptamāsecayettailamāsye || 5.24 ||

droheṇa ca nāmajātigrahaṇe daśāṅgulo'sya śaṅkurnikheyaḥ || 5.25 ||

śrutadeśajātikarmaṇāmanyathāvādī kārśāpaṇaśatadvayaṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.26 ||

kāṇakhañjādīnāṃ tathyavādyapi kārśāpaṇadvayam || 5.27 ||

gurūnākṣipan kārśāpaṇaśatadvayam || 5.28 ||

parasya patanīyākṣepe kṛte tūttamasāhasam || 5.29 ||

upapātakayukte madhyamam || 5.30 ||

traividyavṛddhānāṃ kṣepe jātipūgānāṃ ca || 5.31 ||

grāmadeśayośca prathamasāhasam || 5.32 ||

nyaṅgatāyukte kṣepe kārśāpaṇaśatam || 5.33 ||

mātṛyukte tūttamam || 5.34 ||

samavarṇākrośane dvādaśa paṇān daṇḍyaḥ || 5.35 ||

hīnavarṇākṛośane ṣaṭ || 5.36 ||

yathākālamuttamavarṇākṣepe tatpramāṇo daṇḍaḥ || 5.37 ||

trayo vā kārṣāpaṇāḥ || 5.38 ||

śuktavākyābhidhāne tvevameva || 5.39 ||

pārajāyī savarṇāgamane  tūttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.40 ||

hīnavarṇāgamane madhyamam || 5.41 ||

gogamane ca || 5.42 ||

antyāgamane vadhyaḥ || 5.43 ||

paśugamane kārṣāpaṇaśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.44 ||

doṣamanākhyāya kanyāṃ prayacchaṃśca || 5.45 ||

tāṃ ca bibhṛyāt || 5.46 ||

aduṣṭāṃ duṣṭāmiti bruvannuttamasāhasam || 5.47 ||

gajāśvoṣṭragoghātī tvekakarapādaḥ kāryaḥ || 5.48 ||

vimāṃsavikrayī ca || 5.49 ||

grāmyapaśughātī kārṣāpaṇaśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.50 ||

paśusvāmine tanmūlyaṃ dadyāt || 5.51 ||

āraṇyapaśughātī pañcāśataṃ kārṣāpaṇān || 5.52 ||

pakṣighātī matsyaghātī ca daśa kārṣāpaṇān || 5.53 ||

kīṭopaghātī ca kārṣāpaṇam || 5.54 ||

phalopagamadrumacchedī tūttamasāhasam || 5.55 ||

puṣpopagamadrumacchedī madhyamam || 5.56 ||

vallīgulmalatācchedī kārṣāpaṇaśatam || 5.57 ||

tṛṇacchedyekam || 5.58 ||

sarve ca tatsvāmināṃ tadutpattim || 5.59 ||

hastenodgūrayitā daśakārṣāpaṇam || 5.60 ||

pādena viṃśatim || 5.61 ||

kāṣṭhena prathamasāhasam || 5.62 ||

pāṣāṇena madhyamam || 5.63 ||

śastreṇottamam || 5.64 ||

pādakeśāṃśukakaraluñcane daśa paṇān || 5.65 ||

śoṇitena vinā duḥkhamutpādayitā dvātriṃśatpaṇān || 5.66 ||

saha śoṇitena catuḥṣaṣṭim || 5.67 ||

karapādadantabhaṅge karṇanāsāvikartane madhyamam || 5.68 ||

ceṣṭābhojanavāgrodhe prahāradāne ca || 5.69 ||

netrakaṃdharābāhusakthyaṃsabhaṅge cottamam || 5.70 ||

ubhayanetrabhedinaṃ rājā yāvajjīvaṃ bandhanānna muñcet || 5.71 ||

tādṛśameva vā kuryāt || 5.72 ||

ekaṃ bahūnāṃ nighnatāṃ pratyekamuktāddaṇḍāddviguṇaḥ || 5.73 ||

utkrośantamanabhidhāvatāṃ tatsamīpavartināṃ saṃsaratāṃ ca || 5.74 ||

sarve ca puruṣapīḍākarāstadutthānavyayaṃ dadyuḥ || 5.75 ||

grāmyapaśupīḍākarāśca || 5.76 ||

go'śvoṣṭragajāpahāryekakarapādaḥ kāryaḥ || 5.77 ||

ajāvyapahāryekakaraśca || 5.78 ||

dhānyāpahāryekādaśaguṇaṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.79 ||

sasyāpahārī ca || 5.80 ||

suvarṇarajatavastrāṇāṃ pañcāśatastvabhyadhikamapaharan vikaraḥ || 5.81 ||

tadūnamekādaśaguṇaṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.82 ||

sūtrakārpāsagomayaguḍadadhikṣīratakratṛṇalavaṇamṛdbhasmapakṣimatsyaghṛtatailamāṃsamadhuvaidalaveṇumṛnmayalohabhāṇḍānāmapahartā mūlyāttriguṇaṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.83 ||

pakvānnānāṃ ca || 5.84 ||

puṣpaharitagulmavallīlatāparṇānāmapaharaṇe pañcakṛṣṇalam || 5.85 ||

śākamūlaphalānāṃ ca || 5.86 ||

ratnāpahāryuttamasāhasam || 5.87 ||

anuktadravyāṇāmapahartā mūlyasamam || 5.88 ||

stenāḥ sarvamapahṛtaṃ dhanikasya dāpyāḥ || 5.89 ||

tatasteṣāmabhihitadaṇḍaprayogaḥ || 5.90 ||

yeṣāṃ deyaḥ panthāsteṣāmapathadāyī kārṣāpaṇapañcaviṃśatiṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.91 ||

āsanārhasyāsanamadadacca || 5.92 ||

pūjārhamapūjayaṃśca || 5.93 ||

prātiveśyabrāhmaṇanimantraṇātikramaṇe ca || 5.94 ||
nimantrayitvā bhojanādāyinaśca || 5.95 ||

nimantritastathetyuktvā cābhuñjānaḥ suvarṇamāṣakam || 5.96 ||

niketayituśca dviguṇamannam || 5.97 ||

abhakṣyeṇa brāhmaṇasya dūṣayitā ṣoḍaśa suvarṇān || 5.98 ||

jātyapahāriṇā śatam || 5.99 ||

surayā vadhyaḥ || 5.100 ||

kṣatriyaṃ dūṣayitustadardham || 5.101 ||

vaiśyaṃ dūṣayitustadardhamapi || 5.102 ||

śūdraṃ dūṣayituḥ prathamasāhasam || 5.103 ||

aspṛśyaḥ kāmakāreṇa spṛśan spṛśyaṃ traivarṇikaṃ vadhyaḥ || 5.104 ||

rajasvalāṃ śiphābhistāḍayet || 5.105 ||

pathyudyānodakasamīpe'pyaśucikārī paṇaśatam || 5.106 ||

taccāpāsyāt || 5.107 ||

gṛhabhūkuḍyādyupabhettā madhyamasāhasam || 5.108 ||

tacca yojayet || 5.109 ||

gṛhe pīḍākaraṃ dravyaṃ prakṣipan paṇaśatam || 5.110 ||

sādhāraṇāpalāpī ca || 5.111 ||

preṣitasyāpradātā ca || 5.112 ||

pitṛputrācāryayājyartvijāmanyonyāpatitatyāgī ca || 5.113 ||

na ca tān jahyāt || 5.114 ||

śūdrapravrajitānāṃ daive pitrye bhojakāśca || 5.115 ||

ayogyakarmakārī ca || 5.116 ||

samudragṛhabhedakaśca || 5.117 ||

aniyuktaḥ śapathakārī || 5.118 ||

paśūnāṃ puṃstvopaghātakārī || 5.119 ||

pitāputravirodhe sākṣiṇāṃ daśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ || 5.120 ||

yastayoścāntare syāttasyottamasāhasaḥ || 5.121 ||

tulāmānakūṭakartuśca || 5.122 ||

tadakūṭe kūṭavādinaśca || 5.123 ||

dravyāṇāṃ pratirūpavikrayikasya ca || 5.124 ||

saṃbhūya vaṇijāṃ paṇyamanargheṇāvarundhatām || 5.125 ||

pratyekaṃ vikrīṇatāṃ ca || 5.126 ||

gṛhītamūlyaṃ yaḥ paṇyaṃ kreturnaiva dadyāt, tasyāsau sodayaṃ dāpyaḥ || 5.127 ||

rājñā ca paṇaśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.128 ||

krītamakrīṇato yā hāniḥ sā kretureva syāt || 5.129 ||

rājaniṣiddhaṃ vikrīṇatastadapahāraḥ || 5.130 ||

tarikaḥ sthalajaṃ śulkaṃ gṛhṇan daśapaṇān daṇḍyaḥ || 5.131 ||

brahmacārivānaprasthabhikṣugurviṇītīrthānusāriṇāṃ nāvikaḥ śaulkikaḥ śulkamādadānaśca || 5.132 ||

tacca teṣāṃ dadyāt || 5.133 ||

dyūte kūṭākṣadevināṃ karacchedaḥ || 5.134 ||

upadhidevināṃ saṃdaṃśacchedaḥ || 5.135 ||

granthibhedakānāṃ ca || 5.136 ||

utkṣepakānāṃ ca karacchedaḥ || 5.137 ||

divā paśūnāṃ vṛkādyupaghāte pāle tvanāyati pāladoṣaḥ || 5.138 ||

vinaṣṭapaśumūlyaṃ ca svāmine dadyāt || 5.139 ||

ananujñātāṃ duhan pañcaviṃśatiṃ kārṣāpaṇān || 5.140 ||

mahiṣī cetsasyanāśaṃ kuryāt, tatpālastvaṣṭau māṣān daṇḍyaḥ || 5.141 ||

apālāyāḥ svāmī || 5.142 ||

aśvastūṣṭro gardabho vā || 5.143 ||

gauścettadardham || 5.144 ||

tadardhamajāvikam || 5.145 ||

bhakṣayitvopaviṣṭeṣu dviguṇam || 5.146 ||

sarvatra svāmine vinaṣṭasasyamūlyaṃ ca || 5.147 ||

pathi grāme vivītānte na doṣaḥ || 5.148 ||

anāvṛte ca || 5.149 ||

alpakālam || 5.150 ||

utsṛṣṭavṛṣabhasūtikānāṃ ca || 5.151 ||

yastūttamavarṇān dāsye niyojayettasyottamasāhaso daṇḍaḥ || 5.152 ||

tyaktapravrajyo rājño dāsyaṃ kuryāt || 5.153 ||

bhṛtakaścāpūrṇe kāle bhṛtiṃ tyajan sakalameva mūlyaṃ dadyāt || 5.154 ||

jājñe ca paṇaśataṃ dadyāt || 5.155 ||

taddoṣeṇa yadvinaśyettatsvāmine || 5.156 ||

anyatra daivopaghātāt || 5.157 ||

svāmī cetbhṛtakamapūrṇe kāle jahyāt, tasya sarvameva mūlyaṃ dadyāt || 5.158 ||

paṇaśataṃ ca rājani || 5.159 ||

anyatra bhṛtakadośāt || 5.160 ||

yaḥ kanyāṃ pūrvadattāmanyasmai dadyāt, sa cauravacchāsyaḥ || 5.161 ||

varadoṣaṃ vinā || 5.162 ||

nirdoṣāṃ parityajan || 5.163 ||

patnīṃ ca || 5.164 ||

ajānānaḥ prakāśaṃ yaḥ paradravyaṃ krīṇīyāt, tatra tasya na doṣaḥ || 5.165 ||

svāmī dravyamāpnuyāt || 5.166 ||

yadyaprakāśaṃ hīnamūlyaṃ ca krīṇīyāt, tadā kretā vikretā ca cauravacchāsyau || 5.167 ||

gaṇadravyāpahartā vivāsyaḥ || 5.168 ||

tatsaṃvidaṃ yaśca laṅghayet || 5.169 ||

nikṣepāpahāryarthavṛddhisahitaṃ dhanaṃ dhanikasya dāpyaḥ || 5.170 ||

rājñā cauravacchāsyaḥ || 5.171 ||

yaścānikṣiptaṃ nikṣiptamiti brūyāt || 5.172 ||

sīmābhettāramuttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍayitvā punaḥ sīmāṃ kārayet || 5.173 ||

jātibhraṃśakarasyābhakṣyasya bhakṣayitā vivāsyaḥ || 5.174 ||

abhakṣyasyāvikreyasya vikrayī devapratimābhedakaścottamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ || 5.175 ||

bhiṣaṅmithyācarannuttameṣu puruṣeṣu || 5.176 ||

madhyameṣu madhyamam || 5.177 ||

tiryakṣu prathamam || 5.178 ||

pratiśrutasyāpradāyī taddāpayitvā prathamasāhasaṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 5.179 ||

kūṭasākṣiṇāṃ sarvasvāpahāraḥ kāryaḥ || 5.180 ||

utkocopajīvināṃ sabhyānāṃ ca || 5.181 ||

gocarmamātrādhikāṃ bhuvamanyasyādhīkṛtāṃ tasmādanirmocyānyasya yaḥ prayacchetsa vadhyaḥ || 5.182 ||

ūnāṃ cetṣoḍaśa suvarṇān daṇḍyaḥ || 5.183 ||

eko'śnīyādyadutpannaṃ naraḥ saṃvatsaraṃ phalam  |
gocarmamātrā sā kṣoṇī stokā vā yadi vā bahu  || 5.184 ||

yayornikṣipta ādhistau vivadetāṃ yadā narau  |
yasya bhuktiḥ phalaṃ tasya balātkāraṃ vinā kṛtā  || 5.185 ||

sāgamena tu bhogena bhuktaṃ samyagyadā tu yat |
āhartā labhate tatra nāpahāryaṃ tu tatkvacit || 5.186 ||

pitrā bhuktaṃ tu yaddravyaṃ bhuktyācāreṇa dharmataḥ  |
tasmin prete na vācyo'sau bhuktyā prāptaṃ hi tasya tat || 5.187 ||

tribhireva tu yā bhuktā puruṣairbhūryathāvidhi  |
lekhyābhāve'pi tāṃ tatra caturthaḥ samavāpnuyāt || 5.188 ||

nakhināṃ śṛṅgiṇāṃ caiva daṃṣṭriṇāmātatāyinām  |
hastyaśvānāṃ tathānyeṣāṃ vadhe hantā na doṣabhāk || 5.189 ||

guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā bahuśrutam  |
ātatāyinamāyāntaṃ hanyādevāvicārayan  || 5.190 ||

nātatāyivadhe doṣo hanturbhavati kaścana  |
prakāśaṃ vāprakāśaṃ vā manyustanmanyumṛcchati  || 5.191 ||

udyatāsiviṣāgniṃ ca śāpodyatakaraṃ tathā  |
ātharvaṇena hantāraṃ piśunaṃ caiva rājasu  || 5.192 ||

bhāryātikramiṇaṃ caiva vidyātsaptātatāyinaḥ  |
yaśovittaharānanyānāhurdharmārthahārakān  || 5.193 ||

uddeśataste kathito dhare daṇḍavidhirmayā  |
sarveṣāmaparādhānāṃ vistarādativistaraḥ  || 5.194 ||

aparādheṣu cānyesu jñātvā jātiṃ dhanaṃ vayaḥ  |
daṇḍaṃ prakalpayedrājā saṃmantrya brāhmaṇaiḥ saha  || 5.195 ||

daṇḍyaṃ pramocayan daṇḍyāddviguṇaṃ daṇḍamāvahet |
niyuktaścāpyadaṇḍyānāṃ daṇḍakārī narādhamaḥ  || 5.196 ||

yasya cauraḥ pure nāsti nānyastrīgo na duṣṭavāk |
na sāhasikadaṇḍaghnau sa rājā śakralokabhāk || 5.197 ||



6

athottamarṇo'dhamarṇādyathādattamarthaṃ gṛhṇīyāt || 6.1 ||

dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ ca śataṃ varṇānukrameṇa pratimāsam || 6.2 ||

sarve varṇā vā svapratipannāṃ vṛddhiṃ dadyuḥ || 6.3 ||

akṛtāmapi vatsarātikrameṇa yathāvihitam || 6.4 ||

ādhyupabhoge vṛddhyabhāvaḥ || 6.5 ||

daivarājopaghātādṛte vinaṣṭamādhimuttamarṇo dadyāt || 6.6 ||

antavṛddhau praviṣṭāyāmapi || 6.7 ||

na sthāvaramādhimṛte vacanāt || 6.8 ||

gṛhītadhanapraveśārthameva yatsthāvaraṃ dattaṃ tatgṛhītadhanapraveśe dadyāt || 6.9 ||

dīyamānaṃ prayuktamarthamuttamarṇasyāgṛhṇatastataḥ paraṃ na vardhate || 6.10 ||

hiraṇyasya parā vṛddhirdviguṇā || 6.11 ||

dhānyasya triguṇā || 6.12 ||

vastrasya caturguṇā || 6.13 ||

rasasyāṣṭaguṇā || 6.14 ||

saṃtatiḥ strīpaśūnām || 6.15 ||

kiṇvakārpāsasūtracarmāyudheṣṭakāṅgarāṇāmakṣayā || 6.16 ||

anuktānāṃ dviguṇā || 6.17 ||

prayuktamarthaṃ yathā kathaṃcitsādhayanna rājño vācyaḥ syāt || 6.18 ||

sādhyamānaścedrājānamabhigacchettatsamaṃ daṇḍyaḥ || 6.19 ||

uttamarṇaścedrājānamiyāt, tadvibhāvito'dhamarṇo rājñe dhanadaśabhāgasaṃmitaṃ daṇḍaṃ dadyāt || 6.20 ||

prāptārthaścottamarṇo viṃśatitamamaṃśam || 6.21 ||

sarvāpalāpyekadeśavibhāvito'pi sarvaṃ dadyāt || 6.22 ||

tasya ca bhāvanāstisro bhavanti likhitaṃ sākṣiṇaḥ samayakriyā ca || 6.23 ||

sasākṣikamāptaṃ sasākṣikameva dadyāt || 6.24 ||

likhtārthe praviṣṭe likhitaṃ pāṭayet || 6.25 ||

asamagradāne lekhyāsaṃnidhāne cottamarṇaḥ svalikhitaṃ dadyāt || 6.26 ||

dhanagrāhiṇi prete prevrajite dvidaśāḥ samāḥ pravasite vā tatputrapautrairdhanaṃ deyam || 6.27 ||

nātaḥ paramanicchubhiḥ || 6.28 ||

saputrasya vāpyaputrasya vā rikthagrāhī ṛṇaṃ dadyāt || 6.29 ||

nirdhanasya strīgrāhī || 6.30 ||

na strī patiputrakṛtam || 6.31 ||

na strīkṛtaṃ patiputrau || 6.32 ||

na pitā putrakṛtam || 6.33 ||

avibhaktaiḥ kṛtamṛṇaṃ yastiṣṭhetsa dadyāt || 6.34 ||

paitṛkamṛṇamavibhaktānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ ca || 6.35 ||

vibhaktāśca dāyānurūpamaṃśam || 6.36 ||

gopaśauṇḍikaśailūṣarajakavyādhastrīṇāṃ patirdadyāt || 6.37 ||

vākpratipannaṃ nādeyaṃ kasyacit || 6.38 ||

kuṭumbārthe kṛtaṃ ca || 6.39 ||

yo gṛhītvā ṛṇaṃ sarvaṃ śvo dāsyāmīti sāmakam  |
na dadyāllobhataḥ paścāttathā vṛddhimavāpnuyāt || 6.40 ||

darśane pratyaye dāne prātibhāvyaṃ vidhīyate  |
ādyau tu vitathe dāpyāvitarasya sutā api  || 6 41 ||

bahavaścetpratibhuvo dadyuste'rthaṃ yathākṛtam  |
arthe'viśeṣite tveṣu dhanikacchandataḥ kriyā  || 6 42 ||

yamarthaṃ pratibhūrdadyāddhanikenopapīḍitaḥ  |
ṛṇikastaṃ pratibhuve dviguṇaṃ dātumarhati  || 6 43 ||



7

atha lekhyaṃ trividham || 7.1 ||

rājasākṣikaṃ sasākṣikamasākṣikaṃ ca || 7.2 ||

rājādhikaraṇe tanniyuktakāyasthakṛtaṃ tadadhyakṣakaracihnitaṃ rājasākṣikam || 7.3 ||

yatra kvacana yena kenacillikhitaṃ sākṣibhiḥ svahastacihnitaṃ sasākṣikam || 7.4 ||

svahastalikhitamasākṣikam || 7.5 ||

tatbalātkāritamapramāṇam || 7.6 ||

upadhikṛtāni sarvāṇyeva || 7.7 ||

dūṣitakarmaduṣṭasākṣyaṅkitaṃ sasākṣikamapi || 7.8 ||

tādṛgvidhena lekhakena likhitaṃ ca || 7.9 ||

strībālāsvatantramattonmattabhītatāḍitakṛtaṃ ca || 7.10 ||

deśācārāviruddhaṃ vyaktādhikṛtalakṣaṇamaluptaprakramākṣaraṃ pramāṇam || 7.11 ||

varṇaiśca tatkṛtaiścihnaiḥ patraireva ca yuktibhiḥ  |
saṃdigdhaṃ sādhayellekhyaṃ tadyuktipratirūpitaiḥ  || 7.12 ||

yatra+ṛṇī dhaniko vāpi sākṣī vā lekhako'pi vā  |
mriyate tatra tallekhyaṃ tatsvahastaiḥ prasādhayet || 7.13 ||



8

athāsākṣiṇaḥ || 8.1 ||

na rājaśrotriyapravrajitakitavataskaraparādhīnastrībālasāhasikātivṛddhamattonmatābhiśastapatitakṣuttṛṣṇārtavyasanirāgāndhāḥ || 8.2 ||

ripumitrārthasaṃbandhivikarmadṛṣṭadoṣasahāyāśca || 8.3 ||

anirdiṣṭastu sākṣitve yaścopetya brūyāt || 8.4 ||

ekaścāsākṣī || 8.5 ||

steyasāhasavāgdaṇḍapāruṣyasaṃgrahaṇeṣu sākṣiṇo na parīkṣyāḥ || 8.6 ||

atha sākṣiṇaḥ || 8.7 ||

kulajā vṛttavittasaṃpannā yajvānastapasvinaḥ putriṇo dharmajñā adhīyānāḥ satyavantastraividyavṛddhāśca || 8.8 ||

abhihitaguṇasaṃpanna ubhayānumata eko'pi || 8.9 ||

dvayorvivadamānayoryasya pūrvavādastasya sākṣiṇaḥ praṣṭavyāḥ || 8.10 ||

ādharyaṃ kāryavaśādyatra pūrvapakṣasya bhavettatra prativādino'pi || 8.11 ||

uddiṣṭasākṣiṇi mṛte deśāntaragate ca tadabhihitaśrotāraḥ pramāṇam || 8.12 ||

samakṣadarśanātsākṣī śravaṇādvā || 8.13 ||

sākṣiṇaśca satyena pūyante || 8.14 ||

varṇināṃ yatra vadhastatrānṛtena || 8.15 ||

tatpāvanāya kūśmāṇḍībhirdvijo'gniṃ ghṛtena juhuyāt || 8.16 ||

śūdra ekāhikaṃ godaśakasya grāsaṃ dadyāt || 8.17 ||

svabhāvavikṛtau mukhavarṇavināśe'saṃbaddhapralāpe ca kūṭasākṣiṇaṃ vidyāt || 8.18 ||

sākṣiṇaścāhūya ādityodaye kṛtaśapathān pṛcchet || 8.19 ||

brūhīti brāhmaṇaṃ pṛcchet || 8.20 ||

satyaṃ brūhīti rājanyam || 8.21 ||

gobījakāñcanairvaiśyam || 8.22 ||

sarvamahāpātakaistu śūdram || 8.23 ||

sākṣiṇaśca śrāvayet || 8.24 ||

ye mahāpātakināṃ lokā ye copapātakināṃ te kūṭasākṣiṇāmapi || 8.25 ||

jananamaraṇāntare kṛtasukṛtahāniśca || 8.26 ||

satyenādityastapati || 8.27 ||

satyena bhāti candramāḥ || 8.28 ||

satyena vāti pavanaḥ || 8.29 ||

satyena bhūrdhārayati || 8.30 ||

satyenāpastiṣṭhanti || 8.31 ||

satyenāgniḥ || 8.32 ||

khaṃ ca satyena || 8.33 ||

satyena devāḥ || 8.34 ||

satyena yajñāḥ || 8.35 ||

aśvamedhasahasraṃ ca satyaṃ ca tulayā dhṛtam  |
aśvamedhasahasrāddhi satyameva viśiṣyate  || 8.36 ||

jānanto'pi hi ye sākṣye tūṣṇīṃbhūtā udāsate  |
te kūṭasākṣiṇāṃ pāpaistulyā daṇḍena cāpyatha  || 8.37 ||

evaṃ hi sākṣiṇaḥ pṛcchedvarṇānukramato nṛpaḥ  || 8.38 ||

yasyocuḥ sākṣiṇaḥ satyāṃ pratijñāṃ sa jayī bhavet |
anyathā vādino yasya dhruvastasya parājayaḥ  || 8.39 ||

bahutvaṃ pratigṛhṇīyātsākṣidvaidhe narādhipaḥ  |
sameṣu ca guṇotkṛṣṭān guṇidvaidhe dvijottamān  || 8.40 ||

yasmin yasmin vivāde tu kūṭasākṣyanṛtaṃ vadet |
tattatkāryaṃ nivarteta kṛtaṃ cāpyakṛtaṃ bhavet || 8.41 ||



9

atha samayakriyā || 9.1 ||

rājadrohasāhaseṣu yathākāmam || 9.2 ||

nikṣepasteyeṣvarthapramāṇam || 9.3 ||

sarveṣvevārthajāteṣu mūlyaṃ kanakaṃ kalpayet || 9.4 ||

tatra kṛṣṇalone śūdraṃ dūrvākaraṃ śāpayet || 9.5 ||

dvikṛṣṇalone tilakaram || 9.6 ||

trikṛṣṇalone rajatakaram || 9.7 ||

catuḥkṛṣṇalone suvarṇakaram || 9.8 ||

pañcakṛṣṇalone sīroddhṛtamahīkaram || 9.9 ||

suvarṇārdhone kośo deyaḥ śūdrasya || 9.10 ||

tataḥ paraṃ yathārhaṃ dhaṭāgnyudakaviṣāṇāmanyatamam || 9.11 ||

dviguṇe'rthe yathābhihitāḥ samayakriyā vaiśyasya || 9.12 ||
triguṇe rājanyasya || 9.13 ||

kośavarjaṃ caturguṇe brāhmaṇasya || 9.14 ||

na brāhmaṇasya kośaṃ dadyāt || 9.15 ||

anyatrāgāmikālasamayanibandhanakriyātaḥ || 9.16 ||

kośasthāne brāhmaṇaṃ sītoddhṛtamahīkarameva śāpayet || 9.17 ||

prāgdṛṣṭadoṣe svalpe'pyarthe divyānāmanyatamameva kārayet || 9.18 ||

satsu viditaṃ saccaritaṃ na mahatyarthe'pi || 9.19 ||

abhiyoktā vartayecchīrṣam || 9.20 ||

abhiyuktaśca divyaṃ kuryāt || 9.21 ||

rājadrohasāhaseṣu vināpi śīrṣavartanāt || 9.22 ||

strībrāhmaṇavikalāsamartharogiṇāṃ tulā deyā || 9.23 ||

sā ca na vāti vāyau || 9.24 ||

na kuṣṭhyasamarthalohakārāṇāmagnirdeyaḥ || 9.25 ||

śaradgrīṣmayośca || 9.26 ||

na kuṣṭhipaittikabrāhmaṇānāṃ viṣaṃ deyam || 9.27 ||

prāvṛṣi ca || 9.28 ||

na śleṣmavyādhyarditānāṃ bhīrūṇāṃ śvāsakāsināmambujīvināṃ codakam || 9.29 ||

hemantaśiśirayośca || 9.30 ||

na nāstikebhyaḥ kośo deyaḥ || 9.31 ||

na deśe vyādhimarakopasṛṣṭe ce || 9.32 ||

sacailasnātamāhūya sūryodaya upoṣitam  |
kārayetsarvadivyāni devabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau  || 9.33 ||



10

atha dhaṭaḥ || 10.1 ||

caturhastocchrito dvihastāyataḥ || 10.2 ||

tatra sāravṛkṣodbhavā pañcahastāyatobhayataḥśikyā tulā || 10.3 ||

tāṃ ca suvarṇakārakāṃsyakārāṇāmanyatamo bibhṛyāt || 10.4 ||

tatra caikasmin śikye puruṣaṃ divyakāriṇamāropayet, dvitīye pratimānaṃ śilādi || 10.5 ||

pratimānapuruṣau samadhṛtau sucihnitau kṛtvā puruṣamavatārayet || 10.6 ||

dhaṭaṃ ca samayena gṛhṇīyāt || 10.7 ||

tulādhāraṃ ca || 10.8 ||

brahmaghnāṃ ye smṛtā lokā ye lokāḥ kūṭasākṣiṇām  |
tulādhārasya te lokāstulāṃ dhārayato mṛṣā  || 10.9 ||

dharmaparyāyavacanairdhaṭa ityabhidhīyase  |
tvameva dhaṭa jānīṣe na viduryāni mānuṣāḥ  || 10.10 ||

vyavahārābhiśasto'yaṃ mānuṣastolyate tvayi  |
tadenaṃ saṃśayādasmāddharmatastrātumarhasi  || 10.11 ||

tatastvāropayecchikye bhūya evātha taṃ naram  |
tulito yadi vardheta tataḥ śuddhaḥ sa dharmataḥ  || 10.12 ||

śikyacchedākṣabhaṅgeṣu bhūyastvāropayennaram  |
evaṃ niḥsaṃśayaṃ jñānaṃ yato bhavati nirṇayaḥ  || 10.13 ||



11

athāgniḥ || 11.1 ||

ṣoḍaśāṅgulaṃ tāvadantaraṃ maṇḍalasaptakaṃ kuryāt || 11.2 ||

tataḥ prāṅmukhasya prasāritabhujadvayasya saptāśvatthapatrāṇi karayordadyāt || 11.3 ||

tāni ca karadvayasahitāni sūtreṇa veṣṭayet || 11.4 ||

tatastatrāgnivarṇaṃ lohapiṇḍaṃ pañcāśatpalikaṃ samaṃ nyaset || 11.5 ||

tamādāya nātidrutaṃ nātivilambitaṃ maṇḍaleṣu pādanyāsaṃ kurvan vrajet || 11.6 ||

tataḥ saptamaṃ maṇḍalamatītya bhūmau lohapiṇḍaṃ jahyāt || 11.7 ||

yo hastayoḥ kvaciddagdhastamaśuddhaṃ vinirdiṣet |
na dagdhaḥ sarvathā yastu sa viśuddho bhavennaraḥ  || 11.8 ||

bhayādvā pātayedyastu dagdho vā na vibhāvyate  |
punastaṃ hārayellohaṃ samayasyāviśodhanāt || 11.9 ||

karau vimṛditavrīhestasyādāveva lakṣayet |
abhimantryāsya karayorlohapiṇḍaṃ tato nyaset || 11.10 ||

tvamagne sarvabhūtānāmantaścarasi sākṣivat |
tvamevāgne vijānīṣe na viduryāni mānavāḥ  || 11.11 ||

vyavahārābhiśasto'yaṃ mānuṣaḥ śuddhimicchati  |
tadenaṃ saṃśayādasmāddharmatastrātumarhasi  || 11.12 ||



12

athodakam || 12.1 ||

paṅkaśaivāladuṣṭagrāhamatsyajalaukādivarjite'mbhasi || 12.2 ||

tatra ...nābhimagnasyārogadveṣiṇaḥ puruṣasyānyasya jānunī gṛhītvābhimantritamambhaḥ praviśet || 12.3 ||

tatsamakālaṃ ca nātikrūramṛdunā dhanuṣā puruṣo'paraḥ śarakṣepaṃ kuryāt || 12.4 ||

taṃ cāparaḥ puruṣo javena śaramānayet || 12.5 ||

tanmadhye yo na dṛśyeta sa śuddhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  |
anyathā hyaviśuddhaḥ syādekāṅgasyāpi darśane  || 12.6 ||

tvamambhaḥ sarvabhūtānāmantaścarasi sākṣivat |
tvamevāmbho vijānīṣe na viduryāni mānuṣāḥ  || 12.7 ||

vyavahārābhiśato'yaṃ mānuṣastvayi majjati  |
tadenaṃ saṃśayādasmāddharmatastrātumarhasi  || 12.8 ||



13

atha viṣam || 13.1 ||

viṣānyadeyāni sārvāṇi || 13.2 ||

ṛte himācalodbhavātśārṅgāt || 13.3 ||

tasya ca yavasaptakaṃ ghṛtaplutamabhiśastāya dadyāt || 13.4 ||

viṣaṃ vegaklamāpetaṃ sukhena yadi jīryate  |
viśuddhaṃ tamiti jñātvā divasānte visarjayet || 13.5 ||

viṣatvādviṣamatvācca krūraṃ tvaṃ sarvadehinām  |
tvameva viṣa jānīṣe na viduryāni mānuṣāḥ  || 13.6 ||

vyavahārābhiśasto'yaṃ mānuṣaḥ śuddhimicchati  |
tadenaṃ saṃśayādasmāddharmatastrātumarhasi  || 13.7 ||



14

atha kośaḥ || 14.1 ||

ugrān devān samabhyarcya tatsnānodakātprasṛtitrayaṃ pibet || 14.2 ||

idaṃ mayā na kṛtamiti vadan sthāpitadevatābhimukhaḥ || 14.3 ||

yasya paśyeddvisaptāhāttrisaptāhādathāpi vā  |
rogo'gnirjñātimaraṇaṃ rājātaṅkamathāpi vā  || 14.4 ||

tamaśuddhaṃ vijānīyāttathā śuddhaṃ viparyaye  |
divye ca śuddhaṃ puruṣaṃ satkuryāddhārmiko nṛpaḥ  || 14.5 ||



15

atha dvādaśa putrā bhavanti || 15.1 ||

svakṣetre saṃskṛtāyāmutpāditaḥ svayamaurasaḥ prathamaḥ || 15.2 ||

niyuktāyāṃ sapiṇḍenottamavarṇena votpāditaḥ kṣetrajo dvitīyaḥ || 15.3 ||

putrīkāputrastṛtīyaḥ || 15.4 ||

yastvasyāḥ putraḥ sa me putro bhavediti yā pitrā dattā sā putrikā || 15.5 ||

putrikāvidhiṃ vināpi pratipāditā bhrātṛvihīnā putrikaiva || 15.6 ||

paunarbhavaścaturthaḥ || 15.7 ||

akṣatā bhūyaḥ saṃskṛtā punarbhūḥ || 15.8 ||

bhūyastvasaṃskṛtāpi parapūrvā || 15.9 ||

kānīnaḥ pañcamaḥ || 15.10 ||

pitṛgṛhe asaṃskṛtayaivotpāditaḥ || 15.11 ||

sa ca pāṇigrāhasya || 15.12 ||

gṛhe ca gūḍhotpannaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ || 15.13 ||

yasya talpajastasyāsau || 15.14 ||

sahoḍhaḥ saptamaḥ || 15.15 ||

yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate tasyāḥ putraḥ || 15.16 ||

sa ca pāṇigrāhasya || 15.17 ||

dattakaścāṣṭamaḥ || 15.18 ||

sa ca mātāpitṛbhyāṃ yasya dattaḥ || 15.19 ||

krītaśca navamaḥ || 15.20 ||

sa ca yena krītaḥ || 15.21 ||

svayamupagato daśamaḥ || 15.22 ||

sa ca yasyopagataḥ || 15.23 ||

apaviddhastvekādaśaḥ || 15.24 ||

pitrā mātrā ca parityaktaḥ || 15.25 ||

sa ca yena gṛhītaḥ || 15.26 ||

yatra kvacanotpāditaśca dvādaśaḥ || 15.27 ||

eteṣāṃ pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān || 15.28 ||

sa eva dāyaharaḥ || 15.29 ||
sa cānyān bibhṛyāt || 15.30 ||

anūḍhānāṃ svavittānurūpeṇa saṃskāraṃ kuryāt || 15.31 ||

patitaklībācikitsyarogavikalāstvabhāgahāriṇaḥ || 15.32 ||

rikthagrāhibhiste bhartavyāḥ || 15.33 ||

teṣāṃ caurasāḥ putrā bhāgahāriṇaḥ || 15.34 ||

na tu patitasya || 15.35 ||

patanīye karmaṇi kṛte tvanantarotpannāḥ || 15.36 ||

pratilomāsu strīṣu cotpannāścābhāginaḥ || 15.37 ||

tatputrāḥ paitāmahe'pyarthe || 15.38 ||

aṃśagrāhibhiste bharaṇīyāḥ || 15.39 ||

yaścārthaharaḥ sa piṇḍadāyī || 15.40 ||

ekoḍhānāmapyekasyāḥ putraḥ sarvāsāṃ putra eva || 15.41 ||

bhrātṝṇāmekajātānāṃ ca || 15.42 ||

putraḥ pitṛvittālābhe'pi piṇḍaṃ dadyāt || 15.43 ||

punnāmno narakādyasmātpitaraṃ trāyate sutaḥ  |
tasmātputtra iti proktaḥ svayameva svayaṃbhuvā  || 15.44 ||

ṛṇamasmin saṃnayatyamṛtatvaṃ ca gacchati  |
pitā putrasya jātasya paśyeccejjīvato mukham  || 15.45 ||

putreṇa lokān jayati pautreṇānantyamaśnute  |
atha putrasya pautreṇa bradhnasyāpnoti viṣṭapam  || 15.46 ||

pautradauhitrayorloke viśeṣo nopapadyate  |
dauhitro'pi hyaputraṃ taṃ saṃtārayati pautravat || 15.47 ||



16

samānavarṇāsu putrāḥ savarṇā bhavanti || 16.1 ||

anulomāsu mātṛsavarṇāḥ || 16.2 ||

pratilomāsvāryavigarhitāḥ || 16.3 ||

tatra vaiśyāputraḥ śūdreṇāyogavaḥ || 16.4 ||

pulkasamāgadhau kṣatriyāputrau vaiśyaśūdrābhyām || 16.5 ||

caṇḍālavaidehakasūtāśca brāhmaṇīputrāḥ śūdraviṭkṣatriyaiḥ || 16.6 ||

saṃkarasaṃkarāścāsaṃkhyeyāḥ || 16.7 ||

raṅgāvataraṇamāyogavānām || 16.8 ||

vyādhatā pulkasānām || 16.9 ||

stutikriyā māgadhānām || 16.10 ||

vadhyaghātitvaṃ caṇḍālānām || 16.11 ||

strīraksā tajjīvanaṃ ca vaidehakānām || 16.12 ||

aśvasārathyaṃ sūtānām || 16.13 ||

caṇḍālānāṃ bahirgrāmanivasanaṃ mṛtacailadhāraṇamiti viśeṣaḥ || 16.14 ||

sarveṣāṃ ca samānajātibhirvivāhaḥ || 16.15 ||

svapitṛvittānuharaṇaṃ ca || 16.16 ||

saṃkare jātayastvetāḥ pitṛmātṛpradarśitāḥ  |
pracchannā vā prakāśā vā veditavyāḥ svakarmabhiḥ  || 16.17 ||

brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā dehatyāgo'nupaskṛtaḥ  |
strībālādyavapattau ca bāhyānāṃ siddhikāraṇam  || 16.18 ||



17

pitā cetputrān vibhajettasya svecchā svayamupārjite'rthe || 17.1 ||

paitāmahe tvarthe pitṛputrayostulyaṃ svāmitvam || 17.2 ||

pitṛvibhaktā vibhāgānantarotpannasya bhāgaṃ dadyuḥ || 17.3 ||

aputradhanaṃ patnyabhigāmi || 17.4 ||

tadabhāve duhitṛgāmi || 17.5 ||

tadabhāve pitṛgāmi || 17.6 ||

tadabhāve mātṛgāmi || 17.7 ||

tadabhāve bhrātṛgāmi || 17.8 ||

tadabhāve bhrātṛputragāmi || 17.9 ||

tadabhāve bandhugāmi || 17.10 ||

tadabhāve sakulyagāmi || 17.11 ||

tadabhāve sahādhyāyigāmi || 17.12 ||

tadabhāve brāhmaṇadhanavarjaṃ rājagāmi || 17.13 ||

brāhmaṇārtho brāhmaṇānām || 17.14 ||

vānaprasthadhanamācāryo gṛhṇīyāt || 17.15 ||

śiṣyo vā || 17.16 ||

saṃsṛṣṭinastu saṃsṛṣṭī sodarasya tu sodaraḥ  |
dadyādapahareccāṃśaṃ jātasya ca mṛtasya ca  || 17.17 ||

pitṛmātṛsutabhrātṛdattam, adhyagnyupāgatam, ādhivedanikaṃ, bandhudattaṃ, śulkam, anvādheyakamiti strīdhanam || 17.18 ||

brāhmādiṣu caturṣu vivāheṣvaprajāyāmatītāyāṃ tadbhartuḥ || 17.19 ||

śeṣeṣu ca pitā haret || 17.20 ||

sarveṣveva prasūtāyāṃ yaddhanaṃ tatduhitṛgāmi || 17.21 ||

patyau jīvati yaḥ strībhiralaṃkāro dhṛto bhavet |
na taṃ bhajeran dāyādā bhajamānāḥ patanti te  || 17.22 ||

anekapitṛkāṇāṃ tu pitṛto'ṃśaprakalpanā  |
yasya yatpaitṛkaṃ rikthaṃ sa tadgṛhṇīta netaraḥ  || 17.23 ||



18

brāhmaṇasya caturṣu varṇeṣu cetputrā bhaveyuḥ, te paitṛkaṃ rikthaṃ daśadhā vibhajeyuḥ || 18.1 ||

tatra brāhmaṇīputraścaturo'ṃśānādadyāt || 18.2 ||

kṣatriyāputrastrīn || 18.3 ||

dvāvaṃśau vaiśyāputraḥ || 18.4 ||

śūdrāputrastvekam || 18.5 ||

atha cetśūdravarjaṃ brāhmaṇasya putratrayaṃ bhavet, tadā taddhanaṃ navadhā vibhajeyuḥ || 18.6 ||

varnānukrameṇa catustridvibhāgīkṛtānaṃśānādadyuḥ || 18.7 ||

vaiśyavarjamaṣṭadhā kṛtaṃ caturastrīnekaṃ cādadyuḥ || 18.8 ||

kṣatriyavarjaṃ saptadhā kṛtaṃ caturo dvāvekaṃ ca || 18.9 ||

brāhmaṇavarjaṃ ṣaḍdhā kṛtaṃ trīn dvāvekaṃ ca || 18.10 ||

kṣatriyasya kṣatriyāvaiśyāśūdrāputreṣvayameva vibhāgaḥ || 18.11 ||

atha brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇakṣatriyau putrau syātāṃ, tadā saptadhā kṛtāddhanādbrāhmaṇaścaturo'ṃśānādadyāt || 18.12 ||

trīn rājanyaḥ || 18.13 ||

atha brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇavaiśyau, tadā ṣaḍdhā vibhaktasya caturo'ṃśān brāhmaṇastvādadyāt || 18.14 ||

dvāvaṃśau vaiśyaḥ || 18.15 ||

atha brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇaśūdrau putrau syātāṃ, tadā taddhanaṃ pañcadhā vibhajeyātām || 18.16 ||

caturo'ṃśān brāhmaṇastvādadyāt || 18.17 ||

ekaṃ śūdraḥ || 18.18 ||

atha brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya vā kṣatriyavaiśyau putrau syātāṃ, tadā taddhanaṃ pañcadhā vibhajeyātām || 18.19 ||

trīnaṃśān kṣatriyastvādadyāt || 18.20 ||

dvāvaṃśau vaiśyaḥ || 18.21 ||

atha brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya vā kṣatriyaśūdrau putrau syātāṃ, tadā taddhanaṃ caturdhā vibhajeyātām || 18.22 ||

trīnaṃśān kṣatriyastvādadyāt || 18.23 ||

ekaṃ śūdraḥ || 18.24 ||

atha brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya vaiśyasya vā vaiśyaśūdrau putrau syātāṃ, tadā taddhanaṃ tridhā vibhajeyātām || 18.25 ||

dvāvaṃśau vaiśyastvādadyāt || 18.26 ||

ekaṃ śūdraḥ || 18.27 ||

athaikaputrā brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyāḥ sarvaharāḥ || 18.28 ||

kṣatriyasya rājanyavaiśyau || 18.29 ||

vaiśyasya vaiśyaḥ || 18.30 ||

śūdraḥ śūdrasya || 18.31 ||

dvijātīnāṃ śūdrastvekaḥ putro'rdhaharaḥ || 18.32 ||

aputrarikthasya yā gatiḥ, sātrārdhasya dvitīyasya || 18.33 ||

mātaraḥ putrabhāgānusāreṇa bhāgāpahāriṇyaḥ || 18.34 ||

anūḍhāśca duhitaraḥ || 18.35 ||

samānavarṇāḥ putrāḥ samānaṃśānādadyuḥ || 18.36 ||

jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhamuddhāraṃ dadyuḥ || 18.37 ||

yadi dvau brāhmaṇīputrau syātāmekaḥ śūdrāputraḥ, tadā navadhā vibhaktasyārthasya brāhmaṇīputrāvaṣṭau bhāgānādadyātāmekaṃ śūdrāputraḥ || 18.38 ||

atha śūdrāputrāvubhau syātāmeko brāhmaṇīputraḥ, tadā ṣaḍdhā vibhaktasyārthasya caturo'ṃśān brāhmaṇastvādadyāt, dvāvaṃśau śūdrāputrau || 18.39 ||

anena krameṇānyatrāpyaṃśakalpanā bhavati || 18.40 ||

vibhaktāḥ saha jīvanto vibhajeran punaryadi  |
samastatra vibhāgaḥ syājjyaiṣṭhyaṃ tatra na vidyate  || 18.41 ||

anupaghnan pitṛdravyaṃ śrameṇa yadupārjayet |
svayamīhitalabdhaṃ tannākāmo dātumarhati  || 18.42 ||

paitṛkaṃ tu yadā dravyamanavāptaṃ yadāpnuyāt |
na tatputrairbhajetsārdhamakāmaḥ svayamarjitam  || 18.43 ||

vastraṃ patramalaṃkāraḥ kṛtānnamudakaṃ striyaḥ  |
yogakṣemaṃ pracāraśca na vibhājyaṃ ca pustakam  || 18.44 ||


19

mṛtaṃ dvijaṃ na śūdreṇa nirhārayet || 19.1 ||

na śūdraṃ dvijena || 19.2 ||

pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca putrā nirhareyuḥ || 19.3 ||

na dvijaṃ pitaramapi śūdrāḥ || 19.4 ||

brāhmaṇamanāthaṃ ye brāhmaṇā nirharanti te svargalokabhājaḥ || 19.5 ||

nirhṛtya ca bāndhavaṃ pretaṃ saṃṣkṛtyāpradakṣiṇena citāmabhigamyāpsu savāsaso nimajjanaṃ kuryuḥ || 19.6 ||

pretasyodakanirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvaikaṃ piṇḍaṃ kuśeṣu dadyuḥ || 19.7 ||

privartitavāsasaśca nimbapatrāṇi vidaśya dvāryaśmani padanyāsaṃ kṛtvā gṛhaṃ praviśeyuḥ || 19.8 ||

akṣatāṃścāgnau kṣipeyuḥ || 19.9 ||

caturthe divase'sthisaṃcayanaṃ kuryuḥ || 19.10 ||

teṣāṃ gaṅgāmbhasi prakṣepaḥ || 19.11 ||

yāvatsaṃkhyamasthi puruṣasya gaṅgāmbhasi tiṣṭhati, tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargalokamadhitiṣṭhati || 19.12 ||

yāvadāśaucaṃ tāvatpretasyodakaṃ piṇḍamekaṃ ca dadyuḥ || 19.13 ||

krītalabdhāśanāśca bhaveyuḥ || 19.14 ||

amāṃsāśanāśca || 19.15 ||

sthaṇḍilaśāyinaḥ || 19.16 ||

pṛthakśāyinaśca || 19.17 ||

grāmānniṣkramyāśaucānte kṛtaśmaśrukarmāṇastilakalkaiḥ sarṣapakalkairvā snātāḥ parivartitavāsaso gṛhaṃ praviśeyuḥ || 19.18 ||

tatra śāntiṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇānāṃ ca pūjanaṃ kuryuḥ || 19.19 ||

devāḥ parokṣadevāḥ, pratyakṣadevā brāhmāṇāḥ || 19.20 ||

brāhmaṇairlokā dhāryante || 19.21 ||

brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādena divi tiṣṭhanti devatāḥ  |
brāhmaṇābhihitaṃ vākyaṃ na mithyā jāyate kvacit || 19.22 ||

yadbrāhmaṇāstuṣṭatamā vadanti taddevatāḥ pratyabhinandayanti  |
tuṣṭeṣu tuṣṭāḥ satataṃ bhavanti pratyakṣadeveṣu parokṣadevāḥ  || 19.23 ||

duḥkhānvitānāṃ mṛtabāndhavānāmāśvāsanaṃ kuryuradīnasattvāḥ  |
vākyaistu yairbhūmi tavābhidhāsye vākyānyahaṃ tāni mano'bhirāme  || 19.24 ||



20

yaduttarāyaṇaṃ tadahardevānām || 20.1 ||

dakṣiṇāyanaṃ rātriḥ || 20.2 ||

saṃvatsaro'horātraḥ || 20.3 ||

tattriṃśatā māsāḥ || 20.4 ||

māsā dvādaśa varṣam || 20.5 ||

dvādaśa varṣaśatāni divyāni kaliyugam || 20.6 ||

dviguṇāni dvāparam || 20.7 ||

triguṇāni tretā || 20.8 ||

caturguṇāni kṛtayugam || 20.9 ||

dvādaśavarṣasahasrāṇi divyāni caturyugam || 20.10 ||

caturyugāṇāmekasaptatirmanvantaram || 20.11 ||

caturyugasahasraṃ ca kalpaḥ || 20.12 ||

sa ca pitāmahasyāhaḥ || 20.13 ||

tāvatī cāsya rātriḥ || 20.14 ||

evaṃvidhenāhorātreṇa māsavarṣagaṇanayā sarvasyaiva brahmaṇo varṣaśatamāyuḥ || 20.15 ||

brahmāyuṣā ca paricchinnaḥ pauruṣo divasaḥ || 20.16 ||

tasyānte mahākalpaḥ || 20.17 ||

tāvatyevāsya niśā || 20.18 ||

paurūṣeyāṇāmahorātrāṇāmatītānāṃ saṃkhyaiva nāsti || 20.19 ||

na ca bhaviṣyāṇām || 20.20 ||

anādyantatvātkālasya || 20.21 ||

evamasminnirālambe kāle satatayāyini  |
na tadbhūtaṃ prapaśyāmi sthitiryasya bhaveddhruvā  || 20.22 ||

gaṅgāyāḥ sikatā dhārāstathā varṣati vāsave  |
śakyā gaṇayituṃ loke na vyatītāḥ pitāmahāḥ  || 20.23 ||

caturdaśa vinaśyanti kalpe kalpe sureśvarāḥ  |
sarvalokapradhānāśca manavaśca caturdaśa  || 20.24 ||

bahūnīndrasahasrāṇi daityendraniyutāni ca  |
vinaṣtānīha kālena manujeṣvatha kā kathā  || 20.25 ||

rājarṣayaśca bahavaḥ sarvaiḥ samuditā guṇaiḥ  |
devā brahmarṣayaścaiva kālena nidhanaṃ gatāḥ  || 20.26 ||

ye samarthā jagatyasmin sṛṣṭisaṃhārakāraṇe  |
te'pi kālena nīyante kālo hi duratikramaḥ  || 20.27 ||

ākramya sarvaḥ kālena paralokaṃ ca nīyate  |
karmapāśavaśo jantustatra kā paridevanā  || 20.28 ||

jātasya hi dhruvo mṛtyurdhruvaṃ janma mṛtasya ca  |
arthe duṣparihārye'sminnāsti loke sahāyatā  || 20.29 ||

śocanto nopakurvanti mṛtasyeha janā yataḥ  |
ato na roditavyaṃ hi kriyā kāryā svaśaktitaḥ  || 20.30 ||

sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ cobhau sahāyau yasya gacchataḥ  |
bāndhavaistasya kiṃ kāryaṃ śocadbhiratha vā na vā  || 20.31 ||

bāndhavānāmaśauce tu sthitiṃ preto na vindati  |
atastvabhyeti tāneva piṇḍatoyapradāyinaḥ  || 20.32 ||

arvāksapiṇdīkaraṇātpreto bhavati yo mṛtaḥ  |
pretalokagatasyānnaṃ sodakumbhaṃ prayacchata  || 20.33 ||

pitṛlokagataścānnaṃ śrāddhe bhuṅkte svadhāsamam  |
pitṛlokagatasyāsya tasmācchrāddhaṃ prayacchata  || 20.34 ||

devatve yātanāsthāne tiryagyonau tathaiva ca  |
mānuṣye ca tathāpnoti śrāddhaṃ dattaṃ svabāndhavaiḥ  || 20.35 ||

pretasya śrāddhakartuśca puṣṭiḥ śrāddhe kṛte dhruvam  |
tasmācchrāddhaṃ sadā kāryaṃ śokaṃ tyaktvā nirarthakam  || 20.36 ||

etāvadeva kartavyaṃ sadā pretasya bandhubhiḥ  |
nopakuryānnaraḥ śocan pretasyātmana eva ca  || 20.37 ||

dṛṣṭvā lokamanākrandaṃ mriyamāṇāṃśca bāndhavān  |
dharmamekaṃ sahāyārthaṃ varayadhvaṃ sadā narāḥ  || 20.38 ||

mṛto'pi bāndhavaḥ śakto nānugantuṃ naraṃ mṛtam  |
jāyāvarjaṃ hi sarvasya yāmyaḥ panthā virudhyate  || 20.39 ||

dharma eko'nuyātyenaṃ yatra kvacana gāminam  |
nanvasāre nṛloke'smin dharmaṃ kuruta mā ciram  || 20.40 ||

śvaḥ kāryamadya kurvīta pūrvāhṇe cāparāhṇikam  |
na hi pratīkṣate mṛtyuḥ kṛtaṃ vāsya na vākṛtam  || 20.41 ||

kṣetrāpaṇagṛhāsaktamanyatra gatamānasam  |
vṛkīvoraṇamāsādya mṛtyurādāya gacchati  || 20.42 ||

na kālasya priyaḥ kaściddveṣyaścāsya na vidyate  |
āyuṣye karmaṇi kṣīṇe prasahya harate janam  || 20.43 ||

nāprāptakālo mriyate viddhaḥ śaraśatairapi  |
kuśāgreṇāpi saṃṣpṛṣṭaḥ prāptakālo na jīvati  || 20.44 ||

nauśadhāni na mantrāśca na homā na punarjapāḥ  |
trāyante mṛtyunopetaṃ jarayā vāpi mānavam  || 20.45 ||

āgāminamanarthaṃ hi pravidhānaśatairapi  |
na nivārayituṃ śaktastatra kā paridevanā  || 20.46 ||

yathā dhenusahasreṣu vatso vindati mātaram  |
tathā pūrvakṛtaṃ karma kartāraṃ vindate dhruvam  || 20.47 ||

avyaktādīni bhūtāni vyaktamadhyāni cāpyatha  |
avyaktanidhanānyeva tatra kā paridevanā  || 20.48 ||

dehino'smin yathā dehe kaumāraṃ yauvanaṃ jarā  |
tahtā dehāntaraprāptirdhīrastatra na muhyati  || 20.49 ||

gṛhṇātīha yathā vastraṃ tyaktvā pūrvadhṛtaṃ naraḥ  |
gṛhṇātyevaṃ navaṃ dehī dehaṃ karmanibandhanam  || 20.50 ||

nainaṃ chindanti śastrāṇi nainaṃ dahati pāvakaḥ  |
na cainaṃ kledayantyāpo na śoṣayati mārutaḥ  || 20.51 ||

acchedyo'yamadāhyo'yamakledyo'śoṣya eva ca  |
nityaḥ satatagaḥ sthāṇuracalo'yaṃ sanātanaḥ  || 20.52 ||

avyakto'yamacintyo'yamavikāryo'yamucyate  |
tasmādevaṃ viditvainaṃ nānuśocitumarhatha  || 20.53 ||



21

athāśaucavyapagame susnātaḥ suprakṣālitapāṇipādaḥ svācāntastvevaṃvidhāneva brāhmaṇān yathāśakti udaṅmukhān gandhamālyavastrālaṃkārādibhiḥ pūjitān bhojayet || 21.1 ||

ekavanmantrānūhedekoddiṣṭe || 21.2 ||

ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhāvekameva tannāmagotrābhyāṃ piṇḍaṃ nirvapet || 21.3 ||

bhuktavatsu brāhmaṇeṣu dakṣiṇayābhipūjiteṣu pretanāmagotrābhyāṃ dattākṣayyodakaḥ caturaṅgulapṛthvīḥ tāvadantarāḥ tāvadadhaḥkhātāḥ vitastyāyatāḥ tisraḥ karṣūḥ kuryāt || 21.4 ||

karṣūsamīpe cāgnitrayamupasamādhāya paristīrya tatraikaikasmināhutitrayaṃ juhuyāt || 21.5 ||

somāya pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ || 21.6 ||

agnaye kavyavāhanāya svathā namaḥ || 21.7 ||

yamāyāṅgirase svadhā namaḥ || 21.8 ||

sthānatraye ca prāgvatpiṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kuryāt || 21.9 ||

annadadhighṛtamadhumāṃsaiḥ karṣūtrayaṃ pūrayitvā etatta iti japet || 21.10 ||

evaṃ mṛtāhe pratimāsaṃ kuryāt || 21.11 ||

saṃvatsarānte pretāya tatpitre tatpitāmahāya tatprapitāmahāya ca brāhmaṇān devapūrvān bhojayet || 21.12 ||

atrāgnaukaraṇamāvāhanaṃ pādyaṃ ca kuryāt || 21.13 ||

saṃsṛjatu tvā pṛthivī samānī ca iti ca pretapādyapātre pitṛpādyapātratraye yojayet || 21.14 ||

ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau piṇḍacatuṣṭayaṃ kuryāt || 21.15 ||

brāhmaṇāṃśca svācāntān dattadakṣiṇāṃścānuvrajya visarjayet || 21.16 ||

tataḥ pretapiṇḍaṃ pādyapātrodakavatpiṇḍatraye nidadhyāt || 21.17 ||
karṣūtrayasaṃnikrṣe'pyevameva || 21.18 ||

sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ māsikārthavatdvādaśāhaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā trayodaśe'hni vā kuryāt || 21.19 ||

mantravarjaṃ hi śūdrāṇāṃ dvādaśe'hni || 21.20 ||

saṃvatsarābhyantare yadyadhimāso bhavet, tadā māsikārthe dinamekaṃ vardhayet || 21.21 ||

sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ strīṇāṃ kāryamevaṃ tathā bhavet |
yāvajjīvaṃ tathā kuryācchrāddhaṃ tu prativatsaram  || 21.22 ||

arvāksapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ yasya saṃvatsarātkṛtaṃ  |
tasyāpyannaṃ sodakumbhaṃ dadyādvarṣaṃ dvijanmane  || 21.23 ||



22

brāhmaṇasya sapiṇḍānāṃ jananamaraṇayordaśāhamāśaucam || 22.1 ||

dvādaśāhaṃ rājanyasya || 22.2 ||

pañcadaśāhaṃ vaiśyasya || 22.3 ||

māsaṃ śūdrasya || 22.4 ||

sapiṇḍatā ca puruṣe saptame vinivartate || 22.5 ||

āśauce homadānapratigrahasvādhyāyā nivartante || 22.6 ||

nāśauce kasyacidannamaśnīyāt || 22.7 ||

brāhmaṇādīnāmaśauce yaḥ sakṛdevānnamatti tasya tāvadāśaucaṃ yāvatteṣām || 22.8 ||

āśauśāpagame prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt || 22.9 ||

savarṇasyāśauce dvijo bhuktvā sravantīmāsādya tannimagnastriraghamarṣaṇaṃ japtvottīrya gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ japet || 22.10 ||

kṣatriyāśauce brāhmaṇastvetadevopoṣitaḥ kṛtvā śudhyati || 22.11 ||

vaiśyāśauce rājanyaśca || 22.12 ||

vaiśyāśauce brāhmaṇastrirātropoṣitaśca || 22.13 ||

brāhmaṇāśauce rājanyaḥ kṣatriyāśauce vaiśyaśca sravantīmāsādya gāyatrīśatapañcakaṃ japet || 22.14 ||

vaiśyaśca brāhmaṇāśauce gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet || 22.15 ||

śūdrāśauce dvijo bhuktvā prājāpatyaṃ caret || 22.16 ||

śūdraśca dvijāśauce snānamācaret || 22.17 ||

śūdraḥ śūdrāśauce snātaḥ pañcagavyaṃ pibet || 22.18 ||

patnīnāṃ dāsānāmānulomyena svāminastulyamāśaucam || 22.19 ||

mṛte svāminyātmīyam || 22.20 ||

hīnavarṇānāmadhikavarṇeṣu sapiṇḍeṣu tadaśaucavyapagame śuddhiḥ || 22.21 ||

brāhmaṇasya kṣatriviṭśūdreṣu sapiṇḍeṣu ṣaḍrātratrirātraikarātraiḥ || 22.22 ||

kṣatriyasya viṭśūdrayoḥ ṣaḍrātratrirātrābhyām || 22.23 ||

vaiśyasya śūdreṣu ṣaḍrātreṇa || 22.24 ||

māsatulyairahorātrairgarbhasrāve || 22.25 ||

jātamṛte mṛtajāte vā kulasya sadyaḥ śaucam || 22.26 ||

adantajāte bāle prete sadya eva || 22.27 ||

nāsyāgnisaṃskāro nodakakriyā || 22.28 ||

dantajāte tvakṛtacūḍe tvahorātreṇa || 22.29 ||

kṛtacūḍe tvasaṃskṛte trirātreṇa || 22.30 ||

tataḥ paraṃ yathoktakālena || 22.31 ||

strīṇāṃ vivāhaḥ saṃskāraḥ || 22.32 ||

saṃskṛtāsu strīṣu nāśaucaṃ pitṛpakṣe || 22.33 ||

tatprasavamaraṇe cetpitṛgṛhe syātāṃ , tadā ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ ca || 22.34 ||

jananāśaucamadhye yadyaparaṃ jananāśaucaṃ syāt, tadā pūrvāśaucavyapagame śuddhiḥ || 22.35 ||

rātriśeṣe dinadvayena || 22.36 ||

prabhāte dinatrayeṇa || 22.37 ||

maraṇāśaucamadhye jñātimaraṇe'pyevam || 22.38 ||

śrutvā deśāntarastho jananamaraṇe āśaucaśeṣeṇa śudhyet || 22.39 ||

vyatīte'śauce saṃvatsarāntastvekarātreṇa || 22.40 ||

tataḥ paraṃ snānena || 22.41 ||

ācārye mātāmahe ca vyatīte trirātreṇa || 22.42 ||

anauraseṣu putreṣu jāteṣu ca mṛteṣu ca  |
parapūrvāsu bhāryāsu prasūtāsu mṛtāsu ca  || 22.43 ||

ācāryapatnīputropādhyāyamātulaśvaśuraśvaśuryasahādhyāyiśiṣyeṣvatīteṣvekarātreṇa || 22.44 ||

svadeśarājani ca || 22.45 ||

asapiṇḍe svaveśmani mṛte ca || 22.46 ||

bhṛgvagnyanāśakāmbusaṃgrāmavidyunnṛpahatānāṃ nāśaucam || 22.47 ||

na rājñāṃ rajakarmaṇi || 22.48 ||

na vratināṃ vrate || 22.49 ||

na satriṇāṃ satre || 22.50 ||

na kārūṇāṃ kārukarmaṇi || 22.51 ||

na rājājñākāriṇāṃ tadicchayā || 22.52 ||

na devapratiṣṭhāvivāhayoḥ pūrvasaṃbhṛtayoḥ || 22.53 ||

na deśavibhrame || 22.54 ||

āpadyapi ca kaṣṭāyām || 22.55 ||

ātmatyāginaḥ patitāśca nāśaucodakabhājaḥ || 22.56 ||

patitasya dāsī mṛte'hni padā apāṃ ghaṭamapavarjayet || 22.57 ||

udbandhanamṛtasya yaḥ pāśaṃ chindyātsa taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati || 22.58 ||

ātmatyāgināṃ saṃskartā ca || 22.59 ||

tadaśrupātakārī ca || 22.60 ||

sarvasyaiva pretasya bāndhavaiḥ sahāśrupātaṃ kṛtvā snānena || 22.61 ||

akṛte'sthisaṃcaye sacailasnānena || 22.62 ||

dvijaḥ śūdrapretānugamanaṃ kṛtvā sravantīmāsādya tannimagnaḥ triraghamarṣaṇaṃ japtvottīrya gāyatryaṣṭasahasraṃ japet || 22.63 ||

dvijapretasyāṣṭaśatam || 22.64 ||

śūdraḥ pretānugamanaṃ kṛtvā snānamācaret || 22.65 ||

citādhūmasevane sarve varṇāḥ snānamācareyuḥ || 22.66 ||

maithune duḥsvapne rudhiropagatakaṇṭhe vamanavirekayośca || 22.67 ||

śmaśrukarmaṇi kṛte ca || 22.68 ||

śavaspṛśaṃ ca spṛṣṭvā rajasvalācaṇḍālayūpāṃśca || 22.69 ||

bhakṣyavarjaṃ pañcanakhaśavaṃ tadasthisnehaṃ ca || 22.70 ||

sarveṣveteṣu snāneṣu vastraṃ nāprakṣālitaṃ bibhṛyāt || 22.71 ||

rajasvalā caturthe'hni snānācchudhyati || 22.72 ||

rajasvalā hīnavarṇāṃ rajasvalāṃ spṛṣṭvā na tāvadaśnīyātyāvanna śuddhā || 22.73 ||

savarṇāmadhikavarṇāṃ vā spṛṣṭvā sadyaḥ snātvā śudhyati || 22.74 ||

kṣutvā suptvā bhuktvā bhojanādhyayanepsuḥ pītvā snātvā niṣṭhīvya vāsaḥ paridhāya rathyāmākramya mūtrapurīṣaṃ kṛtvā pañcanakhāsthyasnehaṃ spṛṣṭvā cācamet || 22.75 ||

caṇḍālamlecchasaṃbhāṣaṇe ca || 22.76 ||

nābheradhastātprabāhuṣu ca kāyikairmalaiḥ surābhirmadyaiścopahato mṛttoyaistadaṅgaṃ prakṣālya śudhyet || 22.77 ||

anyatropahato mṛttoyaistadaṅgaṃ prakṣālya snānena || 22.78 ||

vaktropahatastvupoṣya snātvā pañcagavyena || 22.79 ||

daśanacchadopahataśca || 22.80 ||

vasā śukramasṛṅmajjā mūtraṃ viṭkarṇaviṇnakhāḥ  |
śleṣmāśru dūṣikā svedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ  || 22.81 ||

gauḍī mādhvī ca paiṣṭī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā  |
yathaivaikā tathā sarvā na pātavyā dvijātibhiḥ  || 22.82 ||

mādhūkamaikṣavaṃ ṭāṅkaṃ kaulaṃ khārjūrapānase  |
mṛdvīkārasamādhvīke maireyaṃ nārikelajam  || 22.83 ||

amedhyāni daśaitāni madyāni brāhmaṇasya ca  |
rājanyaścaiva vaiśyaśca spṛṣṭvaitāni na duṣyataḥ  || 22.84 ||

guroḥ pretasya śiṣyastu pitṛmedhaṃ samācaran  |
pretāhāraiḥ samaṃ tatra daśarātreṇa śudhyati  || 22.85 ||

ācāryaṃ svamupādhyāyaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum  |
nirhṛtya tu vratī pretānna vratena viyujyate  || 22.86 ||

ādiṣṭī nodakaṃ kuryādā vratasya samāpanāt |
samāpte tūdakaṃ kṛtvā trirātreṇaiva śudhyati  || 22.87 ||

jñānaṃ tapo'gnirāhāro mṛnmano vāryupāñjanam  |
vāyuḥ karmārkakālau ca śuddhikartṝṇi dehinām  || 22.88 ||

sarveṣāmeva śaucānāmannaśaucaṃ paraṃ smṛtam  |
yo'nne śuciḥ sa hi śucirna mṛdvāriśuciḥ śuciḥ  || 22.89 ||

kṣāntyā śudhyanti vidvāṃso dānenākāryakāriṇaḥ  |
pracchannapāpā japyena tapasā vedavittamāḥ  || 22.90 ||

mṛttoyaiḥ śudhyate śodhyaṃ nadī vegena śudhyati  |
rajasā strī manoduṣṭā saṃnyāsena dvijottamāḥ  || 22.91 ||

adbhirgātrāṇi śudhyanti manaḥ satyena śudhyati  |
vidyātapobhyāṃ bhūtātmā buddhirjñānena śudhyati  || 22.92 ||

eṣa śaucasya te proktaḥ śārīrasya vinirṇayaḥ  |
nānāvidhānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śuddheḥ śṛṇu vinirṇayam  || 22.93 ||



23

śārīrairmalaiḥ surābhirmadyairvā yadupahataṃ tadatyantopahatam || 23.1 ||

atyantopahataṃ sarvaṃ lohabhāṇḍamagnau prakṣiptaṃ śudhyet || 23.2 ||

maṇimayamaśmamayamabjaṃ ca saptarātraṃ mahīnikhananena || 23.3 ||

śṛṅgadantāsthimayaṃ takṣaṇena || 23.4 ||

dāravaṃ mṛnmayaṃ ca jahyāt || 23.5 ||

atyantopahatasya vastrasya yatprakṣālitaṃ sadvirajyate tacchindyāt || 23.6 ||

sauvarṇarājatābjamaṇimayānāṃ nirlepānāmadbhiḥ śuddhiḥ || 23.7 ||

aśmamayānāṃ camasānāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca || 23.8 ||

carusruksruvāṇāmuṣṇenāmbhasā || 23.9 ||

yajñakarmaṇi yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇinā saṃmārjanena || 23.10 ||

sphyaśūrpaśakaṭamusalolūkhalānāṃ prokṣaṇena || 23.11 ||

śayanayānāsanānāṃ ca || 23.12 ||

bahūnāṃ ca || 23.13 ||

dhānyājinarajjutāntavavaidalasūtrakārpāsavāsasāṃ ca || 23.14 ||

śākamūlaphalapuṣpāṇāṃ ca || 23.15 ||

tṛṇakāṣṭhaśuṣkapalāśānāṃ ca || 23.16 ||

eteṣāṃ prakṣālanena || 23.17 ||

alpānāṃ ca || 23.18 ||

ūṣaiḥ kauśeyāvikayoḥ || 23.19 ||

ariṣṭakaiḥ kutapānām || 23.20 ||

śrīphalairaṃśupaṭṭānām || 23.21 ||

gaurasarṣapaiḥ kṣaumāṇām || 23.22 ||

śṛṅgāsthidantamayānāṃ ca || 23.23 ||

padmākṣairmṛgalomikānām || 23.24 ||

tāmrarītitrapusīsamayānāmamlodakena || 23.25 ||

bhasmanā kāṃsyalohayoḥ || 23.26 ||

takṣaṇena dāravāṇām || 23.27 ||

govālaiḥ phalasaṃbhavānām || 23.28 ||

prokṣaṇena saṃhatānām || 23.29 ||

utpavanena dravāṇām || 23.30 ||

guḍādīnāmikṣuvikārāṇāṃ prabhūtānāṃ gṛhanihitānāṃ vāryagnidānena || 23.31 ||

sarvalavaṇānāṃ ca || 23.32 ||

punaḥ pākena mṛnmayānām || 23.33 ||

dravyavatkṛtaśaucānāṃ devatārcānāṃ bhūyaḥ pratiṣṭhāpanena || 23.34 ||

asiddhasyānnasya yanmātramupahataṃ tanmātraṃ parityajya śeṣasya kaṇḍanaprakṣālane kuryāt || 23.35 ||

droṇābhyadhikaṃ siddhamannamupahataṃ na duṣyati || 23.36 ||

tasyopahatamātramapāsya gāyatryābhimantritaṃ suvarṇāmbhaḥ prakṣipetbastasya ca pradarśayedagneśca || 23.37 ||

pakṣijagdhaṃ gavā ghrātamavadhūtamavakṣutam  |
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiśca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati  || 23.38 ||

yāvannāpaityamedhyāktādgandho lepaśca tatkṛtaḥ  |
tāvanmṛdvāri deyaṃ syātsarvāsu dravyaśuddhiṣu  || 23.39 ||

ajāśvaṃ mukhato medhyaṃ na gaurna narajā malāḥ  |
panthānaśca viśudhyanti somasūryāṃśumārutaiḥ  || 23.40 ||

rathyākardamatoyāni spṛṣṭānyantyaśvavāyasaiḥ  |
mārutenaiva śudhyanti pakveṣṭakacitāni ca  || 23.41 ||

prāṇināmatha sarveṣāṃ mṛdbhiradbhiśca kārayet |
atyantopahatānāṃ ca śaucaṃ nityamatandritam  || 23.42 ||

bhūmiṣṭhamudakaṃ puṇyaṃ vaitṛṣṇyaṃ yatra gorbhavet |
avyāptaṃ cedamedhyena tadvadeva śilāgatam  || 23.43 ||

mṛtapañcanakhātkūpādatyantopahatāttathā  |
apaḥ samuddharetsarvāḥ śeṣaṃ vastreṇa śodhayet || 23.44 ||

vahniprajvālanaṃ kuryātkūpe pakveṣṭakācite  |
pañcagavyaṃ nyasetpaścānnavatoyasamudbhave  || 23.45 ||

jalāśayeṣvathālpeṣu sthāvareṣu vasuṃdhare  |
kūpavatkathitā śuddhirmahatsu ca na dūṣaṇam  || 23.46 ||

trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānāmakalpayan  |
adṛṣṭamadbhirnirṇiktaṃ yacca vācā praśasyate  || 23.47 ||

nityaṃ śuddhaḥ kāruhastaḥ paṇyaṃ yacca prasāritam  |
brāhamaṇāntaritaṃ bhaikṣyamākarāḥ sarva eva ca  || 23.48 ||

nityamāsyaṃ śuci strīṇāṃ śakuniḥ phalapātane  |
prasrave ca śucirvatsaḥ śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ  || 23.49 ||

śvabhirhatasya yanmāṃsaṃ śuci tatparikīrtitam  |
kravyādbhiśca hatasyānyaiścaṇḍālādyaiśca dasyubhiḥ  || 23.50 ||

ūrdhvaṃ nābheryāni khāni tāni medhyāni nirdiśet |
yānyadhastānyamedhyāni dehāccaiva malāścyutāḥ  || 23.51 ||

makṣikā vipruṣaśchāyā gaurgajāśvamarīcayaḥ  |
rajo bhūrvāyuragniśca mārjāraśca sadā śuciḥ  || 23.52 ||

nocchiṣṭaṃ kurvate mukhyā vipruṣo'ṅge na yānti yāḥ  |
na śmaśrūṇi gatānyāsyaṃ na dantāntaraveṣṭitam  || 23.53 ||

spṛśanti bindavaḥ pādau ya ācāmayataḥ parān  |
bhaumikaiste samā jñeyā na tairaprayato bhavet || 23.54 ||

ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravyahastaḥ kathaṃcana  |
anidhāyaiva taddravyamācāntaḥ śucitāmiyāt || 23.55 ||

mārjanopāñjanairveśma prokṣaṇenaiva pustakam  |
saṃmārjanenāñjanena sekenollekhanena ca  || 23.56 ||

dāhena ca bhuvaḥ śuddhirvāsenāpyatha vā gavām  |
gāvaḥ pavitramaṅgalyaṃ goṣu lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ  || 23.57 ||

gāvo vitanvate yjñaṃ gāvaḥ sarvāghasūdanāḥ  |
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ sarpiḥ kṣīraṃ dadhi ca rocanā  || 23.58 ||

ṣaḍaṅgametatparamaṃ maṅgalyaṃ paramaṃ gavām  |
śṛṅgodakaṃ gavāṃ puṇyaṃ sarvāghaviniṣūdanam  || 23.59 ||

gavāṃ kaṇḍūyanaṃ caiva sarvakalmaṣanāśanam  |
gavāṃ grāsapradānena svargaloke mahīyate  || 23.60 ||

gavāṃ hi tīrthe vasatīha gaṅgā puṣṭistathāsāṃ rajasi pravṛddhā  |
lakṣmīḥ karīṣe praṇatau ca dharmastāsāṃ praṇāmaṃ satataṃ ca kuryāt || 23.61 ||



24

atha brāhmaṇasya varṇānukrameṇa catasro bhāryā bhavanti || 24.1 ||

tisraḥ kṣatriyasya || 24.2 ||

dve vaiśyasya || 24.3 ||

ekā śūdrasya || 24.4 ||

tāsāṃ savarṇāvedane pāṇirgrāhyaḥ || 24.5 ||

asavarṇāvedane śaraḥ kṣatriyakanyayā || 24.6 ||

pratodo vaiśyakanyayā || 24.7 ||

vasanadaśāntaḥ śūdrakanyayā || 24.8 ||

na sagotrāṃ na samānārṣapravarāṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta || 24.9 ||

mātṛtastvā pañcamātpuruṣāt*pitṛtaścā saptamāt[pitṛtāś] || 24.10 ||

nākulīnām || 24.11 ||

na ca vyādhitām || 24.12 ||

nādhikāṅgīm || 24.13 ||

na hīnāṅgīm || 24.14 ||

nātikapilām || 24.15 ||

na vācāṭām || 24.16 ||

athāṣṭau vivāhā bhavanti || 24.17 ||

brāhmo daiva ārṣaḥ prājāpatyo gāndharva āsuro rākṣasaḥ paiśācaśceti || 24.18 ||

āhūya guṇavate kanyādānaṃ brāhmaḥ || 24.19 ||

yajñasthartvije daivaḥ || 24.20 ||

gomithunagrahaṇenārṣaḥ || 24.21 ||

prārthitapradānena prājāpatyaḥ || 24.22 ||

dvayoḥ sakāmayormātāpitṛrahito yogo gāndharvaḥ || 24.23 ||

krayeṇāsuraḥ || 24.24 ||

yuddhaharaṇena rākṣasaḥ || 24.25 ||

suptapramattābhigamanāt*paiśācaḥ [paiśacaḥ] || 24.26 ||

eteṣvādyāścatvāro dharmyāḥ || 24.27 ||

gāndharvo'pi rājanyānām || 24.28 ||

brāhmīputraḥ puruṣānekaviṃśatiṃ punīte || 24.29 ||

daivīputraścaturdaśa || 24.30 ||

ārṣīputraśca sapta || 24.31 ||

prājāpatyaścaturaḥ || 24.32 ||

brāhmeṇa vivāhena kanyāṃ dadatbrahmalokaṃ gamayati || 24.33 ||

daivena svargam || 24.34 ||

ārṣeṇa vaiṣṇavam || 24.35 ||

prājāpatyena devalokam || 24.36 ||

gāndharveṇa gandharvalokaṃ gacchati || 24.37 ||

pitā pitāmaho bhrātā sakulyo mātāmaho mātā ceti kanyāpradāḥ || 24.38 ||

pūrvābhāve prakṛtisthaḥ paraḥ para iti || 24.39 ||

ṛtutrayamupāsyaiva kanyā kuryātsvayaṃ varam  |
ṛtutraye vyatīte tu prabhavatyātmanaḥ sadā  || 24.40 ||

pitṛveśmani yā kanyā rajaḥ paśyatyasaṃskṛtā  |
sā kanyā vṛṣalī jñeyā haraṃstāṃ na viduṣyati  || 24.41 ||



25

atha strīṇāṃ dharmāḥ || 25.1 ||

bhartuḥ samānavratacāritvam || 25.2 ||

śvaśrūśvaśuragurudevatātithipūjanam || 25.3 ||

susaṃskṛtopaskaratā || 25.4 ||

amuktahastatā || 25.5 ||

suguptabhāṇḍatā || 25.6 ||

mūlakriyāsvanabhiratiḥ || 25.7 ||

maṅgalācāratatparatā || 25.8 ||

bhartari pravasite'pratikarmakriyā || 25.9 ||

paragṛheṣvanabhigamanam || 25.10 ||

dvāradeśagavākṣeṣvanavasthānam || 25.11 ||

sarvakarmasvasvatantratā || 25.12 ||

bālyayauvanavārdhakeṣvapi pitṛbhartṛputrādhīnatā || 25.13 ||

mṛte bhartari brahmacaryaṃ tadanvārohaṇaṃ vā || 25.14 ||

nāsti strīṇāṃ pṛthagyajño na vrataṃ nāpyupoṣitam  |
patiṃ śuśrūṣate yattu tena svarge mahīyate  || 25.15 ||

patyau jīvati yā yoṣidupavāsavrataṃ caret |
āyuḥ sā harate bharturnarakaṃ caiva gacchati  || 25.16 ||

mṛte bhartari sādhvī strī brahmacarye vyavasthitā  |
svargaṃ gacchatyaputrāpi yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ  || 25.17 ||



26

savarṇāsu bahubhāryāsu vidyamānāsu *jyeṣṭhayā saha dharmakāryaṃ kuryāt[jyeṣṭayā] || 26.1 ||

miśrāsu ca kaniṣṭhayāpi samānavarṇayā || 26.2 ||

samānavarṇāyā abhāve tvanantarayaivāpdi ca || 26.3 ||

na tveva dvijaḥ śūdrayā || 26.4 ||

dvijasya bhāryā śūdrā tu dharmārthaṃ na kvacidbhavet |
ratyarthameva sā tasya rāgāndhasya prakīrtitā  || 26.5 ||

hīnajātiṃ striyaṃ mohādudvahanto dvijātayaḥ  |
kulānyeva nayantyāśu sasaṃtānāni śūdratāṃ  || 26.6 ||

daivapitryāthiteyāni tatpradhānāni yasya tu  |
nāśnanti pitṛdevāstu na ca svargaṃ sa gacchati  || 26.7 ||



27

garbhasya spaṣṭatājñāne niṣekakarma || 27.1 ||

spandanātpurā puṃsavanam || 27.2 ||

*ṣaṣṭhe'ṣṭame vā māsi sīmantonnayanam [paṣṭhe] || 27.3 ||

jāte ca dārake jātakarma || 27.4 ||

āśaucavyapagame nāmadheyam || 27.5 ||

maṅgalyaṃ brāhamaṇasya || 27.6 ||

balavatkṣatriyasya || 27.7 ||

dhanopetaṃ vaiśyasya || 27.8 ||

jugupsitaṃ śūdrasya || 27.9 ||

caturthe māsyādityadarśanam || 27.10 ||

ṣaṣṭhe'nnaprāśanam || 27.11 ||

tṛtīye'bde cūḍākaraṇam || 27.12 ||

etā eva kriyāḥ strīṇāmamantrakāḥ || 27.13 ||

tāsāṃ samantrako vivāhaḥ || 27.14 ||

garbhāṣṭame'bde brāhmaṇasyopanayanam || 27.15 ||

garbhaikādaśe rājñaḥ || 27.16 ||

garbhadvādaśe viśaḥ || 27.17 ||

teṣāṃ muñjajyābalbajamayyo mauñjyaḥ || 27.18 ||

kārpāsaśāṇāvikānyupavītāni vāsāṃsi ca || 27.19 ||

mārgavaiyāghrabāstāni carmāṇi || 27.20 ||

pālāśakhādiraudumbarā daṇḍāḥ || 27.21 ||

keśāntalalāṭanāsādeśatulyāḥ || 27.22 ||

sarva eva vā || 27.23 ||

akuṭilāḥ satvacaśca || 27.24 ||

bhavadādyaṃ bhavanmadhyaṃ bhavadantaṃ ca bhaikṣyacaranam || 27.25 ||

ā ṣoḍaśādbrāhmaṇasya sāvitrī nātivartate  |
ā dvāviṃśātkṣatrabandhorā caturviṃśaterviśaḥ  || 27.26 ||

ata ūrdhvaṃ trayo'pyete yathākālamasaṃskṛtāḥ  |
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā bhavantyāryavigarhitāḥ  || 27.27 ||

yadyasya vihitaṃ carma yatsūtraṃ yā ca mekhalā  |
yo daṇḍo yacca vasanaṃ tattadasya vrateṣvapi  || 27.28 ||

mekhalāmajinaṃ daṇḍamupavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum  |
apsu prāsya vinaṣṭāni gṛhṇītānyāni mantravat || 27.29 ||



28

atha brahmacariṇāṃ gurukulavāsaḥ || 28.1 ||

saṃdhyādvayopāsanam || 28.2 ||

pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japettiṣṭhan paścimāmāsīnaḥ || 28.3 ||

kāladvayamabhiṣekāgnikarmakaraṇam || 28.4 ||

apsu daṇḍavanmajjanam || 28.5 ||

āhūtādhyayanam || 28.6 ||

guroḥ priyahitācaraṇam || 28.7 ||

mekhalādaṇḍājinopavītadhāraṇam || 28.8 ||

gurukulavarjaṃ guṇavatsu bhaikṣyacaraṇam || 28.9 ||

gurvanujñātaṃ bhaikṣyābhyavaharaṇam || 28.10 ||

śrāddhakṛtalavaṇaśuktaparyuṣitanṛtyagītastrīmadhumāṃsāñjanocchiṣṭaprāṇihiṃsāślīlaparivarjanam || 28.11 ||

adhaḥ śayyā || 28.12 ||

guroḥ pūrvotthānaṃ caramaṃ saṃveśanam || 28.13 ||

kṛtasaṃdhyopāsanaśca gurvabhivādanaṃ kuryāt || 28.14 ||

tasya ca vyatyastakaraḥ pādāvupaspṛśet || 28.15 ||

dakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇenetaramitareṇa || 28.16 ||

svaṃ ca nāmāsyābhivādanānte bhoḥśabdāntaṃ nivedayet || 28.17 ||

tiṣṭhannāsīnaḥ śayāno bhuñjānaḥ parāṅmukhaśca nāsyābhibhāṣaṇaṃ kuryāt || 28.18 ||

āsīnasya sthitaḥ kuryādabhigacchaṃstu gacchataḥ | āgacchataḥ pratyudgamya paścāddhāvaṃstu dhāvataḥ || 28.19 ||

parāṅmukhasyābhimukhaḥ || 28.20 ||

dūrasthasyāntikamupetya || 28.21 ||

śayānasya praṇamya || 28.22 ||

tasya ca cakṣurviṣaye na yatheṣṭāsanaḥ syāt || 28.23 ||

na cāsya kevalaṃ nāma brūyāt || 28.24 ||

gaticeṣṭābhāṣitādyaṃ nāsyānukuryāt || 28.25 ||

yatrāsya nindāparivādau syātāṃ na tatra tiṣṭhet || 28.26 ||

nāsyaikāsano bhavet || 28.27 ||

ṛte śilāphalakanauyānebhyaḥ || 28.28 ||

gurorgurau saṃnihite guruvadvarteta || 28.29 ||

anirdiṣṭaśca guruṇā svān gurunnābhivādayet || 28.30 ||

bāle samānavayasi vādhyāpake guruputre guruvadvarteta || 28.31 ||

nāsya pādau prakṣālayet || 28.32 ||

nocchiṣṭamaśnīyāt || 28.33 ||

evaṃ vedaṃ vedau vedān vā svīkuryāt || 28.34 ||

tato vedāṅgāni || 28.35 ||

yastvanadhītavedo'nyatra śramaṃ kuryādasau sasaṃtānaḥ śūdratvameti || 28.36 ||

māturagre vijananaṃ dvitīyaṃ mauñjibandhanam || 28.37 ||

tatrāsya mātā sāvitrī bhavati pitā tv*ācāryaḥ [ācārthaḥ] || 28.38 ||

etenaiva teṣāṃ dvijatvam || 28.39 ||

prāṅmauñjībandhanāddvijaḥ śūdrasamo bhavati || 28.40 ||

brahmacāriṇā muṇḍena jaṭilena vā bhāvyam || 28.41 ||

vedasvīkaraṇādūrdhvaṃ gurvanujñātastasmai varaṃ dattvā snāyāt || 28.42 ||

tato gurukula eva vā janmanaḥ śeṣaṃ nayet || 28.43 ||

tatrācārye prete guruvatguruputre varteta || 28.44 ||

gurudāreṣu savarṇeṣu vā || 28.45 ||

tadabhāve'gniśuśrūṣurnaiṣṭhiko brahmacārī syāt || 28.46 ||

evaṃ carati yo vipro brahmacaryamatandritaḥ  |
sa gacchatyuttamaṃ sthānaṃ na cehājāyate punaḥ  || 28.47 ||

kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ  |
atikramaṃ vratasyāhurdharmajñā brahmacāriṇaḥ  || 28.48 ||

etasminnenasi prāpte vasitvā gardabhājinam  |
saptāgāraṃ caredbhaikṣaṃ svakarma parikīrtayan  || 28.49 ||

tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣyeṇa vartayannekakālikam  |
upaspṛśaṃstriṣavaṇamabdena sa viśudhyati  || 28.50 ||

svapne siktvā brahmacārī dvijaḥ śukramakāmataḥ  |
snātvārkamarcayitvā triḥ punarmāmityṛcaṃ japet || 28.51 ||

akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇamasamiddhya ca pāvakam  |
anāturaḥ saptarātramavakīrṇivrataṃ caret || 28.52 ||

taṃ cedabhyudiyātsūryaḥ śayānaṃ kāmakārataḥ  |
nimlocedvāpy+avijñānājjapannupavaseddinam  || 28.53 ||



29

yastūpanīya vratādeśaṃ kṛtvā vedamadhyāpayettamācāryaṃ vidyāt || 29.1 ||

yastvenaṃ mūlyenādhyāpayettamupādhyāyamekadeśaṃ vā || 29.2 ||

yo yasya yajñnakarmāṇi kuryāttamṛtvijaṃ vidyāt || 29.3 ||

nāparīkṣitaṃ yojayet || 29.4 ||

nādhyāpayet || 29.5 ||

nopanayet || 29.6 ||

adharmeṇa ca yaḥ prāha yaścādharmeṇa pṛcchati  |
tayoranyataraḥ praiti vidveṣaṃ vādhigacchati  || 29.7 ||

dharmārthau yatra na syātāṃ śuśrūṣā vāpi tadvidhā  |
tatra vidyā na vaktavyā śubhaṃ bījamivoṣare  || 29.8 ||

vidyā ha vai brahmaṇamājagāma gopāya mā śevadhiṣṭe'hamasti  |
asūyakāyānṛjave'yatāya na māṃ brūyā avīryavatī tathā syām  || 29.9 ||

yameva vidyāḥ śucimapramattaṃ medhāvinaṃ brahmacaryopapannam  |
yaste na druhyetkatamacca nāha tasmai māṃ brūyā nidhipāya brahman  || 29.10 ||



30

śrāvaṇyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vā chandāṃsyupākṛtyārdhapañcamānmāsānadhīyīta || 30.1 ||

tatasteṣāmutsargaṃ bahiḥ kuryāt || 30.2 ||

utsarjanopākarmaṇormadhye vedāṅgādhyayanaṃ kuryāt || 30.3 ||

nādhīyītāhorātraṃ caturdaśyaṣṭamīṣu ca || 30.4 ||

na+ṛtvantaragrahasūtake || 30.5 ||

nendraprayāṇe || 30.6 ||

na vāti caṇḍapavane || 30.7 ||

nākālavarṣavidyutstaniteṣu || 30.8 ||

na bhūkaṃpolkāpātadigdāheṣu || 30.9 ||

nāntaḥśave grāme || 30.10 ||

na śāstrasaṃpāte || 30.11 ||

na śvasṛgālagardabhanirhrādeṣu || 30.12 ||

na vāditraśabde || 30.13 ||

na śūdrapatitayoḥ samīpe || 30.14 ||

na devatāyatanaśmaśānacatuṣpatharathyāsu || 30.15 ||

nodakāntaḥ || 30.16 ||

na pīṭhopahitapādaḥ || 30.17 ||

na hastyaśvoṣṭranaugoyāneṣu || 30.18 ||

na vāntaḥ || 30.19 ||

na viriktaḥ || 30.20 ||

nājīrṇī || 30.21 ||

na pañcanakhāntarāgamane || 30.22 ||

na rājaśrotriyagobrāhmaṇavyasane || 30.23 ||

nopākarmaṇi || 30.24 ||

notsarge || 30.25 ||

na sāmadhvanāvṛgyajuṣī || 30.26 ||

nāpararātramadhītya śayīta || 30.27 ||

abhiyukto'pyanadhyāyeṣvadhyayanaṃ pariharet || 30.28 ||

yasmādanadhyāyādhītaṃ nehāmutra phalapradam || 30.29 ||

tadadhyayanenāyuṣaḥ kṣayo guruśiṣyayośca || 30.30 ||

tasmādanadhyāyavarjaṃ guruṇā brahmalokakāmena vidyā satśiṣyakṣetreṣu vaptavyā || 30.31 ||

śiṣyeṇa brahmārambhāvasānayorguroḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kāryam || 30.32 ||

praṇavaśca vyāhartavyaḥ || 30.33 ||

tatra ca yadṛco'dhīte tenāsyājyena pitṝṇāṃ tṛptirbhavati || 30.34 ||

yadyajūṃṣi tena madhunā || 30.35 ||

yatsāmāni tena payasā || 30.36 ||

yadātharvaṇaṃ tena māṃsena || 30.37 ||

yatpurāṇetihāsavedāṅgadharmaśāstrāṇyadhīte tenāsyānnena || 30.38 ||

yaśca vidyāmāsādyāsmin loke tayā jīvet, na sā tasya paraloke phalapradā bhavet || 30.39 ||

yaśca vidyayā yaśaḥ pareṣāṃ hanti || 30.40 ||

ananujñātaścānyasmādadhīyānānna vidyāmādadyāt || 30.41 ||

tadādānamasya brahmasteyaṃ narakāya bhavati || 30.42 ||

laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ vāpi tathādhyātmikameva vā  |
ādadīta yato jñānaṃ na taṃ druhyetkadācana  || 30.43 ||

utpādakabrahmadātrorgarīyān brahmadaḥ pitā  |
brahmajanma hi viprasya pretya ceha ca śāśvatam  || 30.44 ||

kāmānmātā pitā cainaṃ yadutpādayato mithaḥ  |
saṃbhūtiṃ tasya tāṃ vidyādyadyonāviha jāyate  || 30.45 ||

ācāryastvasya yāṃ jātiṃ vidhivadvedapāragaḥ  |
utpādayati sāvitryā sā satyā sājarāmarā  || 30.46 ||

ya āvṛṇotyavitathena karṇāvaduḥkhaṃ kurvannamṛtaṃ saṃprayacchan  |
taṃ manyeta pitaraṃ mātaraṃ ca tasmai na druhyetkṛtamasya jānan  || 30.47 ||



31

trayaḥ puruṣasyātiguravo bhavanti || 31.1 ||

mātā pitā ācāryaśca || 31.2 ||

teṣāṃ nityameva śuśrūṣuṇā bhavitavyam || 31.3 ||

yatte brūyustatkuryāt || 31.4 ||

teṣāṃ priyahitamācaret || 31.5 ||

na tairananujñātaḥ kiṃcidapi kuryāt || 31.6 ||

eta eva trayo vedā eta eva trayaḥ surāḥ  |
eta eva trayo lokā eta eva trayo'gnayaḥ  || 31.7 ||

pitā gārhaptyo'gniḥ dakṣināgnirmātā gururāhavanīyaḥ || 31.8 ||

sarve tasyādṛtā dharmā yasyaite traya ādṛtāḥ  |
anādṛtyāstu yasyaite sarvāstasyāphalāḥ kriyāḥ  || 31.9 ||

imaṃ lokaṃ mātṛbhaktyā pitṛbhaktyā tu madhyamam  |
guruśuśrūṣayā tveva brahmalokaṃ samāśnute  || 31.10 ||



32

rājartvikśrotriyādharmapratiṣedhyupādhyāyapitṛvyamātāmahamātulaśvaśurajyeṣṭhabhrātṛsaṃbandhinaścācāryavat || 32.1 ||

patnya eteṣāṃ savarṇāḥ || 32.2 ||

mātṛṣvasā pitṛṣvasā jyeṣṭhā svasā ca || 32.3 ||

śvaśurapitṛvyamātulartvijāṃ kanīyasāṃ pratyutthānamevābhivādanam || 32.4 ||

hīnavarṇānāṃ gurupatnīnāṃ dūrādabhivādanaṃ na pādopasparśanam || 32.5 ||

gurupatnīnāṃ gotrotsādanāñjanakeśasaṃyamanapādaprakṣālanādīni na kuryāt || 32.6 ||

asaṃstutāpi parapatnī bhaginīti vācyā putrīti māteti vā || 32.7 ||

na ca gurūṇāṃ tvamiti brūyāt || 32.8 ||

tadatikrame nirāhāro divasānte taṃ prasādyāśnīyāt || 32.9 ||

na ca guruṇā saha vigṛhya kathāḥ kuryāt || 32.10 ||

na caivāsya parīvādam || 32.11 ||

na cānabhipretam || 32.12 ||

gurupatnī tu yuvatirnābhivādyeha pādayoḥ  |
pūrṇaviṃśativarṣeṇa guṇadoṣau vijānatā  || 32.13 ||

kāmaṃ tu gurupatnīnāṃ yuvatīnāṃ yuvā bhuvi  |
vidhivadvandanaṃ kuryādasāvahamiti bruvan  || 32.14 ||

viproṣya pādagrahaṇamanvahaṃ cābhivādanam  |
gurudāreṣu kurvīta satāṃ dharmamanusmaran  || 32.15 ||

vittaṃ bandhurvayaḥ karma vidyā bhavati pañcamī  |
etāni mānasthānāni garīyo yadyaduttaram  || 32.16 ||

brāhmaṇaṃ daśavarṣaṃ ca śatavarṣaṃ ca bhūmipam  |
pitāputrau vijānīyādbrāhmaṇastu tayoḥ pitā  || 32.17 ||

viprāṇāṃ jñānato jyaiṣṭhyaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu vīryataḥ  |
vaiśyānāṃ dhānyadhanataḥ śūdrāṇāmeva janmataḥ  || 32.18 ||

[Endof ড়rt1]




33

atha puruṣasya kāmakrodhalobhākhyaṃ riputrayaṃ sughoraṃ bhavati || 33.1 ||

parigrahaprasaṅgādviśeṣeṇa gṛhāśramiṇaḥ || 33.2 ||

tenāyamākrānto'tipātakamahāpātakānupātakopapātakeṣu pravartate || 33.3 ||

jātibhraṃśakareṣu saṃkarīkaraṇeṣV apātrīkaraṇeṣu || 33.4 ||

malāvaheṣu prakīrṇakeṣu ca || 33.5 ||

trividhaṃ narakasyedaṃ dvāraṃ nāśanamātmanaḥ  |
kāmaḥ krodhastathā lobhastasmādetattrayaṃ jyayet || 33.6 ||



34
mātṛgamanaṃ duhitṛgamanaṃ snuṣāgamanamityatipātakāni || 34.1 ||

atipātakinastvete praviśeyurhutāśanam  |
na hyanyā niṣkṛtisteṣāṃ vidyate hi kathaṃcana  || 34.2 ||



35

brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ brāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇaṃ gurudāragamanamiti mahāpātakāni || 35.1 ||

tatsaṃyogaśca || 35.2 ||

saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran || 35.3 ||

ekayānabhojanāśanaśayanaiḥ || 35.4 ||

yaunasrauvamukhyaiḥ saṃbandhaistu sadya eva || 35.5 ||

aśvamedhena śuddhyeyurmahāpātakinastvime  |
pṛthivyāṃ sarvatīrthānāṃ tathānusaraṇena ca  || 35.6 ||



36

yāgasthasya kṣatriyasya vaiśyasya ca rajasvalāyāścāntarvatnyāścātrigotrāyāścāvijñātasya garbhasya śaraṇāgatasya ca ghātanaṃ brahmahatyāsamānīti || 36.1 ||

kauṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadha ityetau surāpānasamau || 36.2 ||

brāhmaṇasya bhūmyapaharaṇaṃ nikṣepāpaharaṇaṃ suvarṇasteyasamam || 36.3 ||

pitṛvyamātāmahamātulaśvaśuranṛpapatnyabhigamanaṃ gurudāragamanasamam || 36.4 ||

pitṛṣvasṛmātṛṣvasṛsvasṛgamanaṃ ca || 36.5 ||

śrotriyartvigupādhyāyamitrapatnyabhigamanaṃ ca || 36.6 ||

svasuḥ sakhyāḥ sagotrāya uttamavarṇāyāḥ kumāryā antyajāyā rajasvalāyāḥ śaraṇāgatāyāḥ *pravrajitāyā nikṣiptāyāśca [pravrajitāyāḥ ni] || 36.7 ||

anupātakinastvete mahāpātakino yathā  |
aśvamedhena śudhyanti tīrthānusaraṇena vā  || 36.8 ||



37

anṛtavacanamutkarṣe || 37.1 ||

rājagāmi paiśunyam || 37.2 ||

guroścālīkanirbandhaḥ || 37.3 ||

vedanindā || 37.4 ||

adhītasya ca tyāgaḥ || 37.5 ||

agnipitṛmātṛsutadārāṇāṃ ca || 37.6 ||

abhojyānnābhakṣyabhakṣaṇam || 37.7 ||

parasvāpaharaṇam || 37.8 ||

paradārābhigamanam || 37.9 ||

ayājyayājanam || 37.10 ||

vikarmajīvanam || 37.11 ||

asatpratigrahaśca || 37.12 ||

kṣatraviṭśūdragovadhaḥ || 37.13 ||

avikreyavikrayaḥ || 37.14 ||

parivittitānujena jyeṣṭhasya || 37.15 ||

parivedanam || 37.16 ||

tasya ca kanyādānam || 37.17 ||

yājanaṃ ca || 37.18 ||

vrātyatā || 37.19 ||

bhṛtakādhyāpanam || 37.20 ||

bhṛtakāccādhyayanādānam || 37.21 ||

sarvākareṣvadhīkāraḥ || 37.22 ||

mahāyantrapravartanam || 37.23 ||

drumagulmavallīlatauṣadhīnāṃ hiṃsā || 37.24 ||

stryā jīvanam || 37.25 ||

abhicārabalakarmasu ca pravṛttiḥ || 37.26 ||

ātmārthe kriyārambhaḥ || 37.27 ||

anāhitāgnitā || 37.28 ||

devarṣipitṛṛṇānāmanapakriyā || 37.29 ||

asatśāstrābhigamanam || 37.30 ||

nāstikatā || 37.31 ||

kuśīlavatā || 37.32 ||

madyapastrīniṣevaṇam || 37.33 ||

ityupapātakāni || 37.34 ||

upapātakinastvete kuryuścāndrāyaṇaṃ narāḥ  |
parākaṃ ca tathā kuryuryajeyurgosavena vā  || 37.36 ||



38

brāhmaṇasya rujaḥ karaṇam || 38.1 ||

aghreyamadyayorghrātiḥ || 38.2 ||

jaihmyam || 38.3 ||

paśuṣu maithunācaraṇam || 38.4 ||

puṃsi ca || 38.5 ||

iti jātibhraṃśakarāṇi || 38.6 ||

jātibhraṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatamamicchayā  |
caretsāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyamanicchayā  || 38.7 ||



39

grāmyāraṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ hiṃsā saṃkarīkaraṇam || 39.1 ||

saṃkarīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā māsamaśnīta yāvakam  |
kṛcchrātikṛcchramatha vā prāyaścittaṃ tu kārayet || 39.2 ||



40

ninditebhyo ghanādānaṃ vāṇijyaṃ kusīdajīvanamasatyabhāṣaṇaṃ śūdrasevanamityapātrīkaraṇam || 40.1 ||

apātrīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyati  |
śītakṛcchreṇa vā bhūyo mahāsāṃtapanena vā  || 40.2 ||



41

pakṣiṇāṃ jalacarāṇāṃ jalajānāṃ ca ghātanam || 41.1 ||

krimikīṭānāṃ ca || 41.2 ||

madhyānugatabhojanam || 41.3 ||

iti malāvahāni || 41.4 ||

malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam  |
kṛcchrātikṛcchramatha vā prāyaścittaṃ viśodhanam  || 41.5 ||



42

yadanuktaṃ tatprakīrṇakam || 42.1 ||

prakīrṇapātake jñātvā gurutvamatha lāghavam  |
prāyaścittaṃ budhaḥ kuryādbrāhmaṇānumato yathā  || 42.2 ||



43

atha narakāḥ || 43.1 ||

tāmisram || 43.2 ||

andhatāmisram || 43.3 ||

rauravam || 43.4 ||

mahārauravam || 43.5 ||

kālasūtram || 43.6 ||

mahānarakam || 43.7 ||

saṃjīvanam || 43.8 ||

avīci || 43.9 ||

tapanam || 43.10 ||

saṃpratāpanam || 43.11 ||

saṃghātakam || 43.12 ||

kākolam || 43.13 ||

kuḍmalam || 43.14 ||

pūtimṛttikam || 43.15 ||

lohaśaṅkuḥ || 43.16 ||

ṛbīsam || 43.17 ||

viṣamapanthāḥ || 43.18 ||

kaṇṭakaśālmaliḥ || 43.19 ||

dīpanadī || 43.20 ||

asipatravanam || 43.21 ||

lohacārakamiti || 43.22 ||

eteṣvakṛtaprāyaścittā atipātakinaḥ paryāyeṇa kalpaṃ pacyante || 43.23 ||

mahāpātakino manvantaram || 43.24 ||

anupātakinaśca || 43.25 ||
upapātakinaścaturyugam || 43.26 ||

kṛtasaṃkarīkaraṇāśca saṃvatsarasahasram || 43.27 ||

kṛtajātibhraṃśakaraṇāśca || 43.28 ||

kṛtāpātrīkaraṇāśca || 43.29 ||

kṛtamalinīkaraṇāśca || 43.30 ||

prakīrṇapātakinaśca bahūn varṣapūgān || 43.31 ||

kṛtapātakinaḥ pāpāḥ prāṇatyāgādanantaram  |
yāmyaṃ panthānamāsādya duḥkhamaśnanti dāruṇam  || 43.32 ||

yamasya puruṣairghoraiḥ kṛṣyamāṇā yatastataḥ  |
sakṛcchreṇānukāreṇa nīyamānāśca te yathā  || 43.33 ||

śvabhiḥ śṛgālaiḥ kravyādaiḥ kākakaṅkabakādibhiḥ  |
agnituṇḍairbhakṣyamāṇā bhujaṅgairvṛścikaistathā  || 43.34 ||

agninā dahyamānāśca tudyamānāśca kaṇṭakaiḥ  |
krakacaiḥ pāṭyamānāśca pīḍyamānāśca tṛṣṇayā  || 43.35 ||

kṣudhayā vyathamānāśca ghorairvyāghragaṇaistathā  |
pūyaśoṇitagandhena mūrchamānāḥ pade pade  || 43.36 ||

parānnapānaṃ lipsantastādyamānāśca kiṃkaraiḥ  |
kākakaṅkabakādīnāṃ bhīmānāṃ sadṛśānanaiḥ  || 43.37 ||

kvacittailena kvāthyante tāḍyante musalaiḥ kvacit |
āyasīṣu ca vaṭyante śilāsu ca tathā kvacit || 43.38 ||

kvacidvāntamathāśnanti kvacitpūyamasṛkkvacit |
kvacidviṣṭhāṃ kvacinmāṃsaṃ pūyagandhi sudāruṇam  || 43.39 ||

andhakāreṣu tiṣṭhanti dāruṇeṣu tathā kvacit |
krimibhirbhakṣyamāṇāśca vahnituṇḍaiḥ sudāruṇaiḥ  || 43.40 ||

kvacicchītena bādhyante kvacitcāmedhyamadhyagāḥ  |
parasparamathāśnanti kvacitpretāḥ sudāruṇāḥ  || 43.41 ||

kvacidbhūtena tāḍyante lambamānāstathā kvacit |
kvacitkṣipyanti bānaughairutkṛtyante tathā kvacit || 43.42 ||

kaṇṭeṣu dattapādāśca bhujaṅgābhogaveṣṭitāḥ  |
pīḍyamānāstathā yantraiḥ kṛṣyamāṇāśca jānubhiḥ  || 43.43 ||

bhagnapṛṣṭhaśirogrīvāḥ sūcīkaṇṭhāḥ sudāruṇāḥ  |
kūṭāgārapramāṇaiśca śarīrairyātanākṣamaiḥ  || 43.44 ||

evaṃ pātakinaḥ pāpamanubhūya suduḥkhitāḥ  |
tiryagyonau prapadyante duḥkhāni vividhāni ca  || 43.45 ||



44

atha pāpātmanāṃ narakeṣvanubhūtaduḥkhānāṃ tiryagyonayo bhavanti || 44.1 ||

atipātakināṃ paryāyeṇa sarvāḥ sthāvarayonayaḥ || 44.2 ||

mahāpātakināṃ ca krimiyonayaḥ || 44.3 ||

anupātakināṃ pakṣiyonayaḥ || 44.4 ||

upapātakināṃ jalajayonayaḥ || 44.5 ||

kṛtajātibhraṃśakarāṇāṃ jalacarayonayaḥ || 44.6 ||

kṛtasaṃkarīkaraṇakarmaṇāṃ mṛgayonayaḥ || 44.7 ||

kṛtāpātrīkaraṇakarmaṇāṃ paśuyonayaḥ || 44.8 ||

kṛtamalinīkaraṇakarmaṇāṃ manuṣyeṣvaspṛśyayonayaḥ || 44.9 ||

prakīrṇakeṣu prakīrṇā hiṃsrāḥ kravyādā bhavanti || 44.10 ||

abhojyānnābhakṣyāśī krimiḥ || 44.11 ||

stenaḥ śyenaḥ || 44.12 ||

prakṛṣṭavartmāpahārī bileśayaḥ || 44.13 ||

ākhurdhānyahārī || 44.14 ||

haṃsaḥ kāṃsyāpahārī || 44.15 ||
