jalahṛjjalābhiplavaḥ || 44.16 ||

madhu daṃśaḥ || 44.17 ||

payaḥ kākaḥ || 44.18 ||

rasaṃ śvā || 44.19 ||

ghṛtaṃ nakulaḥ || 44.20 ||

māṃsaṃ gṛdhraḥ || 44.21 ||

vasāṃ madguḥ || 44.22 ||

tailaṃ tailapāyikaḥ || 44.23 ||

lavaṇaṃ cīvivāk || 44.24 ||

dadhi balākā || 44.25 ||

kauśeyaṃ hṛtvā bhavati tittiriḥ || 44.26 ||

kṣaumaṃ darduraḥ || 44.27 ||

kārpāsatāntavaṃ krauñcaḥ || 44.28 ||

godhā gām || 44.29 ||

vālgudo guḍam || 44.30 ||

chuchundarirgandhān || 44.31 ||

patraśākaṃ barhī || 44.32 ||

kṛtānnaṃ sedhā || 44.33 ||

akṛtānnaṃ śalyakaḥ || 44.34 ||

agniṃ bakaḥ || 44.35 ||

gṛhakāryupaskaram || 44.36 ||

raktavāsāṃsi jīvajīvakaḥ || 44.37 ||

gajaṃ kūrmaḥ || 44.38 ||

aśvaṃ vyāghraḥ || 44.39 ||

phalaṃ puṣpaṃ vā markaṭaḥ || 44.40 ||

ṛkṣaḥ striyam || 44.41 ||

yānamuṣṭraḥ || 44.42 ||

paśūn gṛdhraḥ || 44.43 ||

yadvā tadvā paradravyamapahṛtya balānnaraḥ  |
avaśyaṃ yāti tiryaktvaṃ jagdhvā caivāhutaṃ haviḥ  || 44.44 ||

striyo'pyetena kalpena hṛtvā doṣamavāpnuyuḥ  |
eteṣāmeva jantūnāṃ bhāryātvamupayānti tāḥ  || 44.45 ||



45

narakābhibhūtaduḥkhānāṃ tiryaktvamuttīrṇānāṃ mānuṣyeṣu lakṣaṇāni bhavanti || 45.1 ||

kuṣṭhyatipātakī || 45.2 ||

brahmahā yakṣmī || 45.3 ||

surāpaḥ śyāvadantakaḥ || 45.4 ||

suvarṇahārī kunakhī || 45.5 ||

gurutarlpago duścarmā || 45.6 ||

pūtināsaḥ piśunaḥ || 45.7 ||

pūtivaktraḥ sūcakaḥ || 45.8 ||

dhānyacoro'ṅgahīnaḥ || 45.9 ||

miśracoro'tiriktāṅgaḥ || 45.10 ||

annāpahārakastvāmayāvī || 45.11 ||

vāgapahārako mūkaḥ || 45.12 ||

vastrāpahārakaḥ śvitrī || 45.13 ||

aśvāpahārakaḥ paṅguḥ || 45.14 ||

devabrāhmaṇākrośako mūkaḥ || 45.15 ||

lolajihvo garadaḥ || 45.16 ||

unmatto'gnidaḥ || 45.17 ||

guroḥ pratikūlo'pasmārī || 45.18 ||

goghnastvandhaḥ || 45.19 ||

dīpāpahārakaśca || 45.20 ||

kāṇaśca dīpanirvāpakaḥ || 45.21 ||

trapucāmarasīsakavikrayī rajakaḥ || 45.22 ||

ekaśaphavikrayī mṛgavyādhaḥ || 45.23 ||

kuṇḍāśī bhagāsyaḥ || 45.24 ||

ghāṇṭikaḥ stenaḥ || 45.25 ||

vārdhuṣiko bhrāmarī || 45.26 ||

mṛṣṭāśyekākī vātagulmī || 45.27 ||

samayabhettā khalvāṭaḥ || 45.28 ||

ślīpadyavakīrṇī || 45.29 ||

paravṛttighno daridraḥ || 45.30 ||

parapīḍākaro dīrgharogī || 45.31 ||

evaṃ karmaviśeṣeṇa jāyante lakṣaṇānvitāḥ  |
rogānvitāstathāndhāśca kubjakhañjaikalocanāḥ  || 45.32 ||

vāmanā badhirā mūkā durbalāśca tathāpare  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena prāyaścittaṃ samācaret || 45.33 ||



46

atha kṛcchrāṇi bhavanti || 46.1 ||

tryahaṃ nāśnīyāt || 46.2 ||

pratyahaṃ ca triṣavaṇaṃ snānamācaret || 46.3 ||

triḥ pratisnānamapsu majjanam || 46.4 ||

magnastriraghamarṣaṇaṃ japet || 46.5 ||

divā sthitastiṣṭhet || 46.6 ||

rātrāvāsīnaḥ || 46.7 ||

karmaṇo'nte payasvinīṃ dadyāt || 46.8 ||

ityaghmarṣaṇam || 46.9 ||

tryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryahaṃ prātastryahamayācitamaśnīyāt | eṣa prājāpatyaḥ || 46.10 ||

tryahamuṣṇāḥ pibedapastryahamuṣṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ tryahamuṣṇaṃ payastryahaṃ ca nāśnīyādeṣa taptakṛcchraḥ || 46.11 ||

etaireva śītaiḥ śītakṛcchraḥ || 46.12 ||

kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ payasā divasaikaviṃśatikṣapaṇam || 46.13 ||

udakasaktūnāṃ māsābhyavahāreṇodakakṛcchraḥ || 46.14 ||

bisābhyavahāreṇa mūlakṛcchraḥ || 46.15 ||

bilvābhyavahāreṇa śrīphalakṛcchraḥ || 46.16 ||

padmākṣairvā || 46.17 ||

nirāhārasya dvādaśāhena parākaḥ || 46.18 ||

gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakānyekadivasamaśnīyāt | dvitīyamupavaset | etatsāṃtapanam || 46.19 ||

gomūtrādibhiḥ pratyahamabhyastairmahāsāṃtapanam || 46.20 ||

tryahābhyastaiścātisāṃtapanam || 46.21 ||

piṇyākācāmatakrodakasaktūnāmupavāsāntarito'bhyavahārastulāpuruṣaḥ || 46.22 ||

kuśapalāśodumbarapadmaśaṅkhapuṣpīvaṭabrāhmīsuvarcalāpatraiḥ kvathitasyāmbhasaḥ pratyekaṃ pānena parṇakṛcchraḥ || 46.23 ||

kṛcchrāṇyetāni sarvāṇi kurvīta kṛtavāpanaḥ  |
nityaṃ triṣavaṇasnāyī adhaḥśāyī jitendriyaḥ  || 46.24 ||

strīśūdrapatitānāṃ ca varjayeccātibhāṣaṇam  |
pavitrāṇi japennityaṃ juhuyāccaiva śaktitaḥ  || 46.25 ||



47

atha cāndrāyaṇam || 47.1 ||

grāsānavikārānaśnīyāt || 47.2 ||

tāṃścandrakalābhivṛddhau vardhayet, hānau hrāsayet, amāvāsyāyāṃ nāśnīyāt | eṣa cāndrāyaṇo yavamadhyaḥ || 47.3 ||

pipīlikāmadhyo vā || 47.4 ||

yasyāmāvāsyā madhye bhavati sa pipīlikāmadhyaḥ || 47.5 ||

yasya paurṇamāsī sa yavamadhyaḥ || 47.6 ||

aṣṭau grāsān pratidivasaṃ māsamaśnīyātsa yaticāndrāyaṇaḥ || 47.7 ||

sāyaṃ prātaścaturaścaturaḥ sa śiśucāndrāyaṇaḥ || 47.8 ||

yathā kathaṃcitṣaṣṭyonāṃ triśatīṃ māsenāśnīyātsa sāmānyacāndrāyaṇaḥ || 47.9 ||

vratametatpurā bhūmi kṛtvā saptarṣayo'malāḥ  |
prāptavantaḥ paraṃ sthānaṃ brahmā rudrastathaiva ca  || 47.10 ||



48

atha karmabhirātmakṛtairgurumātmānaṃ manyetātmārthe prasṛtiyāvakaṃ śrapayet || 48.1 ||

na tato'gnau juhuyāt || 48.2 ||

na cātra balikarma || 48.3 ||

aśṛtaṃ śrapyamāṇaṃ śṛtaṃ cābhimantrayet || 48.4 ||

śrapyamāṇe rakṣāṃ kuryāt || 48.5 ||

brahmā devānāṃ padavīḥ kavīnāmṛṣirviprāṇāṃ mahiṣo mṛgāṇām  |
śyeno gṛdhrāṇāṃ svadhitirvanānāṃ somaḥ pavitramatyeti rebhan  || 48.6 ||

iti darbhān badhnāti || 48.6 ||

śṛtaṃ ca tamaśnīyātpātre niṣicya || 48.7 ||

ye devā manojātā monojuṣaḥ sudakṣā dakṣapitaraste naḥ pāntu te no'vantu tebhyo namastebhyaḥ svāhetyātmani juhuyāt || 48.8 ||

athācānto nābhimālabheta || 48.9 ||

snātāḥ pītā bhavata yūyamāpo'smākamudare yavāḥ, tā asmabhyamanamīvā ayakṣmā anāgasaḥ santu devīramṛtāmṛtāvṛdha iti || 48.10 ||

trirātraṃ medhārthī || 48.11 ||

ṣaḍrātraṃ pāpakṛt || 48.12 ||

saptarātraṃ pītvā mahāpātakināmanyatamaṃ punāti || 48.13 ||

dvādaśarātreṇa pūrvapuruṣakṛtamapi pāpaṃ nirdahati || 48.14 ||

māsaṃ pītvā sarvapāpāni || 48.15 ||

gonihāramuktānāṃ yavānāmekaviṃśatirātraṃ ca || 48.16 ||

yavo'si dhānyarājo'si vāruṇo madhusaṃyutaḥ  |
nirṇodaḥ sarvapāpānāṃ pavitramṛṣibhirdhṛtam  || 48.17 ||

ghṛtaṃ yavā madhu yavā āpo vā amṛtaṃ yavāḥ  |
sarve punīta me pāpaṃ yanme kiṃcana duṣkṛtam  || 48.18 ||

vācā kṛtaṃ karmakṛtaṃ manasā durvicintitam  |
alakṣmīṃ kālakarṇīṃ ca nāśayadhvaṃ yavā mama  || 48.19 ||

śvasūkarāvalīḍhaṃ ca ucchiṣṭopahataṃ ca yat |
mātāpitroraśuśrūṣāṃ tatpunīdhvaṃ yavā mama  || 48.20 ||

gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca śūdrānnaṃ śrāddhasūtakam  |
caurasyānnaṃ navaśrāddhaṃ punīdhvaṃ ca yavā mama  || 48.21 ||

bāladhūrtamadharmaṃ ca rājadvārakṛtaṃ ca yat |
suvarṇastainyamavrātyamayājyasya ca yājanam  |
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādaṃ punīdhvaṃ ca yavā mama  || 48.22 ||



49

mārgaśīrṣaśuklaikādaśyāmupoṣito dvādaśyāṃ bhagavantaṃ śrīvāsudevamarcayet || 49.1 ||

puṣpadhūpānulepanadīpanaivedyaiḥ vahnibrāhmaṇatarpaṇaiśca || 49.2 ||

vratametatsaṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā pāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati || 49.3 ||

yāvajjīvaṃ kṛtvā śvetadvīpamāpnoti || 49.4 ||

ubhayapakṣadvādaśīṣvevaṃ saṃvatsareṇa svargalokamāpnoti || 49.5 ||

yāvajjīvaṃ kṛtvā viṣṇulokam || 49.6 ||

evameva pañcadaśīṣvapi || 49.7 ||

brahmabhūtamamāvāsyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ tathaiva ca  |
yogabhūtaṃ paricaran keśavaṃ mahadāpnuyāt || 49.8 ||

dṛśyete sahitau yasyāṃ divi candrabṛhaspatī  |
paurṇamāsī tu mahatī proktā saṃvatsare tu sā  || 49.9 ||

tasyāṃ dānopavāsādyamakṣayaṃ parikīrtitam  |
tathaiva dvādaśī śuklā yā syācchravaṇasaṃyutā  || 49.10 ||



50

vane parṇakuṭīṃ kṛtvā vaset || 50.1 ||

triṣavaṇaṃ snāyāt || 50.2 ||

svakarma cācakṣāṇo grāme grāme bhaikṣyamācaret || 50.3 ||

tṛṇaśāyī ca syāt || 50.4 ||

etanmahāvratam || 50.5 ||

brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā dvādaśasaṃvatsaraṃ kuryāt || 50.6 ||

yāgasthaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ vā || 50.7 ||

gurviṇīṃ rajasvalāṃ vā || 50.8 ||

atrigotrāṃ vā nārīm || 50.9 ||

mitraṃ vā || 50.10 ||

nṛpativadhe mahāvratameva dviguṇaṃ kuryāt || 50.11 ||

pādonaṃ kṣatriyavadhe || 50.12 ||

ardhaṃ vaiśyavadhe || 50.13 ||

tadardhaṃ śūdravadhe || 50.14 ||

sarveṣu śavaśirodhvajī syāt || 50.15 ||

māsamekaṃ kṛtavāpano gavāmanugamanaṃ kuryāt || 50.16 ||

tāsvāsīnāsvāsīta || 50.17 ||

sthitāsu sthitaśca syāt || 50.18 ||

sannāṃ coddharet || 50.19 ||

bhayebhyaśca rakṣet || 50.20 ||

tāsāṃ śītāditrāṇamakṛtvā nātmanaḥ kuryāt || 50.21 ||

gomūtreṇa snāyāt || 50.22 ||

gorasaiśca varteta || 50.23 ||

etadgovrataṃ govadhe kuryāt || 50.24 ||

gajaṃ hatvā pañca nīlavṛṣabhān dadyāt || 50.25 ||

turagaṃ vāsaḥ || 50.26 ||

ekahāyanamanaḍvāhaṃ kharavadhe || 50.27 ||

meṣājavadhe ca || 50.28 ||

suvarṇakṛṣṇalamuṣṭravadhe || 50.29 ||

śvānaṃ hatvā trirātramupavaset || 50.30 ||

hatvā mūṣakamārjāranakulamaṇḍūkaḍuṇḍubhājagarāṇāmanyatamamupoṣitaḥ kṛsaraṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayitvā lohadaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt || 50.31 ||

godholūkakākajhaṣavadhe trirātramupavaset || 50.32 ||

haṃsabakabalākāmadguvānaraśyenabhāsacakravākānāmanyatamaṃ hatvā brāhmaṇāya gāṃ dadyāt || 50.33 ||

sarpaṃ hatvābhrīṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyāt || 50.34 ||

ṣaṇḍhaṃ hatvā palālabhārakam || 50.35 ||

varāhaṃ hatvā ghṛtakumbham || 50.36 ||

tittiriṃ tiladroṇam || 50.37 ||

śukaṃ dvihāyanavatsam || 50.38 ||

krauñcaṃ trihāyanam || 50.39 ||

kravyādamṛgavadhe payasvinīṃ gāṃ dadyāt || 50.40 ||

akravyādamṛgavadhe vatsatarīm || 50.41 ||

anuktamṛgavadhe trirātraṃ payasā varteta || 50.42 ||

pakṣivadhe naktāśī syāt || 50.43 ||

rūpyamāṣaṃ vā dadyāt || 50.44 ||

hatvā jalacaramupavaset || 50.45 ||

asthanvatāṃ tu sattvānāṃ sahasrasya pramāpaṇe  |
pūrṇe cānasyanasthnāṃ tu śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || 50.46 ||

kiṃcideva tu viprāya dadyādasthimatāṃ vadhe  |
anashthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati  || 50.47 ||

phaladānāṃ tu vṛkṣāṇāṃ chedane japyamṛkśatam  |
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca puṣpitānāṃ ca vīrudhām  || 50.48 ||

annādyajānāṃ sattvānāṃ rasajānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ  |
phalapuṣpodbhavānāṃ ca ghṛtaprāśo viśodhanam  || 50.49 ||

kṛṣṭajānāmoṣadhīnāṃ jātānāṃ ca svayaṃ vane  |
vṛthālambhe'nugacchedgāṃ dinamekaṃ payovrataḥ  || 50.50 ||



51

surāpaḥ sarvakarmavarjitaḥ kaṇān varṣamaśnīyāt || 51.1 ||

malānāṃ madyānāṃ cānyatamasya prāśane cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt || 51.2 ||

laśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanaitadgandhiviḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭavānaragomāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca || 51.3 ||

sarveṣveteṣu dvijānāṃ prāyaścittānte bhūyaḥ saṃskāraṃ kuryāt || 51.4 ||

vapanamekhalādaṇḍabhaikṣyacaryāvratāni punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi varjanīyāni || 51.5 ||

śaśakaśalyakagodhākhaḍgakūrmavarjaṃ pañcanakhamāṃsāśane saptarātramupavaset || 51.6 ||

gaṇagaṇikāstenagāyanānnāni bhuktvā saptarātraṃ payasā varteta || 51.7 ||

takṣakānnaṃ carmakartuśca || 51.8 ||

vārdhuṣikakadaryadīkṣitabaddhanigaḍābhiśastaṣaṇḍhānāṃ ca || 51.9 ||

puṃścalīdāmbhikacikitsakalubdhakakrūrogrocchiṣṭabhojināṃ ca || 51.10 ||

avīrastrī suvarṇakārasapatnapatitānāṃ ca || 51.11 ||

piśunānṛtavādikṣatadharmātmarasavikrayiṇāṃ ca || 51.12 ||

śailūṣatantuvāyakṛtaghnarajakānāṃ ca || 51.13 ||

karmakāraniṣādaraṅgāvatārivaiṇaśastravikrayiṇāṃ ca || 51.14 ||

śvajīviśauṇḍikatailikacailanirṇejakānāṃ ca || 51.15 ||

rajasvalāsahopapativeśmānāṃ ca || 51.16 ||

bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitamudakyāsaṃspṛṣṭaṃ patatriṇāvalīḍhaṃ śunā saṃspṛṣṭaṃ gavāghrātaṃ ca || 51.17 ||

kāmataḥ padā spṛṣṭamavakṣutam || 51.18 ||

mattakruddhāturāṇāṃ ca || 51.19 ||

anarcitaṃ vṛthā māṃsaṃ ca || 51.20 ||

pāṭhīnarohitarājīvasiṃhatuṇḍaśakulavarjaṃ sarvamatsyamāṃsāśane trirātramupavaset || 51.21 ||

sarvajalajamāṃsāśane ca || 51.22 ||

āpaḥ surābhāṇḍasthāḥ pītvā saptarātraṃ śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ pibet || 51.23 ||

madyabhāṇḍasthāśca pañcarātram || 51.24 ||

somapaḥ surāpasyāghrāya gandhamudakamagnastriraghamarṣaṇaṃ japtvā ghṛtaprāśanamācaret || 51.25 ||

kharoṣṭrakākamāṃsāśane cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt || 51.26 ||

prāśyājñātaṃ sūnāsthaṃ śuṣkamāṃsaṃ ca || 51.27 ||

kravyādamṛgapakṣimāṃsāśane taptakṛcchram || 51.28 ||

kalaviṅkaplavacakravākahaṃsarajjudālasārasadātyūhaśukasārikābakabalākākokilakhañjarīṭāśane trirātramupavaset || 51.29 ||

ekaśaphobhayadantāśane ca || 51.30 ||

tittirikapiñjalalāvakavartikāmayūravarjaṃ sarvapakṣimāṃsāśane cāhorātram || 51.31 ||

kīṭāśane dinamekaṃ brahmasuvarcalāṃ pibet || 51.32 ||

śunāṃ māṃsāśane ca || 51.33 ||

chatrākakavakāśane sāṃtapanam || 51.34 ||

yavagodhūmapayovikāraṃ snehāktaṃ śuktaṃ khāṇḍavaṃ ca varjayitvā yatparyuṣitaṃ tatprāśyopavaset || 51.35 ||

vraścanāmedhyaprabhavān lohitāṃśca vṛkṣaniryāsān || 51.36 ||

śālūkavṛthākṛsarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīdevānnāni havīṃṣi ca || 51.37 ||

goajāmahiṣīvarjaṃ sarvapayāṃsi ca || 51.38 ||

anirdaśāhāni tānyapi || 51.39 ||

syandinīsandhinīvivatsākṣīraṃ ca || 51.40 ||

amedhyabhujaśca || 51.41 ||

dadhivarjaṃ kevalāni ca śuktāni || 51.42 ||

brahmacaryāśramī śrāddhabhojane trirātramupavaset || 51.43 ||

dinamekaṃ codake vaset || 51.44 ||

madhumāṃsāśane prājāpatyam || 51.45 ||

biḍālakākanakulākhūcchiṣṭabhakṣaṇe brahmasuvarcalāṃ pibet || 51.46 ||

śvocchiṣṭāśane dinamekamupoṣitaḥ pañcagavyaṃ pibet || 51.47 ||

pañcanakhaviṇmūtrāśane saptarātram || 51.48 ||

āmaśrāddhāśane trirātraṃ payasā varteta || 51.49 ||

brāhmaṇaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāśane saptarātram || 51.50 ||

vaiśyocchiṣṭāśane pañcarātram || 51.51 ||

rājanyocchiṣṭāśane trirātram || 51.52 ||

brāhmaṇocchiṣṭāśane tvekāham || 51.53 ||

rājanyaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāśī pañcarātram || 51.54 ||

vaiśyocchiṣṭāśī trirātram || 51.55 ||

vaiśyaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭāśī ca || 51.56 ||

caṇḍālānnaṃ bhuktvā trirātramupavaset || 51.57 ||

siddhaṃ bhuktvā parākaḥ || 51.58 ||

asaṃskṛtān paśūnmantrairnādyādvipraḥ kathaṃcana  |
mantraistu saṃskṛtānadyācchāśvataṃ vidhimāsthitaḥ  || 51.59 ||

yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvatkṛtveha māraṇam  |
vṛthā paśughnaḥ prāpnoti pretya ceha ca niṣkṛtim  || 51.60 ||

yajñārthaṃ paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayameva svayaṃbhuvā  |
yajño hi bhūtyai sarvasya tasmādyajñe vadho'vadhaḥ  || 51.61 ||

na tādṛśaṃ bhavatyeno mṛgahanturdhanārthinaḥ  |
yādṛśaṃ bhavati pretya vṛthā māṃsāni khādataḥ  || 51.62 ||

oṣadhyaḥ paśavo vṛkṣāstiryañcaḥ pakṣiṇastathā  |
yajñārthaṃ nidhanaṃ prāptāḥ prāpnuvantyucchritīḥ punaḥ  || 51.63 ||

madhuparke ca yajñe ca pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi  |
atraiva paśavo hiṃsyā nānyatreti kathaṃcana  || 51.64 ||

yajñārtheṣu paśūn hiṃsan vedatattvārthaviddvijaḥ  |
ātmānaṃ ca paśūṃścaiva gamayatyuttamāṃ gatim  || 51.65 ||

gṛhe gurāvaraṇye vā nivasannātmavān dvijaḥ  |
nāvedavihitāṃ hiṃsāmāpadyapi samācaret || 51.66 ||

yā vedavihitā hiṃsā niyatāsmiṃścarācare  |
ahiṃsāmeva tāṃ vidyādvedāddharmo hi nirbabhau  || 51.67 ||

yo'hiṃsakāni bhūtāni hinastyātmasukhecchayā  |
sa jīvaṃśca mṛtaścaiva na kvacitsukhamedhate  || 51.68 ||

yo bandhanavadhakleśān prāṇināṃ na cikīrṣati  |
sa sarvasya hitaprepsuḥ sukhamatyantamaśnute  || 51.69 ||

yaddhyāyati yatkurute ratiṃ badhnāti yatra ca  |
tadevāpnotyayatnena yo hinasti na kiṃcana  || 51.70 ||

nākṛtvā prāṇināṃ hiṃsāṃ māṃsamutpadyate kvacit |
na ca prāṇivadhaḥ svargyastasmānmāṃsaṃ vivarjayet || 51.71 ||

samutpattiṃ ca māṃsasya vadhabandhau ca dehinām  |
prasamīkṣya nivarteta sarvamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt || 51.72 ||

na bhakṣayati yo māṃsaṃ vidhiṃ hitvā piśācavat |
sa loke priyatāṃ yāti vyādhibhiśca na pīḍyate  || 51.73 ||

anumantā viśasitā nihantā krayavikrayī  |
saṃskartā copahartā ca khādakaśceti ghātakāḥ  || 51.74 ||

svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitumicchati  |
anabhyarcya pitṝn devānna tato'nyo'styapuṇyakṛt || 51.75 ||

varṣe varṣe'śvamedhena yo yajeta śataṃ samāḥ  |
māṃsāni ca na khādedyastayoḥ puṇyaphalaṃ samam  || 51.76 ||

phalamūlāśanairdivyairmunyannānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ  |
na tatphalamavāpnoti yanmāṃsaparivarjanāt || 51.77 ||

māṃ sa bhakṣayitāmutra yasya māṃsamihādmyaham  |
etanmāṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ  || 51.78 ||



52

suvarṇasteyakṛdrājñe karmācakṣāṇo musalamarpayet || 52.1 ||

vadhāttyāgādvā prayato bhavati || 52.2 ||

mahāvrataṃ dvādaśābdāni vā kuryāt || 52.3 ||

nikṣepāpahārī ca || 52.4 ||

dhānyadhanāpahārī ca kṛcchramabdam || 52.5 ||

manuṣyastrīkūpakṣetravāpīnāmapahāre cāndrāyaṇam || 52.6 ||

dravyāṇāmalpasārāṇāṃ sāṃtapanam || 52.7 ||

bhakṣyabhojyayānaśayyāsanapuṣpamūlaphalānāṃ pañcagavyapānam || 52.8 ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumaśuṣkānnaguḍavastracarmāmiṣāṇāṃ trirātramupavaset || 52.9 ||

maṇimuktāpravālatāmrarajatāyaḥkāṃsyānāṃ dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇānaśnīyāt || 52.10 ||

kārpāsakīṭajorṇādyapaharaṇe trirātraṃ payasā varteta || 52.11 ||

dviśaphaikaśaphāpaharaṇe dvirātramupavaset || 52.12 ||

pakṣigandhauṣadhirajjuvaidalānāmapaharaṇe dinamupavaset || 52.13 ||

dattvaivāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ dhanikasyāpyupāyataḥ  |
prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kuryātkalmaṣasyāpanuttaye  || 52.14 ||

yadyatparebhyastvādadyātpuruṣastu niraṅkuśaḥ  |
tena tena vihīnaḥ syādyatra yatrābhijāyate  || 52.15 ||

jīvitaṃ dharmakāmau ca dhane yasmātpratiṣṭhitau  |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena dhanahiṃsāṃ vivarjayet || 52.16 ||

prāṇihiṃsāparo yastu dhanahiṃsāparastathā  |
mahadduḥkhamavāpnoti dhanahiṃsāparastayoḥ  || 52.17 ||



53

athāgamyāgamane mahāvratavidhānenābdaṃ cīravāsā vane prājāpatyaṃ kuryāt || 53.1 ||

paradāragamane ca || 53.2 ||

govrataṃ gogamane ca || 53.3 ||

puṃsyayonāvākāśe'psu divā goyāne ca savāsāḥ snānamācaret || 53.4 ||

caṇḍālīgamane tatsāmyamāpnuyāt || 53.5 ||

ajñānataścāndrāyaṇadvayaṃ kuryāt || 53.6 ||

paśuveśyāgamane ca prājāpatyam || 53.7 ||

sakṛdduṣṭā ca strī yatpuruṣasya paradāre tadvrataṃ kuryāt || 53.8 ||

yatkarotyekarātreṇa vṛṣalīsevanāddvijaḥ  |
tadbhaikṣyabhugjapannityaṃ tribhirvarṣairvyapohati  || 53.9 ||



54

yaḥ pāpātmā yena saha saṃyujyate sa tasyaiva prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt || 54.1 ||

mṛtapañcanakhātkūpādatyantopahatāccodakaṃ pītvā brāhamaṇastrirātramupavaset || 54.2 ||

dvyahaṃ rājanyaḥ || 54.3 ||

ekāhaṃ vaiśyaḥ || 54.4 ||

śūdro naktam || 54.5 ||

sarve cānte vratasya pañcagavyaṃ pibeyuḥ || 54.6 ||

pañcagavyaṃ pibecchūdro brāhmaṇastu surāṃ pibet |
ubhau tau narakaṃ yāto mahārauravasaṃjñitam  || 54.7 ||

parvānārogyavarjamṛtāvavagacchan patnīṃ trirātramupavaset || 54.8 ||

kūṭasākṣī brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret || 54.9 ||

anudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe sacailaṃ snānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomaśca || 54.10 ||

sūryābhyuditanirmuktaḥ sacailasnātaḥ sāvitryaṣṭaśatamāvartayet || 54.11 ||

śvasṛgālaviḍvarāhakharavānaravāyasapuṃścalībhirdaṣṭaḥ sravantīmāsādya ṣoḍaśa prāṇāyāmān kuryāt || 54.12 ||

vedāgnyutsādī triṣavaṇasnāyyadhaḥśāyī saṃvatsaraṃ sakṛdbhaikṣyeṇa varteta || 54.13 ||

samutkarṣānṛte guroścālīkanirbandhe tadākṣepaṇe ca māsaṃ payasā varteta || 54.14 ||

nāstiko nāstikavṛttiḥ kṛtaghnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī brāhmaṇavṛttighnaścaite saṃvatsaraṃ bhaikṣyeṇa varteran || 54.15 ||

parivittiḥ parivettā ca yayā ca parividyate dātā yājakaśca cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt || 54.16 ||

prāṇibhūpuṇyasomavikrayī taptakṛcchram || 54.17 ||

ārdrauṣadhigandhapuṣpaphalamūlacarmavetravidalatuṣakapālakeśabhasmāsthigorasapiṇyākatilatailavikrayī prājāpatyam || 54.18 ||

ślaiṣmajatumadhūcchiṣṭaśaṅkhaśuktitrapusīsakṛṣṇalohaudumbarakhaḍgapātravikrayī cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt || 54.19 ||

raktavastraraṅgaratnagandhaguḍamadhurasorṇāvikrayī trirātramupavaset || 54.20 ||

māṃsalavaṇalākṣākṣīravikrayī cāndrāyaṇaṃ kuryāt || 54.21 ||

taṃ ca bhūyaścopanayet || 54.22 ||

uṣṭreṇa khareṇa vā gatvā nagnaḥ snātvā suptvā bhuktvā prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryāt || 54.23 ||

japitvā trīṇi sāvitryāḥ sahasrāṇi samāhitaḥ  |
māsaṃ goṣṭhe payaḥ pītvā mucyate'satpratigrahāt || 54.24 ||

ayājyayājanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣāmantyakarma ca  |
abhicāramahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrairvyapohati  || 54.25 ||

yeṣāṃ dvijānāṃ sāvitrī nānūcyeta yathāvidhi  |
tāṃścārayitvā trīn kṛcchrān yathāvidhyupanāyayet || 54.26 ||

prāyaścittaṃ cikīrṣanti vikarmasthāstu ye dvijāḥ  |
brāhmaṇyācca parityaktāsteṣāmapyetadādiśet || 54.27 ||

yadgarhitenārjayanti karmaṇā brāhmaṇā dhanam  |
tasyotsargeṇa śudhyanti japyena tapasā tathā  || 54.28 ||

vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ samatikrame  |
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittamabhojanam  || 54.29 ||

avagūrya caretkṛcchramatikṛcchraṃ nipātane  |
*kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ kurvīta viprasyotpādya śoṇitam [kucchra]  || 54.30 ||

enasvibhirnirṇiktairnārthaṃ kiṃcitsamācaret |
kṛtanirṇejanāṃścaitānna jugupseta dharmavit || 54.31 ||

bālaghnāṃśca kṛtaghnāṃśca viśuddhānapi dharmataḥ  |
śaraṇāgatahantṝṃśca strīhantṝṃśca na saṃvaset || 54.32 ||

aśītiryasya varṣāṇi bālo vāpyūnaṣoḍaśaḥ  |
prāyaścittārdhamarhanti striyo rogiṇa eva ca  || 54.33 ||

anuktaniṣkṛtīnāṃ tu pāpānāmapanuttaye  |
śaktiṃ cāvekṣya pāpaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ prakalpyet || 54.34 ||



55

atha rahasyaprāyaścittāni bhavanti || 55.1 ||

sravantīmāsādya snātaḥ pratyahaṃ ṣoḍaśa prāṇāyāmān salakṣaṇān kṛtvaikakālaṃ haviṣyāśī māsena brahmahā pūto bhavati || 55.2 ||

karmaṇo'nte payasvinīṃ gāṃ dadyāt || 55.3 ||

vratenāghamarṣaṇena ca surāpaḥ pūto bhavati || 55.4 ||

gāyatrīdaśasāhasrajapena suvarṇasteyakṛt || 55.5 ||

trirātropoṣitaḥ puruṣasūktajapahomābhyāṃ gurutalpagaḥ || 55.6 ||

yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭsarvapāpāpanodakaḥ  |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpāpanodakam  || 55.7 ||

prāṇāyāmaṃ dvijaḥ kuryātsarvapāpāpanuttaye  |
dahyante sarvapāpāni prāṇāyāmairdvijasya tu  || 55.8 ||

savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā saha  |
triḥ paṭhedāyataprāṇaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ sa ucyate  || 55.9 ||

akāraṃ cāpyukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prajāpatiḥ  |
vedatrayānniraduhadbhūrbhuvaḥ svaritīti ca  || 55.10 ||

tribhya eva tu vedebhyaḥ pādaṃ pādamadūduhat |
tadityṛco'syāḥ sāvitryāḥ parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ  || 55.11 ||

etadakṣarametāṃ ca japan vyāhṛtipūrvikām  |
saṃdhyayorvedavidvipro vedapuṇyena yujyate  || 55.12 ||

sahasrakṛtvastvabhyasya bahiretattrikaṃ dvijaḥ  |
mahato'pyenaso māsāttvacevāhirvimucyate  || 55.13 ||

etattrayavisaṃyuktaḥ kāle ca kriyayā svayā  |
viprakṣatriyaviḍjātirgarhaṇāṃ yāti sādhuṣu  || 55.14 ||

oṃkārapūrvikāstisro mahāvyāhṛtayo'vyayāḥ  |
tripadā caiva gāyatrī vijñeyā brāhmaṇo mukham  || 55.15 ||

yo'dhīte'hanyahanyetāṃ trīṇi varṣāṇyatandritaḥ  |
sa brahma paramabhyeti vāyubhūtaḥ khamūrtimān  || 55.16 ||

ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma prāṇāyāmāḥ paraṃ tapaḥ  |
sāvitryāstu paraṃ nānyanmaunātsatyaṃ viśiṣyate  || 55.17 ||

kṣaranti sarvā vaidikyo juhotiyajatikriyāḥ  |
akṣaraṃ tvakṣaraṃ jñeyaṃ brahmā caiva prajāpatiḥ  || 55.18 ||

vidhiyajñājjapayajño viśiṣṭo daśabhirguṇaiḥ  |
upāṃśuḥ syācchataguṇaḥ sahasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 55.19 ||

ye pākayajñāścatvāro vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ  |
te sarve japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 55.20 ||

japyenaiva tu saṃsidhyedbrāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
kuryādanyanna vā kuryānmaitro brāhmaṇa ucyate  || 55.21 ||



56

athāthaḥ sarvavedapavitrāṇi bhavanti || 56.1 ||

yeṣāṃ japyaiśca homaiśca dvijātayaḥ pāpebhyaḥ pūyante || 56.2 ||

aghamarṣaṇam || 56.3 ||

devakṛtam || 56.4 ||

śuddhavatyaḥ || 56.5 ||

taratsamandīyam || 56.6 ||

kūśmāṇḍyaḥ || 56.7 ||

pāvamānyaḥ || 56.8 ||

durgāsāvitrī || 56.9 ||

atīṣaṅgāḥ || 56.10 ||

padastomāḥ || 56.11 ||

samāni vyāhṛtayaḥ || 56.12 ||

bhāruṇḍāṇi || 56.13 ||

candrasāma || 56.14 ||

puruṣavrate sāmanī || 56.15 ||

abliṅgam || 56.16 ||

bārhaspatyam || 56.17 ||

gosūktam || 56.18 ||

aśvasūktam || 56.19 ||

sāmanī candrasūkte ca || 56.20 ||

śatarudriyam || 56.21 ||

atharvaśiraḥ || 56.22 ||

trisuparṇam || 56.23 ||

mahāvratam || 56.24 ||

nārāyaṇīyam || 56.25 ||

puruṣasūktaṃ ca || 56.26 ||

trīṇyājyadohāni rathantaraṃ ca agnivrataṃ vāmadevyaṃ bṛhacca  |
etāni gītāni punāti jantūn jātismaratvaṃ labhate yadīcchet || 56.27 ||



57

atha tyājyāḥ || 57.1 ||

vrātyāḥ || 57.2 ||

patitāḥ || 57.3 ||

tripuruṣaṃ mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaścāśuddhāḥ || 57.4 ||

sarva evābhojyāścāpratigrāhyāḥ || 57.5 ||

apratigrāhyebhyaśca pratigrahaprasaṅgaṃ varjayet || 57.6 ||

pratigraheṇa brāhmaṇānāṃ brāhmaṃ tejaḥ praṇaśyati || 57.7 ||

dravyāṇāṃ vāvijñāya pratigrahavidhiṃ yaḥ pratigrahaṃ kuryātsa dātrā saha nimajjati || 57.8 ||

pratigrahasamarthaśca yaḥ pratigrahaṃ varjayetsa dātṛlokamavāpnoti || 57.9 ||

edhodakamūlaphalābhayāmiṣamadhuśayyāsanagṛhapuṣpadadhiśākaṃścābhyudyatānna nirṇudet || 57.10 ||

āhūyābhyudyatāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastādanucoditām  |
grāhyāṃ prajāpatirmene api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ  || 57.11 ||

nāśnanti pitarastasya daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca  |
na ca havyaṃ vahatyagniryastāmabhyavamanyate  || 57.12 ||

gurūn bhṛtyānujjihīrṣurarciṣyan pitṛdevatāḥ  |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyānna tu tṛpyetsvayaṃ tataḥ  || 57.13 ||

eteṣvapi ca kāryeṣu samarthastatpratigrahe  |
nādadyātkulaṭāṣaṇḍha- patitebhyastathā dviṣaḥ  || 57.14 ||

guruṣu tvabhyatīteṣu vinā vā tairgṛhe vasan  |
ātmano vṛttimanvicchan gṛhṇīyātsādhutaḥ sadā  || 57.15 ||

ardhikaḥ kulamitraṃ ca dāsagopālanāpitāḥ  |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaścātmānaṃ nivedayet || 57.16 ||



58

atha gṛhāśramiṇastrividho'rtho bhavati || 58.1 ||

śuklaḥ śabalo'sitaśca || 58.2 ||

śuklenārthena yad+aurdhvadehikaṃ karoti tenāsya devatvamāsādayati || 58.3 ||

yacchabalena tanmānuṣyam || 58.4 ||

yatkṛṣṇena tattiryaktvam || 58.5 ||

svavṛttyupārjitaṃ sarveṣāṃ śuklam || 58.6 ||

anantaravṛttyupāttaṃ śabalam || 58.7 ||

ekāntaritavṛttyupāttaṃ ca kṛṣṇam || 58.8 ||

kramāgataṃ prītidāyaṃ prāptaṃ ca saha bhāryayā  |
aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ dhanaṃ śuklamudāhṛtam  || 58.9 ||

utkocaśulkasaṃprāptamavikreyasya vikrayaiḥ  |
kṛtopakārādāptaṃ ca śabalaṃ samudāhṛtam  || 58.10 ||

pārśvikadyūtacauryāpta pratirūpakasāhasaiḥ  |
vyājenopārjitaṃ yacca tatkṛṣṇaṃ samudāhṛtam  || 58.11 ||

yathāvidhena dravyeṇa yatkiṃcitkurute naraḥ  |
tathāvidhamavāpnoti sa phalaṃ pretya ceha ca  || 58.12 ||



59

gṛhāśramī vaivāhikāgnau pākayajñān kuryāt || 59.1 ||

sāyaṃ prātaścāgnihotram || 59.2 ||

*devatābhyo juhuyāt[devātābhyo] || 59.3 ||

candrārkasaṃnikarṣaviprakarṣayordarśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajeta || 59.4 ||

pratyayanaṃ paśunā || 59.5 ||

śaradgrīṣmayośca āgrayaṇena || 59.6 ||

vrīhiyavayorvā pāke || 59.7 ||

traivārṣikābhyadhikānnaḥ || 59.8 ||

pratyabdaṃ somena || 59.9 ||

vittābhāve iṣṭyā vaiśvānaryā || 59.10 ||

yajñārthaṃ bhikṣitamavāptamarthaṃ sakalameva vitaret || 59.11 ||

sāyaṃ prātarvaiśvadevaṃ juhuyāt || 59.12 ||

bhikṣāṃ ca bhikṣave dadyāt || 59.13 ||

arcitabhikṣādānena godānaphalamāpnoti || 59.14 ||

bhikṣvabhāve grāsamātraṃ gavāṃ dadyāt || 59.15 ||

vahnau vā prakṣipet || 59.16 ||

bhukte'pyanne vidyamāne na bhikṣukaṃ pratyācakṣīta || 59.17 ||

kaṇḍanī peṣaṇī cullī udakumbha upaskara iti pañca sūnā gṛhasthasya || 59.18 ||

tanniṣkṛtyarthaṃ ca brahmadevabhūtapitṛnarayajñān kuryāt || 59.19 ||

svādhyāyo brahmayajñaḥ || 59.20 ||

homo daivaḥ || 59.21 ||

pitṛtarpaṇaṃ pitryaḥ || 59.22 ||

balirbhautaḥ || 59.23 ||

nṛyajñaścātithipūjanam || 59.24 ||

devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ pitṝṇāmātmanaśca yaḥ  |
na nirvapati pañcānāmucchvasanna sa jīvati  || 59.25 ||

brahmacārī yatirbhikṣurjīvantyete gṛhāśramāt |
tasmādabhyāgatānetān gṛhastho nāvamānayet || 59.26 ||

gṛhastha eva yajate gṛhasthastapyate tapaḥ  |
pradadāti gṛhasthaśca tasmācchreṣṭho gṛhāśramī  || 59.27 ||

ṛṣayaḥ pitaro devā bhūtānyatithayastathā  |
āśāsate kuḍumbibhyastasmācchreṣṭho gṛhāśramī  || 59.28 ||

trivargasevāṃ satatānnadānaṃ surārcanaṃ brāhmaṇapūjanaṃ ca  |
svādhyāyasevāṃ pitṛtarpaṇaṃ ca kṛtvā gṛhī śakrapadaṃ prayāti  || 59.29 ||



60

brāhme muhūrte utthāya mūtrapurīṣotsargaṃ kuryāt || 60.1 ||

dakṣiṇābhimukho rātrau divā codaṅmukhaḥ saṃdhyayośca || 60.2 ||

nāpracchāditāyāṃ bhūmau || 60.3 ||

na phālakṛṣṭāyām || 60.4 ||

na chāyāyām || 60.5 ||

na coṣare || 60.6 ||

na śādvale || 60.7 ||

na sasattve || 60.8 ||

na garte || 60.9 ||

na valmīke || 60.10 ||

na pathi || 60.11 ||

na rathyāyām || 60.12 ||

na parāśucau || 60.13 ||

nodyāne || 60.14 ||

nodyānodkasamīpayoḥ || 60.15 ||

na bhasmani || 60.16 ||

nāṅgāre || 60.17 ||

na gomaye || 60.18 ||

na govraje || 60.19 ||

nākāśe || 60.20 ||

nodake || 60.21 ||

na pratyanilānalendvarkastrīgurubrāhmaṇānām || 60.22 ||

naivānavaguṇṭhitaśirāḥ || 60.23 ||

loṣṭeṣṭakāparimṛṣṭagudo gṛhītaśiśnaścotthāyādbhirmṛdbhiścoddhṛtābhirgandhalepakṣayakaraṃ śaucaṃ kuryāt || 60.24 ||

ekā liṅge gude tisrastathaikatra kare daśa  |
ubhayoḥ sapta dātavyā mṛdastisrastu pādayoḥ  || 60.25 ||

etacchaucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām  |
triguṇaṃ tu vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ tu caturguṇam  || 60.26 ||



61

atha pālāśaṃ dantadhāvanaṃ nādyāt || 61.1 ||

naiva śleṣmātakāriṣṭavibhītakadhavadhanvanajam || 61.2 ||

na ca bandhūkanirguṇḍīśigrutilvatindukajam || 61.3 ||

na ca kovidāraśamīpīlupippaleṅgudaguggulujam || 61.4 ||

na pāribhadrakāmlikāmocakaśālmalīśaṇajam || 61.5 ||

na madhuram || 61.6 ||

nāmlam || 61.7 ||

nordhvaśuṣkam || 61.8 ||

na suṣiram || 61.9 ||

na pūtigandhi || 61.10 ||

na picchilam || 61.11 ||

na dakṣiṇāparābhimukhaḥ || 61.12 ||

adyāccodaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukho vā || 61.13 ||

vaṭāsanārkakhadirakarañjabadarasarjanimbārimedāpāmārgamālatīkakubhabilvānāmanyatamam || 61.14 ||

kaṣāyaṃ tiktaṃ kaṭukaṃ ca || 61.15 ||

kanīnyagrasamasthaulyaṃ sakūrcaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam  |
prātarbhūtvā ca yatavāgbhakṣayeddantadhāvanam  || 61.16 ||

prakṣālya bhaṅktvā tajjahyācchucau deśe prayatnataḥ  |
amāvāsyāṃ na cāśnīyāddantakāṣṭhaṃ kadācana  || 61.17 ||



62

atha dvijātīnāṃ kanīnikāmūle prājāpatyaṃ nāma tīrtham || 62.1 ||

aṅguṣṭhamūle brāhmam || 62.2 ||

aṅgulyagre daivam || 62.3 ||

tarjanīmūle pitryam || 62.4 ||

anagnyuṣṇābhiraphenilābhiḥ aśūdraikakarāvarjitābhirakṣārābhiradbhiḥ śucau deśe svāsīno'ntarjānu prāṅmukhaścodaṅmukho vā tanmanāḥ sumanāścācāmet || 62.5 ||

brāhmeṇa tīrthena trirācāmet || 62.6 ||

dviḥ pramṛjyāt || 62.7 ||

khānyadbhirmūrdhānaṃ hṛdayaṃ spṛśet || 62.8 ||

hṛtkaṇṭhatālugābhiśca yathāsaṃkhyaṃ dvijātayaḥ  |
śudhyeran strī ca śūdraśca sakṛtspṛṣṭābhirantataḥ  || 62.9 ||



63

atha yogakṣemārthamīśvaramabhigacchet || 63.1 ||

naiko'dhvānaṃ prapadyeta || 63.2 ||

nādhārmikaiḥ sārdham || 63.3 ||

na vṛṣalaiḥ || 63.4 ||

na dviṣadbhiḥ || 63.5 ||

nātipratyūṣasi || 63.6 ||

nātisāyam || 63.7 ||

na saṃdhyayoḥ || 63.8 ||

na madhyāhne || 63.9 ||

na saṃnihitapānīyam || 63.10 ||

nātitūrṇam || 63.11 ||

na satataṃ bālavyādhitārtairvāhanaiḥ || 63.12 ||

na hīnāṅgaiḥ || 63.13 ||

na dīnaiḥ || 63.14 ||

na gobhiḥ || 63.15 ||

nādāntaiḥ || 63.16 ||

yavasodake vāhanānāmadattvā ātmanaḥ kṣuttṛṣṇāpanodanaṃ na kuryāt || 63.17 ||

na catuṣpathamadhitiṣṭhet || 63.18 ||

na rātrau vṛkṣamūle || 63.19 ||

na śūnyālayam || 63.20 ||

na tṛṇam || 63.21 ||

na paśūnāṃ bandhanāgāram || 63.22 ||

na keśatuṣakapālāsthibhasmāṅgārān || 63.23 ||

na kārpāsāsthi || 63.24 ||

catuṣpathaṃ prakakṣiṇīkuryāt || 63.25 ||

devatārcāṃ ca || 63.26 ||

prajñātāṃśca vanasptīn || 63.27 ||

agnibrāhmaṇagaṇikāpūrṇakumbhādarśacchatradhvajapatākāśrīvṛkṣavardhamānanandyāvartāṃśca || 63.28 ||

tālavṛntacāmarāśvagajājagodadhikṣīramadhusiddhārthakāṃśca || 63.29 ||

vīṇācandanāyudhārdragomayaphalapuṣpārdraśākagorocanādūrvāprarohāṃśca || 63.30 ||

uṣṇīṣālaṃkāramaṇikanakarajatavastrāsanayānāmiṣāṃśca || 63.31 ||

bhṛṅgāroddhṛtorvarābaddhaikapaśukumārīmīnāṃśca dṛṣṭvā prayāyāditi || 63.32 ||

atha mattonmattavyaṅgān dṛṣṭvā nivarteta || 63.33 ||

vāntaviriktamuṇḍajaṭilavāmanāṃśca || 63.34 ||

kāṣāyipravrajitamalināṃśca || 63.35 ||

tailaguḍaśuṣkagomayendhanatṛṇapalāśabhasmāṅgārāṃśca || 63.36 ||

lavaṇaklībāsavanapuṃsakakārpāsarajjunigaḍamuktakeśāṃśca || 63.37 ||

vīnācandanārdraśākoṣṇīṣālaṃkaraṇakumārīstu prasthānakāle abhinandayediti || 63.38 ||

devabrāhmaṇagurubabhrudīkṣitānāṃ chāyāṃ nākrāmet || 63.39 ||

niṣṭhyūtavāntarudhiraviṇmūtrasnānodakāni ca || 63.40 ||

na vatsatantrīṃ laṅghayet || 63.41 ||

pravarṣati na dhāvet || 63.42 ||

na vṛthā nadīṃ taret || 63.43 ||

na devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaścodakamapradāya || 63.44 ||

na bāhubhyām || 63.45 ||

na bhinnayā nāvā || 63.46 ||

na kūlamadhitiṣṭhet || 63.47 ||

na kūpamavalokayet || 63.48 ||

na laṅghayet || 63.49 ||

vṛddhabhārinṛpasnāta strīrogivaracakriṇām  |
panthā deyā nṛpastveṣāṃ mānyaḥ snātaśca bhūpateḥ  || 63.50 ||



64

paranipāneṣu na snānamācaret || 64.1 ||

ācaretpañca piṇḍānuddhṛtyāpastadāpadi || 64.2 ||

nājīrṇe || 64.3 ||

na cāturaḥ || 64.4 ||

na nagnaḥ || 64.5 ||

na rātrau || 64.6 ||

rāhudarśanavarjam || 64.7 ||

na saṃdhyayoḥ || 64.8 ||

prātaḥsnānaśīlo'ruṇatāmrāṃ prācīmālokya snāyāt || 64.9 ||

snātaḥ śiro nāvadhunet || 64.10 ||

nāṅgebhyastoyamuddharet || 64.11 ||

na tailavatsaṃspṛśet || 64.12 ||

nāprakṣālitaṃ pūrvadhṛtaṃ vasanaṃ bibhṛyāt || 64.13 ||

snāta eva soṣṇīṣe dhaute vāsasī bibhṛyāt || 64.14 ||

na mlecchāntyajapatitaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kuryāt || 64.15 ||

snāyātprasravaṇadevakhātasarovareṣu || 64.16 ||

uddhṛtātbhūmiṣṭhamudakaṃ puṇyaṃ, sthāvarātprasravat, tasmānnādeyaṃ, tasmādapi sādhuparigṛhītaṃ, sarvata eva gāṅgam || 64.17 ||

mṛttoyaiḥ kṛtamalāpakaṛṣo'psu nimajjyopaviśyāpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhirhiraṇyavarṇeti catasṛbhiridamāpaḥ pravahateti ca tīrthamabhimantrayet || 64.18 ||

tato'psu nimagnastriraghamarṣaṇaṃ japet || 64.19 ||

tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padamiti vā || 64.20 ||

drupadāṃ sāvitrīṃ ca || 64.21 ||

yuñjate mana ityanuvākaṃ vā || 64.22 ||

puruṣasūktaṃ vā || 64.23 ||

snātaścārdravāsā devapitṛtarpaṇamambhaḥstha eva kuryāt || 64.24 ||

parivartitavāsāścettīrthamuttīrya || 64.25 ||

akṛtvā devapitṛtarpaṇaṃ snānaśāṭīṃ na pīḍayet || 64.26 ||

snātvācamya vidhivadupaspṛśet || 64.27 ||

puruṣasūktena pratyṛcaṃ puruṣāya puṣpāni dadyāt || 64.28 ||

udakāñjalīṃśca || 64.29 ||

ādāveva daivena tīrthena devānāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryāt || 64.30 ||

tadanantaraṃ pitryeṇa pitṝṇām || 64.31 ||

tatrādau svavaṃśyānāṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryāt || 64.32 ||

tataḥ saṃbandhibāndhavānām || 64.33 ||

tataḥ suhṛdām || 64.34 ||

evaṃ nityasnāyī syāt || 64.35 ||

snātaśca pavitrāṇi yathāśakti japet || 64.36 ||

viśeṣataḥ sāvitrīm || 64.37 ||

puruṣasūktaṃ ca || 64.38 ||

naitābhyāmadhikamasti || 64.39 ||

snāto'dhikārī bhavati daive pitrye ca karmaṇi  |
pavitrāṇāṃ tathā japye dāne ca vidhibodhite  || 64.40 ||

alaksmīḥ kālakarṇī ca duḥsvapnaṃ durvicintitam  |
abmātreṇābhiṣiktasya naśyanta iti dhāraṇā  || 64.41 ||

yāmyaṃ hi yātanāduḥkhaṃ nityasnāyī na paśyati  |
nityasnānena pūyante ye'pi pāpakṛto narāḥ  || 64.42 ||



65

athātaḥ susnātaḥ suprakṣālitapāṇipādaḥ svācānto devatārcāyāṃ sthale vā bhagavantamanādinidhanaṃ vāsudevamabhyarcayet || 65.1 ||

aśvinoḥ prāṇastau ta iti jīvādānaṃ dattvā yuñjate mana ityanuvākenāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā jānubhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ śirasā ca namaskāraṃ kuryāt || 65.2 ||

āpo hi ṣṭheti tisṛbhirarghyaṃ nivedayet || 65.3 ||

hiraṇyavarṇā iti catasṛbhiḥ pādyam || 65.4 ||

śaṃ na āpo dhanvanyā ityācamanīyam || 65.5 ||

idamāpaḥ pravahateti snānīyam || 65.6 ||

rathe akṣeṣu vrṣabhasya vāje ityanulepanālaṃkārau || 65.7 ||

yuvā suvāsā iti vāsaḥ || 65.8 ||

puṣpāvatīriti puṣpam || 65.9 ||

dhūrasi dhūrveti dhūpam || 65.10 ||

tejo'si śukramiti dīpam || 65.11 ||

dadhikrāvṇa iti madhuparkam || 65.12 ||

hiraṇyagarbha ityaṣṭābhirnaivedyam || 65.13 ||

cāmaraṃ vyajanaṃ mātrāṃ chatraṃ yānāsane tathā  |
sāvitreṇaiva tatsarvaṃ devāya vinivedayet || 65.14 ||

evamabhyarcya tu japetsūktaṃ vai pauruṣaṃ tataḥ  |
tenaiva cājyaṃ juhuyādyadīcchecchāśvataṃ padam  || 65.15 ||



66

na naktaṃ gṛhītenodakena devapitṛkarma kuryāt || 66.1 ||

cadanamṛgamadadārukarpūrakuṅkumajātīphalavarjamanulepanaṃ na dadyāt || 66.2 ||

na vāso nīlīraktam || 66.3 ||

na maṇisuvarṇayoḥ pratirūpamalaṃkaraṇam || 66.4 ||

nograghandhi || 66.5 ||

nāgandhi || 66.6 ||

na kaṇṭakijam || 66.7 ||

kaṇṭakijamapi śuklaṃ sugandhikaṃ tu dadyāt || 66.8 ||

raktamapi kuṅkumaṃ jalajaṃ ca dadyāt || 66.9 ||

na dhūpārthe jīvajātam || 66.10 ||

na ghṛtatailaṃ vinā kiṃcana dīpārthe || 66.11 ||

nābhakṣyaṃ naivedyārthe || 66.12 ||

na bhakṣye apyajāmahiṣīkṣīre || 66.13 ||

pañcanakhamatsyavarāhamāṃsāni ca || 66.14 ||

prayataśca śucirbhūtvā sarvameva nivedayet |
tanmanāḥ sumanā bhūtvā tvarākrodhavivarjitaḥ  || 66.16 ||



67

athāgniṃ parisamuhya paryukṣya paristīrya pariṣicya sarvataḥ pākādagramuddhṛtya juhuyāt || 67.1 ||

vāsudevāya saṃkarṣaṇāya pradyumnāyāniruddhāya puruṣāya satyāyācyutāya vāsudevāya || 67.2 ||

athāgnaye, somāya, mitrāya, varuṇāya, indrāya, indrāgnibhyāṃ, viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ, prajāpataye, anumatyai, dhanvantaraye, vāstoṣpataye, agnaye sviṣṭakṛte ca || 67.3 ||

tato'nnaśeṣeṇa balimupaharet || 67.4 ||

bhakṣopabhakṣābhyām || 67.5 ||

abhitaḥ pūrveṇāgnim || 67.6 ||

ambā nāmāsīti, dulā nāmāsīti, nitantī nāmāsīti, cupuṇīkā nāmāsīti sarvāsām || 67.7 ||

nandini subhage sumaṅgali bhadraṃkarīti svaśriṣvabhipradakṣiṇam || 67.8 ||

sthūṇāyāṃ dhruvāyāṃ śriyai hiraṇyakeśyai vanaspatibhyaśca || 67.9 ||

dharmādharmayordvāre mṛtyave ca || 67.10 ||

udadhāne varuṇāya || 67.11 ||

viṣṇava ityulūkhale || 67.12 ||

marudbhya iti dṛṣadi || 67.13 ||

upari śaraṇe vaiśravaṇāya rājñe bhūtebhyaśca || 67.14 ||

indrāyendrapuruṣebhyaśceti pūrvārdhe || 67.15 ||

yamāya yamapuruṣebhya iti dakṣinārdhe || 67.16 ||

varuṇāya varuṇapuruṣebhya iti paścārdhe || 67.17 ||

somāya somapuruṣebhya ityuttarārdhe || 67.18 ||

brahmaṇe brahmapuruṣebhya iti madhye || 67.19 ||

ūrdhvamākāśāya || 67.20 ||

sthaṇḍile divācarebhyo bhūtebhya iti divā || 67.21 ||

naktaṃcarebhya iti naktam || 67.22 ||

tato *dakṣiṇāgreṣu darbheṣu pitre pitāmahāya prapitāmahāya mātre pitāmahyai prapitāmahyai nāmagotrābhyāṃ ca piṇḍanirvāpaṇaṃ kuryāt[dakṣiṇagreṣu] || 67.23 ||

piṇḍānāṃ cānulepanapuṣpadhūpanaivedyādi dadyāt || 67.24 ||

udakakalaśamupanidhāya svastyayanaṃ vācayet || 67.25 ||

śvakākaśvapacānāṃ bhuvi nirvapet || 67.26 ||

bhikṣāṃ ca dadyāt || 67.27 ||

atithipūjane ca paraṃ yatnamātiṣṭheta || 67.28 ||

sāyamatithiṃ prāptaṃ prayatnenārcayet || 67.29 ||

anāśitamatithiṃ gṛhe na vāsayet || 67.30 ||

yathā varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuryathā strīṇāṃ bhartā tathā gṛhasthasyātithiḥ || 67.31 ||

tatpūjayā svargamāpnoti || 67.32 ||

atithiryasya bhagnāśo gṛhātpratinivartate  |
tasmātsukṛtamādāya duṣkṛtaṃ tu prayacchati  || 67.33 ||

ekarātraṃ hi nivasannatithirbrāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
anityaṃ hi sthito yasmāttasmādatithirucyate  || 67.34 ||

naikagrāmīṇamatithiṃ vipraṃ sāṃgatikaṃ tathā  |
upasthitaṃ gṛhe vindyādbhāryā yatrāgnayo'pi vā  || 67.35 ||

yadi tvatithidharmeṇa kṣatriyo gṛhamāgataḥ  |
bhuktavatsu ca vipresu kāmaṃ tamapi bhojayet || 67.36 ||

vaiśyaśūdrāvapi prāptau kuṭumbe'tithidharmiṇau  |
bhojayetsaha bhṛtyaistāvānṛśaṃsyaṃ prayojayan  || 67.37 ||

itarānapi sakhyādīn saṃprītyā gṛhamāgatān  |
prakṛtānnaṃ yathāśakti bhojayetsaha bhāryayā  || 67.38 ||

svavāsinīṃ kumārīṃ ca rogiṇīṃ gurviṇīṃ tathā  |
atithibhyo'gra evaitān bhojayedavicārayan  || 67.39 ||

adattvā yastu etebhyaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte'vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
bhuñjāno na sa jānāti śvagṛdhrairjagdhimātmanaḥ  || 67.40 ||

bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu sveṣu bhṛtyeṣu caiva hi  |
bhuñjīyātāṃ tataḥ paścādavaśiṣṭaṃ tu daṃpatī  || 67.41 ||

devān pitṝnmanuṣyāṃśca bhṛtyān gṛhyāśca devatāḥ  |
pūjayitvā tataḥ paścādgṛhasthaḥ śeṣabhugbhavet || 67.42 ||

aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkte yaḥ pacatyātmakāraṇāt |
yajñaśiṣṭāśanaṃ hyetatsatāmannaṃ vidhīyate  || 67.43 ||

svādhyāyenāgnihotreṇa yajñena tapasā tathā  |
na cāpnoti gṛhī lokān yathā tvatithipūjanāt || 67.44 ||

sāyaṃ prātastvatithaye pradadyādāsanodake  |
annaṃ caiva yathāśaktyā satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam  || 67.45 ||

pratiśrayaṃ tathā śayyāṃ pādābhyaṅgaṃ sadīpakam  |
pratyekadānenāpnoti gopradānasamaṃ phalam  || 67.46 ||



68

candrārkoparāge nāśnīyāt || 68.1 ||

snātvā muktayoraśnīyāt || 68.2 ||

amuktayorastaṃ gatayoḥ snātvā dṛṣṭvā cāpare'hni || 68.3 ||

na gobrāhmaṇoparāge'śnīyāt || 68.4 ||

na rājño vyasane || 68.5 ||

pravasitāgnihotrī yadāgnihotraṃ kṛtaṃ manyate tadāśnīyāt || 68.6 ||

yadā kṛtaṃ manyeta vaiśvadevamapi || 68.7 ||

parvaṇi ca yadā kṛtaṃ manyeta parva || 68.8 ||

nāśnīyāccājīrṇe || 68.9 ||

nārdharātre || 68.10 ||

na madyāhne || 68.11 ||

na saṃdhyayoḥ || 68.12 ||

nārdravāsāḥ || 68.13 ||

naikavāsāḥ || 68.14 ||

na nagnaḥ || 68.15 ||

nodake || 68.16 ||

notkuṭakaḥ || 68.17 ||

na bhinnāsanagataḥ || 68.18 ||

na ca śayanagataḥ || 68.19 ||

na bhinnabhojane || 68.20 ||

notsaṅge || 68.21 ||

na bhuvi || 68.22 ||

na pāṇau || 68.23 ||

lavaṇaṃ ca yatra dadyāttannāśnīyāt || 68.24 ||

na bālakānnirbhatseyan || 68.25 ||

naiko mṛṣṭam || 68.26 ||

noddhṛtasneham || 68.27 ||

na divā dhānāḥ || 68.28 ||

na rātrau tilasaṃbandham || 68.29 ||

na dadhisaktūn || 68.30 ||

na kovidāravaṭapippalaśāṇaśākam || 68.31 ||

nādattvā || 68.32 ||

nāhutvā || 68.33 ||

nānārdrapādaḥ || 68.34 ||

nānārdrakaramukhaśca || 68.35 ||

nocchiṣṭaśca ghṛtamādadyāt || 68.36 ||

na candrārkatārakā nirīkṣeta || 68.37 ||

na mūrdhānaṃ spṛśet || 68.38 ||

na brahma kīrtayet || 68.39 ||

prāṅmukho'śnīyāt || 68.40 ||

dakṣiṇāmukho vā || 68.41 ||

abhipūjyānnam || 68.42 ||

sumanāḥ sragvyanuliptaśca || 68.43 ||

na niḥśeṣakṛtsyāt || 68.44 ||

anyatra dadhimadhusarpiḥpayaḥsaktupalamodakebhyaḥ || 68.45 ||

nāśnīyādbhāryayā sārdhaṃ nākāśe na tathotthitaḥ  |
bahūnāṃ prekṣamāṇānāṃ naikasmin bahavastathā  || 68.46 ||

śūnyālaye vahnigṛhe devāgāre kathaṃcana  |
pibennāñjalinā toyaṃ nātisauhityamācaret || 68.47 ||

na tṛtīyamathāśnīta na cāpathyaṃ kathaṃcana  |
nātiprage nātisāyaṃ na sāyaṃ prātarāśitaḥ  || 68.48 ||

na bhāvaduṣṭamaśnīyānna bhāṇḍe bhāvadūṣite  |
śayānaḥ prauḍhapādaśca kṛtvā caivāvasakthikām  || 68.49 ||



69

nāṣṭamīcaturdaśīpañcadaśīṣu striyamupeyāt || 69.1 ||

na śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā || 69.2 ||

na dattvā || 69.3 ||

nopanimantritaḥ śrāddhe || 69.4 ||

na vratī || 69.5 ||

na dīkṣitaḥ || 69.6 ||

na devāyatanaśmaśānaśūnyālayeṣu || 69.7 ||

na vṛkṣamūleṣu || 69.8 ||

na divā || 69.9 ||

na saṃdhyayoḥ || 69.10 ||

na malinām || 69.11 ||

na malinaḥ || 69.12 ||

nābhyaktām || 69.13 ||

nābhyaktaḥ || 69.14 ||

na rogārtām || 69.15 ||

na rogārtaḥ || 69.16 ||

na hīnāṅgīṃ nādhikāṅgīṃ tathaiva ca vayo'dhikām  |
nopeyādgurviṇīṃ nārīṃ dīrghamāyurjijīviṣuḥ  || 69.17 ||



70

nārdrapādaḥ supyāt || 70.1 ||

nottarāparaśirāḥ || 70.2 ||

na nagnaḥ || 70.3 ||

nānuvaṃśam || 70.4 ||

nākāśe || 70.5 ||

na pālāśe śayane || 70.6 ||

na pañcadārukṛte || 70.7 ||

na gajabhagnakṛte || 70.8 ||

na vidyuddagdhakṛte || 70.9 ||

na bhinne || 70.10 ||

nāgnipṛṣṭhe || 70.11 ||

na ghaṭāsiktadrumaje || 70.12 ||

na śmaśānaśūnyālayadevatāyataneṣu || 70.13 ||

na capalamadhye || 70.14 ||

na nārīmadhye || 70.15 ||

na dhānyagoguruhutāśanasurāṇāmupari || 70.16 ||

nocchiṣṭo na divā supyātsaṃdhyayorna ca bhasmani  |
deśe na ca aśucau nārdre na ca parvatamastake  || 70.17 ||



71

atha na kaṃcanāvamanyeta || 71.1 ||

na ca hīnādhikāṅgānmūrkhān dhanahīnānavahaset || 71.2 ||

na hīnān seveta || 71.3 ||

svādhyāyavirodhi karma nācaret || 71.4 ||

vayo'nurūpaṃ veṣaṃ kuryāt || 71.5 ||

śrutasyābhijanasya dhanasya deśasya ca || 71.6 ||

noddhataḥ || 71.7 ||

nityaṃ śāstrādyavekṣī syāt || 71.8 ||

sati vibhave na jīrṇamalavadvāsāḥ syāt || 71.9 ||

na nāstītyabhibhāṣeta || 71.10 ||

na nirgandhogragandhi raktaṃ ca mālyaṃ bibhṛyāt || 71.11 ||

bibhṛyājjalajaṃ raktamapi || 71.12 ||

yaṣṭiṃ ca vaiṇavīm || 71.13 ||

kamaṇḍaluṃ ca sodakam || 71.14 ||

kārpāsamupavītam || 71.15 ||

raukme ca kuṇḍale || 71.16 ||

nādityamudyantamīkṣeta || 71.17 ||

nāstaṃ yāntam || 71.18 ||

na vāsasā tirohitam || 71.19 ||

na cādarśajalamadhyastham || 71.20 ||

na madhyāhne || 71.21 ||

na kruddhasya gurormukham || 71.22 ||

na tailodakayoḥ svāṃ chāyām || 71.23 ||

na malavatyādarśe || 71.24 ||

na patnīṃ bhojanasamaye || 71.25 ||

na striyaṃ nagnām || 71.26 ||

na kaṃcana mehamānam || 71.27 ||

na cālānabhraṣṭaṃ kuñjaram || 71.28 ||

na ca viṣamastho vṛṣādiyuddham || 71.29 ||

nonmattam || 71.30 ||

na mattam || 71.31 ||

nāmedhyamagnau prakṣipet || 71.32 ||

nāsṛk || 71.33 ||

na viṣam || 71.34 ||

ambhasyāpi || 71.35 ||

nāgniṃ laṅghayet || 71.36 ||

na pādau pratāpayet || 71.37 ||

na kuśeṣu parimṛjyāt || 71.38 ||

na kāṃsyabhājane dhāvayet || 71.39 ||

na pādaṃ pādena || 71.40 ||

na bhuvamālikhet || 71.41 ||

na loṣṭamardī syāt || 71.42 ||

na tṛṇacchedī syāt || 71.43 ||

na dantairnakhalomāni chindyāt || 71.44 ||

dyūtaṃ ca varjayet || 71.45 ||

bālātapasevāṃ va || 71.46 ||

vastropānahamālyopavītānyanyadhṛtāni na dhārayet || 71.47 ||

na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyāt || 71.48 ||

nocchiṣṭahaviṣī || 71.49 ||

na tilān || 71.50 ||

na cāsyopadiśeddharmam || 71.51 ||

na vratam || 71.52 ||

na saṃhatābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ śira udaraṃ ca kaṇḍūyet || 71.53 ||

na dadhisumanasī pratyācakṣīta || 71.54 ||

nātmanaḥ srajamapakarṣet || 71.55 ||

suptaṃ na prabodhayet || 71.56 ||

na raktaṃ virāgayet || 71.57 ||

nodakyāmabhibhāṣeta || 71.58 ||

na mlecchāntyajān || 71.59 ||

agnidevabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇimuddharet || 71.60 ||

na parakṣetre carantīṃ gāmācakṣīta || 71.61 ||

na pibantaṃ vatsakam || 71.62 ||

noddhatān praharṣayet || 71.63 ||

na śūdrarājye nivaset || 71.64 ||

nādhārmikajanākīrṇe || 71.65 ||

na saṃvasedvaidyahīne || 71.66 ||

nopasṛṣṭe || 71.67 ||

na ciraṃ parvate || 71.68 ||

na vṛthāceṣṭāṃ kuryāt || 71.69 ||

na nṛtyagīte || 71.70 ||

nāsphoṭanam || 71.71 ||

nāślīlaṃ kīrtayet || 71.72 ||

nānṛtam || 71.73 ||

nāpriyam || 71.74 ||

na kaṃcinmarmaṇi spṛśet || 71.75 ||

nātmānamavajānīyāddīrghamāyurjijīviṣuḥ || 71.76 ||

ciraṃ saṃdhyopāsanaṃ kuryāt || 71.77 ||

na sarpaśastraiḥ kṛīḍet || 71.78 ||

animittataḥ khāni na spṛśet || 71.79 ||

parasya daṇḍaṃ nodyacchet || 71.80 ||

śāsyaṃ śāsanārthaṃ tāḍayet || 71.81 ||

taṃ veṇudalena rajjvā vā pṛṣṭhe || 71.82 ||

devabrāhmaṇaśāstramahātmanāṃ parivādaṃ pariharet || 71.83 ||

dharmaviruddhau cārthakāmau || 71.84 ||

lokavidviṣṭaṃ ca dharmamapi || 71.85 ||

parvasu ca śāntihomān kuryāt || 71.86 ||

na tṛṇamapi chindyāt || 71.87 ||

alaṃkṛtaśca tiṣṭhet || 71.88 ||

evamācārasevī syāt || 71.89 ||

śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmyaṃ sādhubhiśca niṣevitam  |
tamācāraṃ niṣeveta dharmakāmo jitendriyaḥ  || 71.90 ||

ācārāllabhate cāyurācārādīpsitāṃ gatim  |
ācārāddhanamakṣayyamācārāddhantyalakṣaṇam  || 71.91 ||

sarvalakṣanahīno'pi yaḥ sadācāravānnaraḥ  |
śraddhadhāno'nasūyaśca śataṃ varṣāṇi jīvati  || 71.93 ||



72

damayamena tiṣṭhet || 72.1 ||

damaścendriyāṇāṃ prakīrtitaḥ || 72.2 ||

dāntasyāyaṃ lokaḥ paraśca || 72.3 ||

nādāntasya kriyā kācitsamṛdhyati || 72.4 ||

damaḥ pavitraṃ paramaṃ maṅgalyaṃ paramaṃ damaḥ  |
damena sarvamāpnoti yatkiṃcinmanasecchati  || 72.5 ||

daśārdhayuktena rathena yāto manovaśenāryapathānuvartinā  |
taṃ cedrathaṃ nāpaharanti vājinastathāgataṃ nāvajayanti śatravaḥ  || 72.6 ||

āpūryamāṇamacalapratiṣṭhaṃ samudramāpaḥ praviśanti yadvat |
tadvatkāmā yaṃ praviśanti sarve sa śāntimāpnoti na kāmakāmī  || 72.7 ||



73

atha śrāddhepsuḥ pūrvedyurbrāhmaṇānāmantrayet || 73.1 ||

dvitīye'hni śuklapakṣasya pūrvāhṇe kṛṣṇapakṣasyāparāhṇe viprān susnātān svācāntān yathābhūyo vayaḥkrameṇa kuśottareṣvāsaneṣūpaveśayet || 73.2 ||

dvau daive prāṅmukhau trīṃśca pitrye udaṅmukhān || 73.3 ||

ekaikamubhayatra veti || 73.4 ||

āmaśrāddheṣu kāmyeṣu ca prathamapañcakenāgniṃ hutvā || 73.5 ||

paśuśrāddheṣu madhyamapañcakena || 73.6 ||

amāvāsyāsūttamapañcakena || 73.7 ||

āgrahāyaṇyā ūrdhvaṃ kṛṣṇāṣṭakāsu ca krameṇaiva prathamamadhyamottamapañcakaiḥ || 73.8 ||

anvaṣṭakāsu ca || 73.9 ||

tato brāhmaṇānujñātaḥ pitṝnāvāhayet || 73.10 ||

apayantvasurā iti dvābhyāṃ tilaiḥ yātudhānānāṃ visarjanaṃ kṛtvā || 73.11 ||

eta pitaraḥ sarvāṃstānagra ā me yantvetadvaḥ pitara ityāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā, kuśatilamiśreṇa gandhodakena yāstiṣṭhantyamṛtā vāgiti yanme māteti ca pādyaṃ nivedya, arghyaṃ kṛtvā nivedya cānulepanaṃ kṛtvā kuśatilavastrapuṣpālaṃkāradhūpadīpairyathāśaktyā viprān samabhyarcya ghṛtaplutamannamādāya ādityā rudrā vasava iti vīkṣya, agnau karavāṇītyuktvā tacca vipraiḥ kurvityukte āhutitrayaṃ dadyāt || 73.12 ||

ye māmakāḥ pitara etadvaḥ pitaro'yaṃ yajña iti ca haviranumantraṇaṃ kṛtvā yathopapanneṣu pātreṣu viśeṣādrajatamayeṣvannaṃ namo viśvebhyo devebhya ityannamādau prāṅmukhayornivedayet || 73.13 ||

pitre pitāmahāya prapitāmahāya ca nāmagotrābhyāmudaṅmukheṣu || 73.14 ||

tadadatsu brāhmaṇeṣu yanme prakāmādahorātrairyadvaḥ kravyāditi japet || 73.15 ||

itihāsapurāṇadharmaśāstrāṇi ceti || 73.16 ||

ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau dakṣināgreṣu kuśeṣu pṛthivī darvirakṣitā ityekaṃ piṇḍaṃ pitre nidadhyāt || 73.17 ||

antarikṣaṃ darvirakṣitā itydvitīyaṃ piṇḍaṃ pitāmahāya || 73.18 ||

dyaurdarvirakṣitā iti tṛtīyaṃ prapitāmahāya || 73.19 ||

ye atra pitaraḥ pretā iti vāso deyam || 73.20 ||

vīrānnaḥ pitaro dhatta ityannam || 73.21 ||

atra pitaro mādayadhvaṃ yathābhāgamiti darbhamūle karāvagharṣaṇam || 73.22 ||

ūrjaṃ vahantīrityanena sodakena pradakṣiṇaṃ piṇḍānāṃ vikiraṇaṃ kṛtvā arghapuṣpadhūpālepanānnādibhakṣyabhojyāni nivedayet || 73.23 ||

udakapātraṃ madhughṛtatilaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ ca || 73.24 ||

bhuktavatsu brāhmaṇeṣu tṛptimāgateṣu, mā me kṣeṣthetyannaṃ satṛṇamabhyukṣyānnavikiramucchiṣṭāgrataḥ kṛtvā, tṛptā bhavantaḥ saṃpannamiti ca pṛṣṭvā udaṅmukheṣvācamanamādau dattvā, tataḥ prāṅmukheṣu dattvā, tataśca suprokṣitamiti śrāddhadeśaṃ saṃprokṣya, darbhapāṇiḥ sarvaṃ kuryāt || 73.25 ||

tataḥ prāṅmukhāgrato yanme rāma iti pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā pratyetya ca, yathāśaktidakṣiṇābhiḥ samabhyarcya, abhiramantu bhavanta ityuktvā, tairukte'bhiratāḥ sma iti, devāśca pitaraścetyabhijapet || 73.26 ||

akṣayyodakaṃ ca nāmagotrābhyāṃ dattvā viśve devāḥ prīyantāmiti prāṅmukhebhyastataḥ prāñjaliridaṃ tanmanāḥ sumanā yāceta || 73.27 ||

dātāro no'bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ saṃtatireva ca  |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamadbahu deyaṃ ca no'stviti  || 73.28 ||

tathāstviti brūyuḥ || 73.29 ||

annaṃ ca no bahu bhavedatithīṃśca labhemahi  |
yācitāraśca naḥ santu mā ca yāciṣma kaṃcana  || 73.30 ||

ityetābhyāmāśiṣaḥ pratigṛhya || 73.31 ||

vāje vāja ityca tato brāhmaṇāṃśca visarjayet |
pūjayitvā yathānyāyamanuvrajyābhivādya ca  || 73.32 ||



74

aṣṭakāsu daivapūrvaṃ śākamāṃsāpūpaiḥ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvānvaṣṭakāsvaṣṭakāvadvahnau hutvā daivapūrvameva mātre pitāmahyai prapitāmahyai ca pūrvavadbrāhmaṇān bhojayitvā dakṣiṇābhiścābhyarcyānuvrajya visarjayet || 74.1 ||

tataḥ karṣūḥ kuryāt || 74.2 ||

tanmūle prāgudagagnyupasamādhānaṃ kṛtvā piṇḍanirvapaṇam || 74.3 ||

karṣūtrayamūle puruṣāṇāṃ karṣūtrayamūle strīṇām || 74.4 ||

puruṣakarṣūtrayaṃ sānnenodakena pūrayet || 74.5 ||

strīkarṣūtrayaṃ sānnena payasā || 74.6 ||

dadhnā māṃsena payasā pratyekaṃ karṣūtrayam || 74.7 ||

pūrayitvā japedetadbhavadbhyo bhavatībyo'stu cākṣayam || 74.8 ||



75

pitari jīvati yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt, sa yeṣāṃ pitā kuryātteṣāṃ kuryāt || 75.1 ||

pitari pitāmahe ca jīvati yeṣāṃ pitāmahaḥ || 75.2 ||

pitari pitāmahe prapitāmahe ca jīvati naiva kuryāt || 75.3 ||

yasya pitā pretaḥ syātsa pitre piṇḍaṃ nidhāya pitāmahātparaṃ dvābhyāṃ dadyāt || 75.4 ||

yasya pitā pitāmahaśca pretau syātāṃ sa tābhyāṃ piṇḍau dattvā pitāmahapitāmahāya dadyāt || 75.5 ||

yasya pitāmahaḥ pretaḥ syātsa tasmai piṇḍaṃ nidhāya prapitāmahātparaṃ dvābhyāṃ dadyāt || 75.6 ||

mātāmahānāmapyevaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ  |
mantroheṇa yathānyāyaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ mantravarjitam  || 75.7 ||



76

amāvāsyāstisro'ṣṭakāstisro'nvaṣṭakā māghī prauṣṭhapadyūrdhvaṃ kṛṣṇatrayodaśī vrīhiyavapākau ceti || 76.1 ||

etāṃstu śrāddhakālān vai nityānāha prajāpatiḥ  |
śrāddhameteṣvakurvāṇo narakaṃ pratipadyate  || 76.2 ||



77

ādityasaṃkramaṇam || 77.1 ||

viṣuvaddvayam || 77.2 ||

viśeṣeṇāyanadvayam || 77.3 ||

vyatīpātaḥ || 77.4 ||

janmarkṣam || 77.5 ||

abhyudayaśca || 77.6 ||

etāṃstu śrāddhakālān vai kāmyānāha prajāpatiḥ  |
śrāddhameteṣu yaddattaṃ tadānantyāya kalpate  || 77.7 ||

saṃdhyārātryorna kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ khalu vicakṣaṇaiḥ  |
tayorapi ca kartavyaṃ yadi syādrāhudarśanam  || 77.8 ||

rāhudarśanadattaṃ hi śrāddhamācandratārakam  |
guṇavatsarvakāmīyaṃ pitṝṇāmupatiṣṭhate  || 77.9 ||



78

satatamāditye'hni śrāddhaṃ kurvannārogyamāpnoti || 78.1 ||

saubhāgyaṃ cāndre || 78.2 ||

samaravijayaṃ kauje || 78.3 ||

sarvān kāmān baudhe || 78.4 ||

vidyāmabhīṣṭāṃ jaive || 78.5 ||

dhanaṃ śaukre || 78.6 ||

jīvitaṃ śanaiścare || 78.7 ||

svargaṃ kṛttikāsu || 78.8 ||

apatyaṃ rohiṇīṣu || 78.9 ||

brahmavarcasyaṃ saumye || 78.10 ||

karmasiddhiṃ raudre || 78.11 ||

bhuvaṃ punarvasau || 78.12 ||

puṣṭiṃ puṣye || 78.13 ||

śriyaṃ sārpe || 78.14 ||

sarvān kāmān paitrye || 78.15 ||

saubhāgyaṃ bhāgye || 78.16 ||

dhanamāyamaṇe || 78.17 ||

jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ haste || 78.18 ||

rūpavataḥ sutāṃstvāṣṭre || 78.19 ||

vāṇijyasiddhiṃ svātau || 78.20 ||

kanakaṃ viśākhāsu || 78.21 ||

mitrāṇi maitre || 78.22 ||

rājyaṃ śākre || 78.23 ||

kṛṣiṃ mūle || 78.24 ||

samudrayānasiddhimāpye || 78.25 ||

sarvān kāmān vaiśvadeve || 78.26 ||

śraiṣṭhyamabhijiti || 78.27 ||

sarvān kāmān śravaṇe || 78.28 ||

lavaṇaṃ vāsave || 78.29 ||

ārogyaṃ vāruṇe || 78.30 ||

kupyadravyamāje || 78.31 ||

gṛhamāhirbudhnye || 78.32 ||

gāḥ pauṣṇe || 78.33 ||

turaṅgamānāśvine || 78.34 ||

jīvitaṃ yāmye || 78.35 ||

gṛhaṃ surūpāḥ striyaḥ pratipadi || 78.36 ||

kanyāṃ varadāṃ dvitīyāyām || 78.37 ||

sarvān kāmāṃstṛtīyāyām || 78.38 ||

paśūṃścaturthyām || 78.39 ||

surūpān sutān pañcamyām || 78.40 ||

dyūtavijayaṃ ṣaṣṭhyām || 78.41 ||

kṛṣiṃ saptamyām || 78.42 ||

vāṇijyamaṣṭamyām || 78.43 ||

paśūnnavamyām || 78.44 ||

vājino daśamyām || 78.45 ||

putrān brahmavarcasvina ekādaśyām || 78.46 ||

kanakarajataṃ dvādaśyām || 78.47 ||

sāubhāgyaṃ trayodaśyām || 78.48 ||

sarvān kāmān pañcadaśyām || 78.49 ||

śastrahatānāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi caturdaśī śastā || 78.50 ||

api pitṛgīte gāthe bhavataḥ || 78.51 ||

api jāyeta so'smākaṃ kule kaścinnarottamaḥ  |
prāvṛṭkāle'site pakṣe trayodaśyāṃ samāhitaḥ  || 78.52 ||

madhūtkaṭena yaḥ śrāddhaṃ pāyasena samācaret |
kārttikaṃ sakalaṃ māsaṃ prākchāye kuñjarasya ca  || 78.53 ||



79

atha na naktaṃ gṛhītenodakena śrāddhaṃ kuryāt || 79.1 ||

kuśābhāve kuśasthāne kāśān dūrvāṃ vā dadyāt || 79.2 ||

vāso'rthe kārpāsotthaṃ sūtram || 79.3 ||

daśāṃ visarjayetyadyapyahatavastrajā syāt || 79.4 ||

ugragandhīnyagandhīni kaṇṭakijāni ca puṣpāṇi || 79.5 ||

śuklāni sugandhīni kaṇṭakijānyapi jalajāni raktānyapi dadyāt || 79.6 ||

vasāṃ medaṃ ca dīpārthe na dadyāt || 79.7 ||

ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ vā dadyāt || 79.8 ||

jīvajaṃ sarvaṃ dhūpārthe na dadyāt || 79.9 ||

madhughṛtasaṃyuktaṃ gugguluṃ dadyāt || 79.10 ||

candanakuṅkumakarpūrāgarupadmakānyanulepanārthe || 79.11 ||

na pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ dadyāt || 79.12 ||

hastena ca ghṛtavyañjanādi || 79.13 ||

taijasāni pātrāṇi dadyāt || 79.14 ||

viśeṣato rājatāni || 79.15 ||

khaḍgakutapakṛṣṇājinatilasiddhārthakākṣatāni ca pavitrāṇi rakṣoghnāni ca nidadhyāt || 79.16 ||

pippalīmukundakabhūstṛṇaśigrusarṣapasurasāsarjakasuvarcalakūśmāṇḍālābuvārtākapālakyopodakītaṇḍulīyakakusumbhapiṇḍālukamahiṣīkṣīrāṇi varjayet || 79.17 ||

rājamāṣamasūraparyuṣitakṛtalavaṇāni ca || 79.18 ||

kopaṃ pariharet || 79.19 ||

nāśru pātayet || 79.20 ||

na tvarāṃ kuryāt || 79.21 ||

ghṛtādidāne taijasāni pātrāṇi khaḍgapātrāṇi phalgupātrāṇi ca praśastāni || 79.22 ||

atra ca śloko bhavati || 79.23 ||

sauvarṇarājatābhyāṃ ca khaḍgenaudumbareṇa vā  |
dattamakṣayyatāṃ yāti phalgupātreṇa cāpyatha  || 79.24 ||



80

tilairvrīhiyavairmāṣairadbhirmūlaphalaiḥ śākaiḥ śyāmākaiḥ priyaṅgubhirnīvārairmudgairgodhūmaiśca māsaṃ prīyante || 80.1 ||

dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena || 80.2 ||

trīn hāriṇena || 80.3 ||

caturaścaurabhreṇa || 80.4 ||

pañca śākunena || 80.5 ||

ṣaṭchāgena || 80.6 ||

sapta rauraveṇa || 80.7 ||

aṣṭau pārṣatena || 80.8 ||

nava gāvayena || 80.9 ||

daśa māhiṣeṇa || 80.10 ||

ekādaśa tūpareṇājena || 80.11 ||

saṃvatsaraṃ gavyena payasā tadvikārairvā || 80.12 ||

atra pitṛgītā gāthā bhavati || 80.13 ||

kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkaṃ māṃsaṃ vārdhrīṇasasya ca  |
viṣāṇavarjyā ye khaḍgā āsūryaṃ tāṃstu bhuṅkṣmahe  || 80.14 ||



81

nānnamāsanamāropayet || 81.1 ||

na padā spṛśet || 81.2 ||

nāvakṣutaṃ kuryāt || 81.3 ||

tilaiḥ sarṣapairvā yātudhānān visarjayet || 81.4 ||

saṃvṛte ca śrāddhaṃ kuryāt || 81.5 ||

na rajasvalāṃ paśyet || 81.6 ||

na śvānam || 81.7 ||

na viḍvarāham || 81.8 ||

na grāmakukkuṭam || 81.9 ||

prayatnātśrāddhamajasya darśayet || 81.10 ||

aśnīyurbrāhmaṇāśca vāgyatāḥ || 81.11 ||

na veṣṭitaśirasaḥ || 81.12 ||

na sopānatkāḥ || 81.13 ||

na pīṭhopahitapādāḥ || 81.14 ||

na hīnāṅgā adhikāṅgāḥ śrāddhaṃ paśyeyuḥ || 81.15 ||

na śūdrāḥ || 81.16 ||

na patitāḥ || 81.17 ||

na mahārogiṇaḥ || 81.18 ||

tatkālaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brāhmaṇānumatena bhikṣukaṃ vā pūjayet || 81.19 ||

havirguṇānna brāhmaṇā brūyurdātrā pṛṣṭāḥ || 81.20 ||

yāvadūṣmā bhavatyanne yāvadaśnanti vāgyatāḥ  |
tāvadaśnanti pitaro yāvannoktā havirguṇāḥ  || 81.21 ||

sārvavarṇikamannādyaṃ saṃnīyāplāvya vāriṇā  |
samutsṛjedbhuktavatāmagrato vikiran bhuvi  || 81.22 ||

asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitām  |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ syāddarbheṣu vikiraśca yaḥ  || 81.23 ||

uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigatamajihmasyāśaṭhasya ca  |
dāsavargasya tatpitrye bhāgadheyaṃ pracakṣate  || 81.24 ||



82

daive karmaṇi brāhmaṇaṃ na parīkṣeta || 82.1 ||

prayatnātpitrye parīkṣeta || 82.2 ||

hīnādhikāṅgān vivarjayet || 82.3 ||

vikarmasthāṃśca || 82.4 ||

baiḍālavratikān || 82.5 ||

vṛthāliṅginaḥ || 82.6 ||

nakṣatrajīvinaḥ || 82.7 ||

devalakāṃśca || 82.8 ||

cikitsakān || 82.9 ||

anūḍhāputrān || 82.10 ||

tatputrān || 82.11 ||

bahuyājinaḥ || 82.12 ||

grāmayājinaḥ || 82.13 ||

śūdrayājinaḥ || 82.14 ||

ayājyayājinaḥ || 82.15 ||

vrātyān || 82.16 ||

tadyājinaḥ || 82.17 ||

parvakārān || 82.18 ||

sūcakān || 82.19 ||

bhṛtakādhyāpakān || 82.20 ||

bhṛtakādhyāpitān || 82.21 ||

śūdrānnapuṣṭān || 82.22 ||

patitasaṃsargān || 82.23 ||

anadhīyānān || 82.24 ||

saṃdhyopāsanabhraṣṭān || 82.25 ||

rājasevakān || 82.26 ||

nagnān || 82.27 ||

pitrā vivadamānān || 82.28 ||

pitṛmātṛgurvagnisvādhyāyatyāginaśca || 82.29 ||

brāhmaṇāpaśadā hyete kathitāḥ paṅktidūṣakāḥ  |
etān vivarjayedyatnācchrāddhakarmaṇi paṇḍitaḥ  || 82.30 ||



83

atha paṅktipāvanāḥ || 83.1 ||

triṇāciketaḥ || 83.2 ||

pañcāgniḥ || 83.3 ||

jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ || 83.4 ||

vedapāragaḥ || 83.5 ||

vedāṅgasyāpyekasya pāragaḥ || 83.6 ||

purāṇetihāsavyākaraṇapāragaḥ || 83.7 ||

dharmaśāstrasyāpyekasya pāragaḥ || 83.8 ||

tīrthapūtaḥ || 83.9 ||

yajñapūtaḥ || 83.10 ||

tapaḥpūtaḥ || 83.11 ||

satyapūtaḥ || 83.12 ||

mantrapūtaḥ || 83.13 ||

gāyatrījapanirataḥ || 83.14 ||

brahmadeyānusaṃtānaḥ || 83.15 ||

trisuparṇaḥ || 83.16 ||

jāmātā || 83.17 ||

dauhitraśceti pātram || 83.18 ||

viśeṣeṇa ca yoginaḥ || 83.19 ||

atra pitṛgītā gāthā bhavati || 83.20 ||

api sa syātkule'smākaṃ bhojayedyastu yoginam  |
vipraṃ śrāddhe prayatnena yena tṛpyāmahe vayam  || 83.21 ||



84

na mlecchaviṣaye śrāddhaṃ kuryāt || 84.1 ||

na gacchenmlecchaviṣayam || 84.2 ||

paranipāneṣvapaḥ pītvā tatsāmyamupagacchatīti || 84.3 ||

cāturvarṇyavyavasthānaṃ yasmin deśe na vidyate  |
sa mlecchadeśo jijñeya āryāvartastataḥ paraḥ  || 84.4 ||



85

atha puṣkareṣvakṣayaṃ śrāddham || 85.1 ||

japyahomatapāṃsi ca || 85.2 ||

puṣkare snānamātrātsarvapāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati || 85.3 ||

evameva gayāśīrṣe || 85.4 ||

vaṭe || 85.5 ||

amarakaṇṭakaparvate || 85.6 ||

varāhaparvate || 85.7 ||

yatra kvacana narmadātīre || 85.8 ||

yamunātīre || 85.9 ||

gaṅgāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 85.10 ||

kuśāvarte || 85.11 ||

binduke || 85.12 ||

nīlaparvate || 85.13 ||

kanakhale || 85.14 ||

kubjāmre || 85.15 ||

bhṛgutuṅge || 85.16 ||

kedāre || 85.17 ||

mahālaye || 85.18 ||

naḍantikāyām || 85.19 ||

sugandhāyām || 85.20 ||

śākaṃbharyām || 85.21 ||

phalgutīrthe || 85.22 ||

mahāgaṅgāyām || 85.23 ||
trihalikāgrāme || 85.24 ||

kumāradhārāyām || 85.25 ||

prabhāse || 85.26 ||

yatra kvacana sarasvatyāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 85.27 ||

gaṅgādvāre || 85.28 ||

prayāge ca || 85.29 ||

gaṅgāsāgarasaṃgame || 85.30 ||

satataṃ naimiṣāraṇye || 85.31 ||

vārāṇasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 85.32 ||

agastyāśrame || 85.33 ||

kaṇVāśrame || 85.34 ||

kauśikyām || 85.35 ||

sarayūtīre || 85.36 ||

śoṇasya jyotiṣāyāśca saṃgame || 85.37 ||

śrīparvate || 85.38 ||

kālodake || 85.39 ||

uttaramānase || 85.40 ||

baḍabāyām || 85.41 ||

mataṅgavāpyām || 85.42 ||

saptārṣe || 85.43 ||

viṣṇupade || 85.44 ||

svargamārgapade || 85.45 ||

godāvaryām || 85.46 ||

gomatyām || 85.47 ||

vetravatyām || 85.48 ||

vipāśāyām || 85.49 ||

vitastāyām || 85.50 ||

śatadrūtīre || 85.51 ||

candrabhāgāyām || 85.52 ||

irāvatyām || 85.53 ||

sindhostīre || 85.54 ||

dakṣiṇe pañcanade || 85.55 ||

ausaje || 85.56 ||

evamādiṣvathānyeṣu tīrtheṣu || 85.57 ||

saridvarāsu || 85.58 ||

sarveṣvapi svabhāveṣu || 85.59 ||

pulineṣu || 85.60 ||

prasravaṇeṣu || 85.61 ||

parvateṣu || 85.62 ||

nikuñjeṣu || 85.63 ||

vaneṣu || 85.64 ||

upavaneṣu || 85.65 ||

gomayenopalipteṣu gṛheṣu || 85.66 ||

manojñeṣu || 85.67 ||

atra ca pitṛgītā gāthā bhavanti || 85.68 ||

kule'smākaṃ sa jantuḥ syādyo no dadyājjalāñjalīn  |
nadīṣu bahutoyāsu śītalāsu viśeṣataḥ  || 85.69 ||

api jāyeta so'smākaṃ kule kaścinnarottamaḥ  |
gayāśīrṣe vaṭe śrāddhaṃ yo naḥ kuryātsamāhitaḥ  || 85.70 ||

eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yadyeko'pi gayāṃ vrajet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣamutsṛjet || 85.71 ||



86

atha vṛṣotsargaḥ || 86.1 ||

kārttikyāmāśvayujyāṃ vā || 86.2 ||

tatrādāveva vṛṣabhaṃ parīkṣeta || 86.3 ||

jīvadvatsāyāḥ payasvinyāḥ putram || 86.4 ||

sarvalakṣaṇopetam || 86.5 ||

nīlam || 86.6 ||

lohitaṃ vā mukhapucchapādaśṛṅgaśuklam || 86.7 ||

yūthasyācchādakam || 86.8 ||

tato gavāṃ madhye susamiddhamagniṃ paristīrya pauṣṇaṃ caruṃ payasā śrapayitvā pūṣā gā anvetu na iha ratiriti ca hutvā vṛṣamayaskārastvaṅkayet || 86.9 ||

ekasmin pārśve cakreṇāparasmin pārśve śūlena || 86.10 ||

aṅkitaṃ ca hiraṇyavarṇeti catasṛbhiḥ śaṃ no devīriti ca snāpayet || 86.11 ||

snātamalaṃkṛtaṃ snātālaṃkṛtābhiścatasṛbhirvatsatarībhiḥ sārdhamānīya rudrān puruṣasūktaṃ kūśmāṇḍīśca japet || 86.12 ||

pitā vatsānāmiti vṛṣabhasya dakṣiṇe karṇe paṭhet || 86.13 ||

imaṃ ca || 86.14 ||

vṛṣo hi bhagavān dharmaścatuṣpādaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  |
vṛṇomi tamahaṃ bhaktyā sa me rakṣatu sarvataḥ  || 86.15 ||

etaṃ yuvānaṃ patiṃ vo dadāmyanena krīḍantīścarata priyeṇa  |
mā hāsmahi prajayā mā tanūbhirmā radhāma dviṣate soma rājan  || 86.16 ||

vṛṣaṃ vatsatarīyuktamaiśānyāṃ kārayeddiśi  |
hoturvastrayugaṃ dadyātsuvarṇaṃ kāṃsyameva ca  || 86.17 ||

ayaskārasya dātavyaṃ vetanaṃ manasepsitam  |
bhojanaṃ bahusarpiṣkaṃ brāhmaṇāṃścātra bhojayet || 86.18 ||

utsṛṣṭo vṛṣabho yasmin pibatyatha jalāśaye  |
jalāśayaṃ tatsakalaṃ pitṝṃstasyopatisṭhati  || 86.19 ||

śṛṅgeṇollikhate bhūmiṃ yatra kvacana darpitaḥ  |
pitṝṇāmannapānaṃ tatprabhūtamupatiṣṭhati  || 86.20 ||



87

atha vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ kṛṣṇājinaṃ sakhuraṃ saśṛṅgaṃ suvarṇaśṛṅgaṃ raupyakhuraṃ mauktikalāṅgūlabhūṣitaṃ kṛtvāvike ca vastre prasārayet || 87.1 ||

tatastilaiḥ pracchādayet || 87.2 ||

suvarṇanābhiṃ ca kuryāt || 87.3 ||

ahatena vāsoyugena pracchādayet || 87.4 ||

sarvagandharatnaiścālaṃkṛtaṃ kuryāt || 87.5 ||

catasṛṣu dikṣu catvāri taijasāni pātrāṇi kṣīradadhimadhughṛtapūrṇāni nidhyāyāhitāgnaye brāhmaṇāyālaṃkṛtāya vāsoyugena pracchāditāya dadyāt || 87.6 ||

atra ca gāthā bhavanti || 87.7 ||

yastu kṛṣṇājinaṃ dadyātsakhuraṃ śṛṅgasaṃyutam  |
tilaiḥ pracchādya vāsobhiḥ sarvaratnairalaṃkṛtam  || 87.8 ||

sasamudraguhā tena saśailavanakānanā  |
caturantā bhaveddattā pṛthivī nātra saṃśayaḥ  || 87.9 ||

kṛṣṇājine tilān kṛtvā hiraṇyaṃ madhusarpiṣī  |
dadāti yastu viprāya sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtam  || 87.10 ||



88

atha prasūyamānā gauḥ pṛthivī bhavati || 88.1 ||

tāmalaṃkṛtāṃ brāhmaṇāya dattvā pṛthivīdānaphalamāpnoti || 88.2 ||

atra gāthā bhavati || 88.3 ||

savatsāromatulyāni yugānyubhayatomukhīm  |
dattvā svargamavāpnoti śraddadhānaḥ samāhitaḥ  || 88.4 ||



89

māsaḥ kārttiko'gnidaivatyaḥ || 89.1 ||

agniśca sarvadevānāṃ mukham || 89.2 ||

tasmāttu kārttikaṃ māsaṃ bahiḥsnāyī gāyatrījapanirataḥ sakṛdeva haviṣyāśī saṃvatsarakṛtātpāpātpūto bhavati || 89.3 ||

kārttikaṃ sakalaṃ māsaṃ nityasnāyī jitendriyaḥ  |
japan haviṣyabhugdāntaḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 89.4 ||



90

mārgaśīrṣaśuklapañcadaśyāṃ mṛgaśirasā yuktāyāṃ cūrṇitalavaṇasya suvarṇanābhaṃ prasthamekaṃ candrodaye brāhmaṇāya pradāpayet || 90.1 ||

anena karmaṇā rūpasaubhāgyavānabhijāyate || 90.2 ||

pauṣī cetpuṣyayuktā syāt, tasyāṃ gaurasarṣapakalkodvartitaśarīro gavyaghṛtapūrṇakumbhenābhiṣiktaḥ sarvauṣadhibhiḥ sarvagandhaiḥ sarvabījaiśca snāto ghṛtena bhagavantaṃ vāsudevaṃ snāpayitvā gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpanaivedyādibhirabhyarcya vaiṣṇavaiḥ śākrairbārhaspatyaiśca mantraiḥ pāvake hutvā sasuvarṇena ghṛtena brāhmaṇān svasti vācayet || 90.3 ||

vāsoyugaṃ kartre dadyāt || 90.4 ||

anena karmaṇā puṣyate || 90.5 ||

māghī maghāyutā cet, tasyāṃ tilaiḥ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā pūto bhavati || 90.6 ||

phālgunī phalgunīyutā cet, tasyāṃ brāhmaṇāya susaṃskṛtaṃ svāstīrṇaṃ śayanaṃ nivedya bhāryāṃ manojñāṃ rūpavatīṃ draviṇavatīṃ cāpnoti || 90.7 ||

nāryapi bhartāram || 90.8 ||

caitrī citrāyutā cet, tasyāṃ citravastrapradānena saubhāgyamāpnoti || 90.9 ||

vaiśākhī viśākhāyutā cet, tasyāṃ brāhmaṇasaptakaṃ kṣaudrayuktaistilaiḥ saṃtarpya dharmarājānaṃ prīṇayitvā pāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati || 90.10 ||

jyaiṣṭhī jyeṣṭhāyutā cetsyāt, tasyāṃ chatropānahapradānena gavādhipatyaṃ prāpnoti || 90.11 ||

āṣāḍhyāmāṣāḍhāyuktāyāmannapānadānena tadevākṣayyamāpnoti || 90.12 ||

śrāvaṇyāṃ śravaṇayuktāyāṃ jaladhenuṃ sānnāṃ vāsoyugācchāditāṃ dattvā svargamāpnoti || 90.13 ||

prauṣṭhapadyāṃ proṣṭhapadāyuktāyāṃ godānena sarvapāpavinirmukto bhavati || 90.14 ||

āśvayujyāmaśvinīgate candramasi ghṛtapūrṇaṃ bhājanaṃ suvarṇayutaṃ viprāya dattvā dīptāgnirbhavati || 90.15 ||

kārttikī kṛttikāyutā cetsyāt, tasyāṃ sitamukṣāṇamanyavarṇaṃ vā śaśāṅkodye sarvasasyaratnagandhopetaṃ dīpamadhye brāhmaṇāya dattvā kāntārabhayaṃ na paśyati || 90.16 ||

vaiśākhaśuklatṛtīyāyāmupoṣito'kṣataiḥ śrīvāsudevamabhyarcya tāneva hutvā dattvā ca sarvapāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati || 90.17 ||

yacca tasminnahani prayacchati tadakṣayyatāmāpnoti || 90.18 ||

pauṣyāṃ samatītāyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣadvādaśyāṃ sopavāsastilaiḥ snātastilokakaṃ dattvā tilairvāsudevamabhyarcya tāneva hutvā dattvā bhuktvā ca pāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati || 90.19 ||

māghyāṃ samatītāyāṃ kṛṣṇadvādaśyāṃ sopavāsaḥ śravaṇaṃ prāpya śrīvāsudevāgrato mahāvartidvayena dīpadvayaṃ dadyāt || 90.20 ||

dakṣiṇapārśve mahārajanaraktena samagreṇa vāsasā ghṛtatulāmaṣṭādhikāṃ dattvā || 90.21 ||

vāmapārśve tilatailayutāṃ sāṣṭāṃ dattvā śvetena samagreṇa vāsasā || 90.22 ||

etatkṛtvā yasmin rāṣṭre'bhijāyate yasmin deśe yasmin kule tatrojjvalo bhavati || 90.23 ||

āśvinaṃ sakalaṃ māsaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pratyahaṃ ghṛtaṃ pradāyāśvinau prīṇayitvā rūpabhāgbhavati || 90.24 ||

tasminneva māsi pratyahaṃ gorasairbrāhmaṇān bhojayitvā rājyabhāgbhavati || 90.25 ||

pratimāsaṃ revatīyute candramasi madhughṛtayutaṃ pāyasaṃ revatīprītyai paramānnaṃ brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā revatīṃ prīṇayitvā rūpasya bhāgī bhavati || 90.26 ||

māghe māsyagniṃ pratyahaṃ tilairhutvā saghṛtaṃ kulmāṣaṃ brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā dīptāgnirbhavati || 90.27 ||

sarvāṃ caturdaśīṃ nadījale snātvā dharmarājānaṃ pūjayitvā sarvapāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati || 90.28 ||

yadīcchedvipulān bhogāṃścandrasūryagrahopagān  |
prātaḥsnāyī bhavennityaṃ dvau māsau māghaphālgunau  || 90.29 ||



91

atha kūpakartustatpravṛtte pānīye duṣkṛtasyārdhaṃ vinaśyati || 91.1 ||

taḍāgakṛnnityatṛpto vāruṇaṃ lokamaśnute || 91.2 ||

jalapradaḥ sadā tṛpto bhavati || 91.3 ||

vṛkṣāropayiturvṛkṣāḥ paraloke putrā bhavanti || 91.4 ||

vṛkṣaprado vṛkṣaprasūnairdevān prīṇayati || 91.5 ||

phalaiścātithīn || 91.6 ||

chāyayā cābhyāgatān || 91.7 ||

deve varṣatyudakena pitṝn || 91.8 ||

setukṛtsvargamāpnoti || 91.9 ||

devāyatanakārī yasya devasyāyatanaṃ karoti tasyaiva lokamāpnoti || 91.10 ||

sudhāsiktaṃ kṛtvā yaśasā virājate || 91.11 ||

vicitraṃ kṛtvā gandharvalokamāpnoti || 91.12 ||

puṣpapradānena śrīmān bhavati || 91.13 ||

anulepanapradānena kīrtimān || 91.14 ||

dīpapradānena cakṣuṣmān sarvatrojjvalaśca || 91.15 ||

annapradānena balavān || 91.16 ||

devanirmālyāpanayanātgodānaphalamāpnoti || 91.17 ||

devagṛhamārjanāttadupalepanātbrāhmaṇocchiṣṭamārjanātpādaśaucādakalyaparicaraṇācca || 91.18 ||

kūpārāmataḍāgeṣu devatāyataneṣu ca  |
punaḥ saṃskārakartā ca labhate maulikaṃ phalam  || 91.19 ||



92

sarvadānādhikamabhayapradānam || 92.1 ||

tatpradānenābhīpsitaṃ lokamāpnoti || 92.2 ||

bhūmidānena ca || 92.3 ||

gocarmamātrāmapi bhuvaṃ pradāya sarvapāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati || 92.4 ||

gopradānena svargalokamāpnoti || 92.5 ||

daśadhenuprado golokān || 92.6 ||

śatadhenuprado brahamalokān || 92.7 ||

suvarṇaśṛṅgīṃ raupyakhurāṃ muktālāṅgūlāṃ kāṃsyopadohāṃ vastrottarīyāṃ dattvā dhenuromasaṃkhyāni varṣāṇi svargalokamāpnoti || 92.8 ||

viśeṣataḥ kapilām || 92.9 ||

dāntaṃ dhuraṃdharaṃ dattvā daśadhenuprado bhavati || 92.10 ||

aśvadaḥ sūryasālokyamāpnoti || 92.11 ||

vāsodaścandrasālokyam || 92.12 ||

suvarṇadānenāgnisālokyam || 92.13 ||

rūpyadānena rūpam || 92.14 ||

taijasānāṃ pātrāṇāṃ pradānena pātrībhavati sarvakāmānām || 92.15 ||

ghṛtamadhutailapradānenārogyam || 92.16 ||

auṣadhapradānena || 92.17 ||

lavaṇadānena ca lāvaṇyam || 92.18 ||

dhānyapradānena tṛptim || 92.19 ||

sasyapradānena ca || 92.20 ||

annadaḥ sarvam || 92.21 ||

dhānyapradānena saubhāgyam || 92.22 ||

tilapradaḥ prajāmiṣṭām || 92.23 ||

indhanapradānena dīptāgnirbhavati || 92.24 ||

saṃgrāme ca sarvajayamāpnoti || 92.25 ||

āsanapradānena sthānam || 92.26 ||

śayyāpradānena bhāryām || 92.27 ||

upānatpradānenāśvatarīyuktaṃ ratham || 92.28 ||

chatrapradānena svargam || 92.29 ||

tālavṛntacāmarapradānenādhvasukhitvam || 92.30 ||

vāstupradānena nagarādhipatyam || 92.31 ||

yadyadiṣṭatamaṃ loke yaccāsti dayitaṃ gṛhe  |
tattadguṇavate deyaṃ tadevākṣayamicchatā  || 92.32 ||



93

abrāhmaṇe dattaṃ tatsamameva pāralaukikam || 93.1 ||

dviguṇaṃ brāhmaṇabruve || 93.2 ||

sahasraguṇaṃ prādhīte || 93.3 ||

anantaṃ vedapārage || 93.4 ||

purohitastvātmana eva pātram || 93.5 ||

svasā duhitṛjāmātaraśca || 93.6 ||

na vāryapi prayaccheta baiḍālavratike dvije  |
na bakavratike pāpe nāvedavidi dharmavit || 93.7 ||

dharmadhvajī sadā lubdhaśchādmiko lokadāmbhikaḥ  |
baiḍālavratiko jñeyo hiṃsraḥ sarvābhisaṃdhakaḥ  || 93.8 ||

adhodṛṣṭirnaikṛtikaḥ svārthasādhanatatparaḥ  |
śaṭho mithyāvinītaśca bakavrataparo dvijaḥ  || 93.9 ||

ye bakavratino loke ye ca mārjāraliṅginaḥ  |
te patantyandhatāmisre tena pāpena karmaṇā  || 93.10 ||

na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret |
vratena pāpaṃ pracchādya kurvan strīśūdradambhanam  || 93.11 ||

pretyeha cedṛśo vipro garhyate brahmavādibhiḥ  |
chadmanācaritaṃ yacca vrataṃ rakṣāṃsi gacchati  || 93.12 ||

aliṅgī liṅgiveṣeṇa yo vṛttimupajīvati  |
sa liṅgināṃ haratyenastiryagyonau prajāyate  || 93.13 ||

na dānaṃ yaśase dadyānna bhayānnopakāriṇe  |
na nṛtyagītaśīlebhyo dharmārthamiti niścitam  || 93.14 ||



94

gṛhī valīpalitadarśane vanāśrayo bhavet || 94.1 ||

apatyasya cāpatyadarśane vā || 94.2 ||

putreṣu bhāryāṃ nikṣipya tayānugmyamāno vā || 94.3 ||

tatrāpyagnīnupacaret || 94.4 ||

aphālakṛṣṭena pañca yajñānna hāpayet || 94.5 ||

svādhyāyaṃ ca na jahyāt || 94.6 ||

brahmacaryaṃ pālayet || 94.7 ||

carmacīravāsāḥ syāt || 94.8 ||

jaṭāśmaśrulomanakhāṃśca bibhṛyāt || 94.9 ||

triṣavaṇasnāyī syāt || 94.10 ||

kapotavṛttirmāsanicayaḥ saṃvatsaranicayo vā || 94.11 ||

saṃvatsaranicayī pūrvanicitamāśvayujyāṃ jahyāt || 94.12 ||

grāmādāhṛtya vāśnīyādaṣṭau grāsān vane vasan  |
puṭenaiva palāśena pāṇinā śakalena vā  || 94.13 ||



95

vānaprasthastapasā śarīraṃ śoṣayet || 95.1 ||

grīṣme pañcatapāḥ syāt || 95.2 ||

ākāśaśāyī prāvṛṣi || 95.3 ||

ārdravāsā hemante || 95.4 ||

naktāśī syāt || 95.5 ||

ekāntaradvyantaratryantarāśī vā syāt || 95.6 ||

puṣpāśī || 95.7 ||

phalāśī || 95.8 ||

śākāśī || 95.9 ||

parṇāśī vā || 95.10 ||

yavānnaṃ pakṣāntayorvā sakṛdaśnīyāt || 95.11 ||

cāndrāyaṇairvā varteta || 95.12 ||

aśmakuṭṭaḥ || 95.13 ||

dantolūkhaliko vā || 95.14 ||

tapomūlamidaṃ sarvaṃ devamānuṣikaṃ jagat |
tapomadhyaṃ tapo'ntaṃ ca tapasā ca tathā dhṛtam  || 95.15 ||

yadduṣcaraṃ yaddurāpaṃ yaddūraṃ yacca duṣkaram  |
sarvaṃ tattapasā sādhyaṃ tapo hi duratikramam  || 95.16 ||



96

atha triṣvāśrameṣu pakvakaṣāyaḥ prājāpatyāmiṣṭiṃ kṛtvā sarvavedasaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā pravrajyāśramī syāt || 96.1 ||

ātmanyagnīnāropya bhikṣārthaṃ grāmamiyāt || 96.2 ||

saptāgārikaṃ bhaikṣamācaret || 96.3 ||

alābhe na vyatheta || 96.4 ||

na bhikṣukaṃ bhikṣeta || 96.5 ||

bhuktavati jane atīte pātrasaṃpāte bhaikṣamādadyāt || 96.6 ||

mṛnmaye dārupātre'lābupātre vā || 96.7 ||

teṣāṃ ca tasyādbhiḥ śuddhiḥ syāt || 96.8 ||

abhipūjitalābhādudvijeta || 96.9 ||

śūnyāgāraniketanaḥ syāt || 96.10 ||

vṛkṣamūlaniketano vā || 96.11 ||

na grāme dvitīyāṃ rātrimāvaset || 96.12 ||

kaupīnācchādanamātrameva vasanamādadyāt || 96.13 ||

dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyasetpādam || 96.14 ||

vastrapūtaṃ jalamādadyāt || 96.15 ||

satyapūtaṃ vadet || 96.16 ||

manaḥpūtamācaret || 96.17 ||

maraṇaṃ nābhikāmayeta jīvitaṃ ca || 96.18 ||

ativādāṃstitikṣeta || 96.19 ||

na kaṃcanāvamanyeta || 96.20 ||

nirāśīḥ syāt || 96.21 ||

nirnamaskāraḥ || 96.22 ||

vāsyaikaṃ takṣato bāhuṃ candanenaikamukṣataḥ  |
nākalyāṇaṃ na kalyāṇaṃ tayorapi ca cintayet || 96.23 ||

prāṇāyāmadhāraṇādhyānanityaḥ syāt || 96.24 ||

saṃsārasyānityatāṃ paśyet || 96.25 ||

śarīrasyāśucibhāvam || 96.26 ||

jarayā rūpaviparyayam || 96.27 ||

śārīramānasāgantukavyādhibhiścopatāpam || 96.28 ||

sahajaiśca || 96.29 ||

nityāndhakāre garbhe vasatim || 96.30 ||

mūtrapurīṣamadhye ca || 96.31 ||

tatra ca śītoṣṇaduḥkhānubhavanam || 96.32 ||

janmasamaye yonisaṃkaṭanirgamanātmahadduḥkhānubhavanam || 96.33 ||

bālye mohaṃ guruparavaśyatām || 96.34 ||

adhyayanādanekakleśam || 96.35 ||

yauvane ca viṣayāprāptāvamārgeṇa tadavāptau viṣayasevanānnarake patanam || 96.36 ||

apriyairvasatiṃ priyaiśca viprayogam || 96.37 ||

narake ca sumahadduḥkham || 96.38 ||

saṃsārasaṃsṛtau tiryagyoniṣu ca || 96.39 ||

evamasmin satatayāyini saṃsāre na kiṃcitsukham || 96.40 ||

yadapi kiṃcitduḥkhābhāvāpekṣayā sukhasaṃjñaṃ tadapyanityam || 96.41 ||

tatsevāśaktāvalābhe vā mahadduḥkham || 96.42 ||

śarīraṃ cedaṃ saptadhātukaṃ paśyet || 96.43 ||

vasārudhiramāṃsamedo'sthimajjāśukrātmakam || 96.44 ||

carmāvanaddham || 96.45 ||

durgandhi ca || 96.46 ||

malāyatanam || 96.47 ||

sukhaśatairapi vṛtaṃ vikāri || 96.48 ||

prayatnāddhṛtamapi vināśi || 96.49 ||

kāmakrodhalobhamohamadamātsaryasthānam || 96.50 ||

pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśātmakam || 96.51 ||

asthisirādhamanīsnāyuyutam || 96.52 ||

rajasvalam || 96.53 ||

ṣaṭtvacam || 96.54 ||

asthnāṃ tribhiḥ śataiḥ ṣaṣtyadhikairdhāryamāṇam || 96.55 ||

teṣāṃ vibhāgaḥ || 96.56 ||

sūkṣmaiḥ saha catuḥṣaṣṭirdaśanāḥ || 96.57 ||

viṃśatirnakhāḥ || 96.58 ||

pāṇipādaśalākāśca || 96.59 ||

ṣaṣṭiraṅgulīnāṃ parvāṇi || 96.60 ||

dve pārṣṇyoḥ || 96.61 ||

catuṣṭayaṃ gulpheṣu || 96.62 ||

catvāryaratnyoḥ || 96.63 ||

catvāri jaṅghayoḥ || 96.64 ||

dve dve jānukapolayoḥ || 96.65 ||

ūrvaṃsayoh || 96.66 ||

akṣatālūṣakaśroṇiphalakeṣu || 96.67 ||

bhagāsthyekam || 96.68 ||

pṛṣṭhāsthi pañcacatvāriṃśadbhāgam || 96.69 ||

pañcadaśāsthīni grīvā || 96.70 ||

jatrvekam || 96.71 ||

tathā hanuḥ || 96.72 ||

tanmūle ca dve || 96.73 ||

dve lalāṭākṣigaṇḍe || 96.74 ||

nāsā ghanāsthikā || 96.75 ||

arbudaiḥ sthālakaiśca sārdhaṃ dvāsaptatiḥ pārśvakāḥ || 96.76 ||

uraḥ saptadaśa || 96.77 ||

dvau śaṅkhakau || 96.78 ||

catvāri kapālāni śirasaśceti || 96.79 ||

śarīre'smin sapta sirāśatāni || 96.80 ||

nava snāyuśatāni || 96.81 ||

dhamanīśate dve || 96.82 ||

pañca peśīśatāni || 96.83 ||

kṣudradhamanīnāmekonatriṃśallakṣāṇi navaśatāni ṣaṭpañcāśaddhamanyaḥ || 96.84 ||

lakṣatrayaṃ śmaśrukeśakūpānām || 96.85 ||

saptottaraṃ marmaśatam || 96.86 ||

saṃdhiśate dve || 96.87 ||

catuṣpañcāśadromakoṭyaḥ saptaṣaṣṭiśca lakṣāṇi || 96.88 ||

nābhirājo gudaṃ śukraṃ śoṇitaṃ śaṅkhakau mūrdhā kaṇṭho hṛdayaṃ ceti prāṇāyatanāṇi || 96.89 ||

bāhudvayaṃ jaṅghādvayaṃ madhyaṃ śīrṣamiti ṣaḍaṅgāni || 96.90 ||

vasā vapā avahananaṃ nābhiḥ klomā yakṛtplīhā kṣudrāntraṃ vṛkkakau bastiḥ purīṣādhānamāmāśayaḥ hṛdayaṃ sthūlāntraṃ gudamudaraṃ gudakoṣṭham || 96.91 ||

kanīnike akṣikūṭe śaṣkulī karṇau karṇapatrakau gaṇḍau bhruvau śaṅkhau dantaveṣṭau oṣṭhau kakundare vaṅkṣaṇau vṛṣaṇau vṛkkau śleṣmasaṃghātikau stanau upajihvā sphicau bāhū jaṅghe ūrū piṇḍike tālūdaraṃ bastiśīrṣau cibukaṃ galaśuṇḍike avaṭuścetyasmin śarīre sthānāni || 96.92 ||

śabdasparśarūparasagandhāśca viṣayāḥ || 96.93 ||

nāsikālocanatvagjihvāśrotramiti buddhīndriyāṇi || 96.94 ||

hastau pādau pāyūpasthaṃ jihveti karmendriyāṇi || 96.95 ||

mano buddhirātmā cāvyaktamitīndriyātītāḥ || 96.96 ||

idaṃ śarīraṃ vasudhe kṣetramityabhidhīyate  |
etadyo vetti taṃ prāhuḥ kṣetrajñamiti tadvidaḥ  || 96.97 ||

kṣetrajñamapi māṃ viddhi sarvakṣetreṣu bhāvini  |
kṣetrakṣetrajñavijñānaṃ jñeyaṃ nityaṃ mumukṣuṇā  || 96.98 ||



97

ūrusthottānacaraṇaḥ savye kare karamitaraṃ nyasya tālusthācalajihvo dantairdantānasaṃspṛśan svaṃ nāsikāgraṃ paśyan diśaścānavalokayan vibhīḥ praśāntātmā caturviṃśatyā tattvairvyatītaṃ cintayet || 97.1 ||

nityamatīndriyamaguṇaṃ śabdasparśarūparasagandhātītaṃ sarvajñamatisthūlam || 97.2 ||
sarvagamatisūkṣmam || 97.3 ||

sarvataḥpāṇipādaṃ sarvato'kṣiśiromukhaṃ sarvataḥ sarvendriyaśaktim || 97.4 ||

evaṃ dhyāyet || 97.5 ||

dhyānaniratasya ca saṃvatsareṇa yogāvirbhāvo bhavati || 97.6 ||

atha nirākāre lakṣabandhaṃ kartuṃ na śaknoti, tadā pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśamanobuddhyātmāvyaktapuruṣāṇāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ dhyātvā tatra labdhalakṣaḥ tatparityajyāparamaparaṃ dhyāyet || 97.7 ||

evaṃ puruṣadhyānamārabheta || 97.8 ||

tatrāpyasamarthaḥ svahṛdayapadmasya avāṅmukhasya madhye dīpavatpuruṣaṃ dhyāyet || 97.9 ||

tatrāpyasamartho bhagavantaṃ vāsudevaṃ kirīṭinaṃ kuṇḍalinamaṅgadinaṃ śrīvatsāṅkaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitoraskaṃ saumyarūpaṃ caturbhujaṃ śaṅkhacakragadāpadmadharaṃ caraṇamadhyagatabhuvaṃ dhyāyet || 97.10 ||

yaddhyāyati tadāpnotīti dhyānaguhyam || 97.11 ||

tasmātsarvameva kṣaraṃ tyaktvā akṣarameva dhyāyet || 97.12 ||

na ca puruṣaṃ vinā kiṃcidapyakṣaramasti || 97.13 ||

taṃ prāpya mukto bhavati || 97.14 ||

puramākramya sakalaṃ śete yasmānmahāprabhuḥ  |
tasmātpuruṣa ityevaṃ procyate tattvacintakaiḥ  || 97.15 ||

prāgrātrāpararātreṣu yogī nityamatandritaḥ  |
dhyāyeta puruṣaṃ viṣṇuṃ nirguṇaṃ pañcaviṃśakam  || 97.16 ||

tattvātmānamagamyaṃ ca sarvatattvavivarjitam  |
aśaktaṃ sarvabhṛccaiva nirguṇaṃ guṇabhoktṛ ca  || 97.17 ||

bahirantaśca bhūtānāmacaraṃ carameva ca  |
sūkṣmatvāttadavijñeyaṃ dūrasthaṃ cāntike ca tat || 97.18 ||

avibhaktaṃ ca bhūtena vibhaktamiva ca sthitam  |
bhūtabhavyabhavadrūpaṃ grasiṣṇu prabhaviṣṇu ca  || 97.19 ||

jyotiṣāmapi tajjyotistamasaḥ paramucyate  |
jñānaṃ jñeyaṃ jñānagamyaṃ hṛdi sarvasya dhiṣṭhitam  || 97.20 ||

iti kṣetraṃ tathā jñānaṃ jñeyaṃ coktaṃ samāsataḥ  |
madbhakta etadvijñāya madbhāvāyopapadyate  || 97.21 ||



98

ityevamuktā vasumatī jānubhyāṃ śirasā ca namaskāraṃ kṛtvovāca || 98.1 ||

bhagavan, tvatsamīpe satatamevaṃ catvāri bhūtāni kṛtālayāni ākāśaḥ śaṅkharūpī, vāyuścakrarūpī, tejaśca gadārūpi, ambho'mbhoruharūpi | ahamapyanenaiva rūpeṇa bhagavatpādamadhye parivartinī bhavitumicchāmi || 98.2 ||

ityevamukto bhagavāṃstathetyuvāca || 98.3 ||

vasudhāpi labdhakāmā tathā cakre || 98.4 ||

devadevaṃ ca tuṣṭāva || 98.5 ||

oṃ namaste || 98.6 ||

devadeva || 98.7 ||

vāsudeva || 98.8 ||

ādideva || 98.9 ||

kāmadeva || 98.10 ||

kāmapāla || 98.11 ||

mahīpāla || 98.12 ||

anādimadhyanidhana || 98.13 ||

prajāpate || 98.14 ||

suprajāpate || 98.15 ||

mahāprajāpate || 98.16 ||

ūrjaspate || 98.17 ||

vācaspate || 98.18 ||

jagatpate || 98.19 ||

divaspate || 98.20 ||

vanaspate || 98.21 ||

payaspate || 98.22 ||

pṛthivīpate || 98.23 ||

salilapate || 98.24 ||

dikpate || 98.25 ||

mahatpate || 98.26 ||

marutpate || 98.27 ||

lakṣmīpate || 98.28 ||

brahmarūpa || 98.29 ||

brāhmaṇapriya || 98.30 ||

sarvaga || 98.31 ||

acintya || 98.32 ||

jñānagamya || 98.33 ||

puruhūta || 98.34 ||

puruṣṭuta || 98.35 ||

brahmaṇya || 98.36 ||

brahmapriya || 98.37 ||

brahmakāyika || 98.38 ||

mahākāyika || 98.39 ||

mahārājika || 98.40 ||

caturmahārājika || 98.41 ||

bhāsvara || 98.42 ||

mahābhāsvara || 98.43 ||

sapta || 98.44 ||

mahābhāga || 98.45 ||

svara || 98.46 ||

tuṣita || 98.47 ||

mahātuṣita || 98.48 ||

pratardana || 98.49 ||

parinirmita || 98.50 ||

aparinirmita || 98.51 ||

vaśavartin || 98.52 ||

yajña || 98.53 ||

mahāyajña || 98.54 ||

yajñayoga || 98.55 ||

yajñagamya || 98.56 ||

yajñanidhana || 98.57 ||

ajita || 98.58 ||

vaikuṇṭha || 98.59 ||

apāra || 98.60 ||

para || 98.61 ||

purāṇa || 98.62 ||

lekhya || 98.63 ||

prajādhara || 98.64 ||

citraśikhaṇḍadhara || 98.65 ||

yajñabhāgahara || 98.66 ||

puroḍāśahara || 98.67 ||

viśveśvara || 98.68 ||

viśvadhara || 98.69 ||

śuciśravaḥ || 98.70 ||

acyutārcana || 98.71 ||

ghṛtārciḥ || 98.72 ||

khaṇḍaparaśo || 98.73 ||

padmanābha || 98.74 ||

padmadhara || 98.75 ||

padmadhārādhara || 98.76 ||

hṛṣīkeśa (hṛṣīkeśa orhṛṣīkeśa ) || 98.77 ||

ekaśṛṅga || 98.78 ||

mahāvarāha || 98.79 ||

druhiṇa || 98.80 ||

acyuta || 98.81 ||

ananta || 98.82 ||

puruṣa || 98.83 ||

mahāpuruṣa || 98.84 ||

kapila || 98.85 ||

sāṃkhyācārya || 98.86 ||

viṣvaksena || 98.87 ||

dharma || 98.88 ||

dharmada || 98.89 ||

dharmāṅga || 98.90 ||

dharmavasuprada || 98.91 ||

varaprada || 98.92 ||
viṣṇo || 98.93 ||

jiṣṇo || 98.94 ||

sahiṣṇo || 98.95 ||

kṛṣṇa || 98.96 ||

puṇḍarīkākṣa || 98.97 ||

nārāyaṇa || 98.98 ||

parāyaṇa || 98.99 ||

jagatparāyana || 98.100 ||

namonama iti || 98.101 ||

stutvā tvevaṃ prasannena manasā pṛthivī tadā  |
uvāca saṃmukhaṃ devīṃ labdhakāmā vasuṃdharā  || 98.102 ||



99

dṛṣṭvā śriyaṃ devadevasya viṣṇorgṛhītapādāṃ tapasā jvalantīm  |
sutaptajāmbūnadacāruvarṇāṃ papraccha devīṃ vasudhā prahṛṣṭā  || 99.1 ||

unnidrakokanadacārukare vareṇye unnidrakokanadanābhigṛhītapāde  |
unnidrakokanadasadmasadāsthitīte unnidrakokanadamadhyasamānavarṇe  || 99.2 ||

nīlābjanetre tapanīyavarṇe śuklāmbare ratnavibhūṣitāṅgi  |
candrānane sūryasamānabhāse mahāprabhāve jagataḥ pradhāne  || 99.3 ||

tvameva nidrā jagataḥ pradhānā lakṣmīrdhṛtiḥ śrīrviratirjayā ca  |
kāntiḥ prabhā kīrtiratho vibhūtiḥ sarasvatī vāgatha pāvanī ca  || 99.4 ||

svadhā titikṣā vasudhā pratiṣṭhā sthitiḥ sudīkṣā ca tathā sunītiḥ  |
khyātirviśālā ca tathānasūyā svāhā ca medhā ca tathaiva buddhiḥ  || 99.5 ||

ākramya sarvaṃ tu yathā trilokīṃ tiṣṭhatyayaṃ devavaro'sitākṣi  |
tathā sthitā tvaṃ varade tathāpi pṛcchāmyahaṃ te vasatiṃ vibhūteḥ  || 99.6 ||

ityevamuktā vasudhāṃ babhāṣe lakṣmīstadā devavarāgrataḥsthā  |
sadā sthitāhaṃ madhusūdanasya devasya pārśve tapanīyavarṇe  || 99.7 ||

asyājñayā yaṃ manasā smarāmi śriyā yutaṃ taṃ pravadanti santaḥ  |
saṃsmāraṇe cāpyatha yatra cāhaṃ sthitā sadā tacchṛṇu lokadhātri  || 99.8 ||

vasāmyathārke ca niśākare ca tārāgaṇāḍhye gagane vimeghe  |
meghe tathā lambapayodhare ca śakrāyudhāḍhye ca taḍitprakāśe  || 99.9 ||

tathā suvarṇe vimale ca rūpye ratneṣu vastreṣvamaleṣu bhūme  |
prāsādamālāsu ca pāṇḍurāsu devālayeṣu dhvajabhūṣiteṣu  || 99.10 ||

sadyaḥ kṛte cāpyatha gomaye ca matte gajendre turage prahṛṣṭe  |
vṛṣe tathā darpasamanvite ca vipre tathaivādhyayanaprapanne  || 99.11 ||

siṃhāsane cāmalake ca bilve chatre ca śaṅkhe ca tathaiva padme  |
dīpte hutāśe vimale ca khḍga ādarśabimbe ca tathā sthitāham  || 99.12 ||

pūrṇodakumbheṣu sacāmareṣu satālavṛnteṣu vibhūṣiteṣu  |
bhṛṅgārapātreṣu manohareṣu mṛdi sthitāhaṃ ca navoddhṛtāyām  || 99.13 ||

kṣīre tathā sarpiṣi śādbale ca kṣaudre tathā dadhni puraṃdhrigātre  |
dehe kumāryāśca tathā surāṇāṃ tapasvināṃ yajñahutāṃ ca dehe  || 99.14 ||

śare ca saṃgrāmavinirgate ca sthitā mṛte svargasadaḥprayāte  |
vedadhvanau cāpyatha śaṅkhaśabde svāhāsvadhāyāmatha vādyaśabde  || 99.15 ||

rājyābhiṣeke ca tathā vivāhe yajñe vare snātaśirasyathāpi  |
puṣpeṣu śukleṣu ca parvateṣu phaleṣu ramyeṣu saridvarāsu  || 99.16 ||

saraḥsu pūrṇeṣu tathā jaleṣu saśādvalāyāṃ bhuvi padmakhaṇḍe  |
vane ca vatse ca śiśau prahṛṣṭe sādhau nare dharmaparāyaṇe ca  || 99.17 ||

ācārasevinyatha śāstranitye vinītaveṣe ca tathā suveṣe  |
suśuddhadānte malavarjite ca mṛṣṭāśane cātithipūjake ca  || 99.18 ||

svadāratuṣṭe nirate ca dharme dharmotkaṭe cātyaśanādvimukte  |
sadā sapuṣpe sasugandhigātre sugandhalipte ca vibhūṣite ca  || 99.19 ||

satye sthite bhūtahite niviṣṭe kṣamānvite krodhavivarjite ca  |
svakāryadakṣe parakāryadakṣe kalyāṇacitte ca sadā vinīte  || 99.20 ||

nārīṣu nityaṃ suvibhūṣitāsu pativratāsu priyavādinīṣu  |
amuktahastāsu sutānvitāsu suguptabhāṇḍāsu balipriyāsu  || 99.21 ||

saṃmṛṣṭaveśmāsu jitendriyāsu kalivyapetāsvavilolupāsu  |
dharmavyapekṣāsu dayānvitāsu sthitā sadāhaṃ madhusūdane ca  || 99.22 ||

nimeṣamātraṃ ca vinā kṛtāhaṃ na jātu tiṣṭhe puruṣottamena  || 99.23 ||



100

dharmaśāstramidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ svayaṃ devena bhāṣitam  |
ye dvijā dhārayiṣyanti teṣāṃ svarge gatiḥ parā  || 100.1 ||

idaṃ pavitraṃ maṅgalyaṃ svargyamāyuṣyameva ca  |
jñānaṃ caiva yaśasyaṃ ca dhanasaubhāgyavardhanam  || 100.2 ||

adhyetavyaṃ dhāraṇīyaṃ śrāvyaṃ śrotavyameva ca  |
śrāddheṣu śrāvaṇīyaṃ ca bhūtikāmairnaraiḥ sadā  || 100.3 ||

ya idaṃ paṭhate nityaṃ bhūtikāmo naraḥ sadā  |
idaṃ rahasyaṃ paramaṃ kathitaṃ ca dhare tava  || 100.4 ||

mayā prasannena jagaddhitārthaṃ saubhāgyametatparamaṃ yaśasyam  |
duḥsvapnanāśaṃ bahupuṇyayuktaṃ śivālayaṃ śāśvatadharmaśāstram  || 100.5 ||

[Endof ড়rt2 = endof Viṣṇu.]
